# The Book of Formation
<note>original Hebrew: *Sefer Yetzirah*; the foundational text of Jewish mysticism, exploring how the world was created through the ten divine numbers (Sefirot) and the twenty-two letters of the Hebrew alphabet</note>

->Attributed to our father Abraham, peace be upon him<-
->With five lovely commentaries, refined<-
->and more precious than much fine gold; a grace and holiness for the fellows<-
->who wander the paths of<-
->the true wisdom <note>A traditional term for Kabbalah</note>, may they spread<-
->their springs outward <note>A reference to Proverbs 5:16, implying the dissemination of esoteric knowledge to those ready to receive it</note><-
->to satisfy the longing<-
->soul.<-

->Printed<-

## Here in Mantua

->Under the rule of his excellency our lord the Duke<-
->Guglielmo Gonzaga, may his majesty be exalted<-
->and his glory, and may his kingdom be raised up, Amen<-

->By the hand of the youngest of printers<-
->Jacob Cohen of Gazzuolo<-

<summary>This is the title page of the 1562 Mantua edition of the *Sefer Yetzirah* (Book of Formation), the earliest and most influential work of Jewish mystical thought. The page identifies the printer as Jacob Cohen of Gazzuolo and acknowledges the patronage of Duke Guglielmo Gonzaga.</summary>

--- Page 6 ---
<meta>This page serves as an introduction to Sefer Yetzirah (The Book of Formation). It establishes the mystical connection between the "32 Paths of Wisdom" and the 32 times the divine name "Elohim" (God) appears in the first chapter of Genesis. It further bridges Kabbalistic thought with Greek philosophy by discussing "Hyle" (prime matter) and the emanation of the Sefirot (divine attributes).</meta>

# Chapter 1

<header>Introduction of the RAvaD, may his memory be a blessing</header>

->With thirty-two paths—thus said the Lord—concerning health and creation:<-
->And they were explained through compassion, wisdom, and the fear of the Lord, as He has graced us in the place of rest.<-
->And these are the thirty-two powers called by the name *Elohim* <note>God</note>: and they are as follows:<-

According to the order of the verses in Genesis, in the portion of *Berashit* <note>In the Beginning</note>: 
1. In the beginning **God** created. 
2. And the spirit of **God** hovered. 
3. And **God** said, "Let there be light." 
4. And **God** saw the light. 
5. And **God** divided between the light [and the darkness]. 
6. And **God** called the light "Day." 
7. And **God** said, "Let there be a firmament." 
8. And **God** made the firmament. 
9. And **God** called the firmament "Heaven." 
10. And **God** said, "Let the waters be gathered." 
11. And **God** called the dry land "Earth." 
12. And **God** saw that it was good. 
13. And **God** said, "Let the earth sprout." 
14. And **God** saw that it was good. 
15. And **God** said, "Let there be luminaries." 
16. And **God** made the two luminaries. 
17. And **God** set them in the firmament of the heaven. 
18. And **God** saw that it was good. 
19. And **God** said, "Let the waters swarm." 
20. And **God** created the great sea monsters. 
21. And **God** saw that it was good. 
22. And **God** blessed them, saying, "Be fruitful and multiply." 
23. And **God** said, "Let the earth bring forth." 
24. And **God** made the beast of the earth. 
25. And **God** saw that it was good. 
26. And **God** said, "Let us make man." 
27. And **God** created man. 
28. In the image of **God** He created him. 
29. And **God** blessed them. 
30. And **God** said to them, "Be fruitful and multiply." 
31. And **God** said, "Behold, I have given to you." 
32. And **God** saw all that He had made. 

It is already known in the wisdom of the philosophers the truth that the "Name" is applied to "prepared matter," which is a metaphor for its receptacle, and it is called in the Greek language *Hyle* <term>Hyle: A philosophical term for the most basic, formless matter that has the potential to become anything</term>. From the quantity of that matter, it was prepared in the thought of the Awesome Name. The existence of the order of beings is as they are found in truth through three intentional ways, which exist in potential, as a metaphor of a child born this day being held within the mind. Even the one who thinks of it now, [though it is not yet visible], it is called "folly" <note>likely referring to a potentiality not yet realized</note>, but its future forms are revealed as they move from this potential into actual reality. They emerge from potential to actual reality according to their levels, from the power of the Divine Will toward the whole "excess" which was mentioned to you as *Hyle*. It was never without the power of the elements within it; therefore, its "matter" <note>original: *chomro*</note> is not entirely in potential nor entirely in action, but is intermediate in its forms between what is potential and what is actual. 

It is the beginning and the offspring of all existing things. All beings from the **Supernal Crown** <term>Keter Elyon: The first and highest of the ten Sefirot, representing the Divine Will and the source of all emanation</term> and below were only brought into existence because of its existence. It does not pass away by law of change, nor by the law of "generation and corruption" <note>Aristotelian concept of *coming-to-be* and *passing-away*</note>, for it is the beginning of all things. It is called in the language of the Prophets a *Golem* <term>Golem: An unformed substance or raw material, as in Psalm 139:16</term>, as it says: "Your eyes saw my unformed substance" <note>Hebrew: *galmi*</note>. The word *Golem* comes from the language of *gelem* <note>meaning a shapeless mass</note>, for it is like a mass without form, and is therefore ready to receive all forms. 

The meaning of the phrase "And in Your book they were all written" <note>Psalm 139:16</note> is explained as: 
*Sefer* <note>Book/Wisdom</note>, *Sephar* <note>Story/Tiferet</note>, and *Sippur* <note>Number/Atarah</note>. 
Likewise in **Wisdom** <note>Chokhmah</note>, that is the letter *Yod*, within it is written and depicted every action of the upper and lower worlds. "The days were formed, and there was not one among them" <note>Psalm 139:16</note> refers to the six days of creation from **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> and below, as it is said: "Yours, O Lord, is the **Greatness** <note>Chesed</note>, the **Might** <note>Gevurah</note>, the **Beauty** <note>Tiferet</note>, the **Victory** <note>Netzach</note>, and the **Glory** <note>Hod</note>, for **All** <note>Yesod</note> that is in heaven..." Behold, these are the six names that acted during the six days of creation. Each one of them was uniquely assigned to a single day, and so were they all. 

"And not one <note>Hebrew: *echad*</note> among them" means that the **Supernal Crown** <note>Keter</note>, which is "One," was not written among them, for it is uniquely set apart above them. This means everything receives merit from the Supernal Crown, and through it, everything is made. Therefore, "matter" is in them, but not "matter" from them <note>suggesting the source remains transcendent</note>. This is the origin of all creatures...

In the [work of] Creation.

<summary>This page introduces Sefer Yetzirah by identifying the 32 "Paths of Wisdom" with the 32 times "Elohim" appears in the Genesis creation story. It explains the concept of "Hyle" (prime matter) as the "Golem" of the universe and begins a Kabbalistic mapping of the Sefirot onto the days of creation.</summary>

--- Page 8 ---
<meta>This page continues the introductory section, specifically providing the "Introduction of the Rabad" (Rabbi Abraham ben David), a 12th-century French scholar and Kabbalist. The previous page established the theological framework; this page moves into an exegesis of the Book of Job to explain the mechanics of creation.</meta>

->Introduction of the Rabad, of blessed memory<-

12. This is the living one, this is the way where light dwells. <note>original: "חי זה הדרך ישכון אור" — a play on Job 38:19</note> 13. And from there to his place. 14. Blue and birth... 16. and birth of hail... 17. The living one, this is the way light is divided. 18. Set apart to drip... 18. And a way for the lightning of the thunder. 19. Has the rain a father? 20. The living one, who has begotten the drops of dew? 21. <margin>From whose womb came the ice?</margin> From whose womb came the ice? 22. And the frost of heaven, who has given it birth? 23. Can you bind the sweet influences of Pleiades? 24. Or loose the bands of Orion? 25. Can you bring forth the constellations in their season? 26. Or can you guide Arcturus with his sons? 27. Know you the ordinances of heaven? 28. Can you lift up your voice to the clouds? 29. Can you send lightnings, that they may go? 30. Who has put wisdom in the inward parts? 31. The living one, who has given understanding to the rooster? 32. Who can number the clouds in wisdom? 33. Or who can stay the bottles of heaven? 34. Will you hunt the prey for the lion? 35. And fill the appetite of the young lions? 36. Who provides for the raven his food? 37. Know you the time when the wild goats of the rock bring forth? 38. Can you mark when the hinds do calve? 39. Who has sent out the wild donkey free? 40. Or who has loosed the bands of the wild donkey? 41. Will the wild ox be willing to serve you? 42. Can you bind the wild ox with his band in the furrow? 43. Will you trust him, because his strength is great? 44. Will you believe him, that he will bring home your seed? 45. Gave you the goodly wings unto the peacocks? 46. Have you given the horse strength? 47. Can you make him afraid as a grasshopper? 48. He paws in the valley, and rejoices in his strength. 49. Does the eagle mount up at your command? 50. Does he make his nest on high? **B. Who has laid its measures?**

Know that after it was clarified that in every <term>Hephah</term> <note>original: "חיפה" — likely a technical or geographical reference used as a mnemonic here</note> there is purity and a platform for the place of contemplation, as it is said, "Hephah is the secret of his friend." And because it is required by necessity for the Sabbath of my rest and the cause of rest for all those who rest, the one occupied with it found <term>Hephah</term> to be his cause. For the word <term>Hephah</term> is a compound of two words: <term>Chai Peh</term> <note>Literally "Living Mouth"</note>. "Chai" (Living) is the sustenance of power, more than the "Saphah" (Lip) from the language of the "Cheyk" (Bosom). And the word "Peh" (Mouth) is interpreted from the language of "Leko Peh" (Go here), a praise. And behold, Hephah is written with the letter *He* and not with a *Chet*, to hint at the explanation of the Rabbi <term>R.L.</term> <note>Resh-Lamed: Likely "Rabbi Levi" or "Our Rabbis of blessed memory"</note> who said that through the power of the heart and wisdom, which are called "Chai" (<gloss>Wisdom</gloss>) together with the "Peh" (<gloss>The Crown/Malchut</gloss>), your sukkah was established. From there, grace and knowledge are drawn out and extended, for knowledge (<gloss>Beauty/Tiferet</gloss>) is drawn from Understanding (<gloss>Binah</gloss>). For all those who are filled are sprung from that name. 

He returned and asked, <quote>Who set its measures?</quote> <note>Job 38:5</note> to create the "chariots" <note>The celestial structures</note> that inherit what is needed for the limit and definition of every single species of the upper and lower inhabitants and what is between them. Furthermore, even the lives of every species require a known limit, for the life of the multitude inherits its boundary. For their spiritual essence is mixed—Simeon, Levi, and Judah—none of them are like their fellow, not in length, nor in width, nor in thickness. Furthermore, every single member of a living species is distinct from another in length, width, and thickness. For the punishment <note>likely "measure" or "limit" here</note> of the small joint of the little finger is not equal to the measure adjacent to it, nor is its limit in length, nor in width, nor in thickness. Rather, each one of them is different from the other, and so it is in every species. 

He explained and said that this comes from the level called <term>Mi</term> <note>Literally "Who" — in Kabbalah, this refers to the Sephirah of Binah (Understanding)</note>, as it is written: "Whom did He consult, and who instructed Him?" <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. In my opinion, the essence and the generator require an explanation of the power of change through judgment, in the power of exchange and scattering. The power of change is simply awesome, and the "destruction" of the whole comes from the power of the "Houn." By the voice of the "Cheyk," it is "destruction." The explanation: it is called "destruction" because it is hidden from us. In reality, it is the power of existence, for existence is the opposite of destruction. It is what prepares the <term>Golem</term> <note>Prime matter/Formless substance</note> from the life of this species to the life of that species, until it is fit to receive "Form." This is the secret of "Sabin and Pang" and "Tarachma" and "Zasonchol," who understand everything through His wisdom. For Understanding (*Binah*) contemplates Wisdom (*Chokhmah*), and from there comes the entire drop of the *Golem*, which is Understanding, and it is <margin><unclear>And there for her is the company of the hind and around the rest (?)</unclear></margin> ready to receive the decree. And the daughter who gives intelligence to all senses, all species, and all individuals is called the "Mistress of the World."
(siṭin) <note>catchword for the next page</note>

<summary>This page features the Rabad’s introduction, using a poetic sequence of verses from Job to illustrate God's mastery over creation. He provides a Kabbalistic analysis of the word "Hephah" and explains how the divine attribute of Understanding (Binah) determines the specific physical and spiritual measurements of every individual being.</summary>
<keywords>Rabad, Job, Kabbalah, Binah, Chokhmah, Golem, Creation, Etymology, Divine Measures</keywords>

--- Page 9 ---
<meta>This page continues the introductory commentary traditionally attributed to the "Rabad" (Rabbi Abraham ben David of Posquières), though modern scholars often attribute this specific text to the 14th-century Kabbalist Joseph ben Shalom Ashkenazi. Following the list of verses from the Book of Job on the previous page, the author now explains the philosophical and mystical structure of the 32 Paths of Wisdom and the 50 Gates of Understanding, which are the foundational blueprints of the Sefer Yetzirah (Book of Formation).</meta>

### Introduction of the Rabad, of blessed memory

The commentary that arose in the thought of the Supreme Scribe <note>A poetic reference to God as the author of reality</note> is according to the measure of the Wisdom of the Creator (blessed be He). To use a metaphor, it is like the form of a blueprint in the mind of an architect, for He is the Actor, He is the Form, and He is the Ultimate Purpose. Therefore, all the enumerated powers are carved out, and His wisdom is within their hollows, fixed by the mark of the name *Shaddai* <note>The divine name associated with boundaries and "sufficiency"</note>; for within it, all the powers were formed in their perfection, responding to the count of the surrounding circle <note>Likely referring to the ratio of a circle's circumference to its diameter, used here as a metaphor for divine proportions</note> which is approximately ten.

And you must know, by way of the secret of the Divine Mention, that within the Name <gloss>Tiferet (Beauty/Harmony) within the stars</gloss> lies the secret of perfections. Therefore, it is called the "Portal," meaning the Crown of Lights, for all the lights that were formed in the world were formed from it. Seek out God as you would seek your own soul, for my words are according to the measure of the Wisdom of God <gloss>Binah (Understanding)</gloss>. It is a secret: now, should you think to say that there is a soulish power with independent action <note>i.e., a power separate from God's unity</note>, God forbid! Anyone who allows it to enter their mind to say such a thing has no portion in the God of Israel or His Torah. All these matters and their secrets have already been hinted to you "mouth to mouth" <note>An idiom for oral tradition/esoteric transmission</note>. Therefore, I have set it in my mind to respond to you, to turn your eyes a very little toward what I have received in explaining this holy book. Before I begin to open the gates of these cycles, I will start by explaining one response from the "Book of Necessity" <note>Possibly a reference to a philosophical work on logical necessity</note> and so on. Our objective will be clarified for all our secrets: the hidden paths of the Foundation of the Creator and the foundation of the Fifty Gates of Understanding shall all be clarified as requested and required.

**My Lord, open my lips, and my mouth shall declare Your praise.** <note>Psalm 51:15, a standard opening for Jewish prayer and study</note> You must know that the entire *Sefer Yetzirah* (Book of Formation) is divided into three ways: 1. In the **World** <term>Olam: Space/Macrocosm</term>, 2. In the **Year** <term>Shanah: Time/The Cycle of the Calendar</term>, 3. In the **Soul** <term>Nefesh: The Individual/Humanity</term>. From these, the secret of the entire changing matter is established: **World, Year, Soul**.

The knowledge is this: no living thing or physical object is brought into being unless it is categorized under one genus. For quantity is not seen from quality, nor quality from quantity. It is a logical certainty that an "accident" <note>A philosophical term for a non-essential property, like color</note> cannot be created from a "substance," nor a substance from an accident, unless they are all included under the existence of the "Emanations." Even more so, a soul cannot be created from that which is not a soul. Since everything is a void of vitality, and matter serves as its canopy in the foundations of the earth: this is the first philosophy, which has the power to prove a hidden thing from the power of its absolute literal meaning, leaving no room for doubt.

The secret is that the Cause of Causes <note>The First Cause/God</note> is exalted above a "Simple Intellect" <note>The first stage of divine emanation</note> of such absolute simplicity that there is nothing between it and its Cause. This Intellect is the "Effect" <note>The first thing caused by God</note>. This Intellect comprehends itself and its Cause. The nature of the distinction between it and its Cause is specified. Thus, there are three comprehensions. Each one is further divided into two comprehensions, according to its comprehension of each and every thing.

How so?
1. The Intellect comprehending itself.
2. The Intellect comprehending its Cause.
3. The Intellect comprehending that which is between itself and its Cause.
Now, 1. The Intellect with itself; and its relationship; and the distinction between it and its Cause.
2. The Intellect itself and the Cause of its Cause; and the distinction between itself and the Cause of its Cause.
3. The Intellect [comprehending] the essence of its Cause and the Cause of its Cause; and the distinction between them.
This makes nine comprehensions. Before they ascend to the "Inclusive Whole," there are **Ten Comprehensions** <note>These correspond to the Ten Sefirot</note>. Each one has a beginning, an end, and a middle, making thirty powers. Adding the [original two] levels of the Cause makes **32 Powers**, which are the **Paths of Wisdom**. This Wisdom is a "Hyle" <note>An ancient Greek term for "prime matter"</note> stripped of all physical matter and form, being purely intellectual.

Therefore, I have seen fit to write for you the 32 Paths along with the 50 Gates of Understanding, all arranged one after the other. And these are they:
1. "Where were you when I laid the foundations of the earth?" <note>The following list correlates biblical verses from Job 38 to the mystical gates/paths</note>
2. "Who marked off its dimensions? Surely you know!"
3. "Who stretched a measuring line across it?"
4. "On what were its footings set?"
5. "Or who laid its cornerstone?"
6. "While the morning stars sang together?"
7. "Have you ever given orders to the morning?"
8. "Or shown the dawn its place?"
9. "Have you journeyed to the springs of the sea?"
10. "Have the gates of death been shown to you?"
11. "Have you seen the gates of the deepest darkness?"

And so on... <note>original: וג' (u-gevei)</note>

<summary>This page presents the philosophical introduction to the Sefer Yetzirah, explaining how the 32 Paths of Wisdom are derived from the interactions of the "Simple Intellect" with its Source. It also introduces the fundamental Kabbalistic triad of World, Year, and Soul (Space, Time, and Spirit) as the framework for all creation.</summary>
<keywords>Rabad, Sefer Yetzirah, 32 Paths of Wisdom, 50 Gates of Understanding, World-Year-Soul, Cause of Causes, Simple Intellect, Ten Sefirot, Job 38, Kabbalistic philosophy</keywords>

--- Page 10 ---
<meta>This page continues the philosophical and Kabbalistic introduction to the Sefer Yetzirah. While the previous page established God as the ultimate architect and the "Supreme Scribe," this page shifts to a defense of the doctrine of "Creation from Nothing" (Creatio ex nihilo) against the arguments of medieval philosophers and heretics who believed in the eternity of matter.</meta>

### Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

To the soul that has strayed in the wasteland, He gave a healing remedy, saying that we have an established Cause. These are the "Living Creatures," the "Families," and the "Apportionment of the Firstborn." <note>These terms refer to different levels of angelic beings and emanations that govern the structure of the universe.</note> Therefore, they are the "Beginnings," the "Hidden Things," and their "Treasuries." Some pertain to the Intellect, which are called the **Morning Stars**; others pertain to Might <note>Gevurah</note>, which are called **the Foolish One** <note>original: "Kesil," often identified with the constellation Orion, here representing the attribute of Severity</note>. All of them were made wise by the Creator of All to measure His world, as it is written: *“When the morning stars sang together, and all the sons of God shouted for joy.”* <note>Job 38:7</note> He desisted not from the twelve sons within the "Daughter of Beauty" <note>likely referring to the twelve zodiac signs or tribes within the Sefirah of Tiferet</note> before the **Foundation Stone** <note>the mystical center of the world</note> and the "Veil" existed, when the world and its fullness, the earth and all that is in it, were established.

However, the philosophers say that nothing comes into being except from something else that already exists. But according to our view and our holy Torah, the world is newly created <note>created "ex nihilo," or from nothingness</note>. If this is the case, the heretics <term>Epicureans (Apiqorsim) - a term for those who deny divine creation or providence</term> have a claim to entice us, saying: "The world was not newly created, because there must have been something prior to it from which it was necessitated." Even if only the **Cause of Causes** <note>The ultimate Divine Source</note> existed alone, if you say there was "something else" there, then you are suggesting a primordial matter existed alongside God, which is a denial of the Torah. And if you say there was nothing but the Holy One, blessed be He, and He created the world from Himself—then His very essence would be like something physical or corporeal <note>having a body</note>. This would imply a flaw in His absolute oneness, for Heaven forbid that His essence should be the foundation of the earth!

Rather, "He acquired wisdom, knowledge, and understanding." The explanation is: through the power of His Wisdom and the power of His Understanding, the blueprint of the world came to be. Thus, a seeker might ask: "Why was the world made to this specific measure, and not more or less?" or "How could a measured, finite thing emerge from a power that is infinite <note>Ein Sof</note> by way of necessity?" To these two claims, one might answer: "If there is a Creator who renews the world, who could know 'who stretched the line upon it'?" <note>Job 38:5</note> Furthermore, a seeker might ask: "Is it possible for qualities that are opposites to emerge from a single, simple Essence?" If you say they emerged by "choice," we must still resolve the four qualities—Heat, Cold, Moisture, and Dryness. They are necessitated in nature by the "First Effect" <note>the first emanation</note>, and the First Effect by its own nature. If these opposites existed within the essence of the First Cause, then His nature would contain conflicting things. But you say He is "Simple" <note>indivisible and uniform</note>. From what source, then, is the beginning of these qualities and their transformations?

On this pillar, the foundation of the natural earth <gloss>dust/matter</gloss> depends. The explanation of "on what" refers to the Wisdom called **Mah** <note>Hebrew for "What," a mystical name for the Sefirah of Chokhmah</note>. For this Wisdom is "simple" relative to the qualities, just as the **Hyle** <term>Hyle - a Greek philosophical term for "prime matter" that has no form of its own</term> carries the simple life-forces according to the opinion of the heretics and philosophers. But our view is not according to the philosophers. For the philosopher believes that by nature, matter exists because of form, and no form exists without matter. But according to our holy view, **"Wisdom—where shall it be found?"** <note>Job 28:12</note>. It serves as the Form for everything, and its "Matter" is the source of the qualities and the "life-forces" which are simple. Their sources emerge from the power of the Wisdom called *Mah*.

Thus, we must resolve whether He brings things from potentiality into actuality. If He does not bring things from potentiality into actuality, then who "laid the cornerstone" and the "foundation of the world's rest"? The answer is that whoever receives the power of **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> will understand. Therefore, Wisdom prepared and a fire burned at its corner, and with a "Scale" <note>the mystical balance</note> He weighed the voices, like an artist who paints all possible forms. 

Regarding the secret of the world's boundary—as if it were matter—"Who measured the waters in the hollow of his hand, and meted out heaven with the span, and comprehended the dust of the earth in a measure, and weighed the mountains in scales, and the hills in a balance?" <note>Isaiah 40:12</note>. The explanation of "in his hollow" <note>she'alo</note> is related to the "narrow path" <note>mish'ol</note> of the vineyards. 
<margin>I shall listen to the Word in its place</margin>
It is written: *“To tread and go around.”* The Creator, blessed be He, has a single measure. You see that the "work of the waters" was done alone, and with a proof: "with a span He meted." This means He established them with His proportions. "And comprehended the dust of the earth in a measure <note>shlish - literally 'a third'</note>." This "Third" was the measure of **Tiferet** (Beauty), which is the "third" language of the spread. According to our calculation, to encompass a compound object, the circumference is three times the diameter <note>The text is referencing the mathematical approximation of Pi as 3, as discussed in the Talmudic tractate Eruvin</note>. Therefore, it says "And comprehended the dust of the earth in a *third*." And the word "comprehended" <note>v'khal</note> refers to a measure. In the language of lengths, the word "all" <note>kol</note> refers to stretching a square, as in the Tractate Eruvin regarding the "Sea" of Solomon: *“It was ten cubits from brim to brim, round in compass... and a line of thirty cubits did compass it round about.”* <note>1 Kings 7:23</note> Thus, the word "comprehended" <gloss>v'khal</gloss> in "the third" <gloss>shlish</gloss> refers to that which encompasses the entirety of the "dust of the earth."

<summary>This page addresses the theological challenge of how a finite, physical world of opposing qualities (like hot and cold) could be created by a single, infinite, and non-physical God. It uses the concept of Divine Wisdom (Chokhmah) and mathematical proportions, such as the 3:1 ratio of a circle's circumference to its diameter, to explain the divine "blueprint" of creation.</summary>

<keywords>Creatio ex nihilo, Prime Matter, Hyle, Wisdom, Understanding, Tiferet, Epicureans, Philosophy, Circle Ratio, Foundation Stone, Isaiah 40:12, Job 38:7</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the philosophical exploration of the divine "Architect" and the specific mechanics of how the physical world was formed. While the previous page focused on the debate against the eternity of matter, this page delves into the "Forms" (Platonic-style archetypes) and the divine name *Shaddai* as the force that sets limits on creation.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

(Precisely in the language of the Talmud) <note>original: "mitin b'lashon talmud m'duka"</note> because it is from the Knowledge of the World, and you must know this language. Similarly, it contains the likeness of every living thing, each according to its species. In the Scripture, these likenesses represent the essence of every form, and this is the secret of the **Living Creatures running and returning** <note>original: "Chayot retzo va-shov," a reference to the angelic movement in Ezekiel 1:14, here signifying the dynamic pulse of creation</note>. Man was made in His image and His likeness because in that high degree exists the form of all forms. Therefore, there is an **Agent**, a **Form**, and a **Purpose**. This is not according to the path of the philosophers who say that the form of everything exists only as a chain of **Cause and Effect** <note>original: "ilah v'alul"</note>.

In the *Book of Measurements* <note>Sefer Ha-Middot</note>, just as there is an Agent, so too must there be ordered powers—both simple and complex—all arranged in this manner. For example, the power of the **Imagination** <note>or "Form-giver," original: "Ha-Mitzayer"</note> to draw is not weak or lacking; it follows an order that changes for the general death but not for the specific death. The Agent must possess the **Power of Engraving** <note>original: "Koach Ha-Chakikah"</note> to carve out the details of these great existences and to balance them.

Consider the intent: the bones are hollow and marrow-filled, and all the joints of the hand are shaped according to the specific needs of every individual part of the species, until every kind is perfected according to its "Upper Species." In this way, God is called **Creator**, **Former**, and **Engraver**. He must set limits through His engraving so that every being is sufficient to its kind.

<margin>
The Heaven-Rider...
and He is called
the **Encompasser**
because every
single thing
lives by His measure.
</margin>

This applies to all hollow spaces, whether in the bones, the sinews, or the veins; some places are wider and stronger, some thick and some thin. It would be an error to call this "chance," for if it were so, the species would not align. All plants are formed in their specific details—length, thickness, and width—according to a general plan. 

As an example: if you take a twenty-year-old man and a ten-day-old infant, you will see they are ordered according to the same proportions; the large parts are large and the small parts are small, each in their proper place. Thus, the Agent must have the **Encompassing Power** <note>original: "Koach Ha-Makif"</note> to distribute every lack or law according to its necessity—some parts recessed and some protruding—and they do not fluctuate by mistake, but are precisely strengthened.

This rule applies to all: the silent (mineral), the growing (vegetable), the living (animal), and the speaking (human), as well as the heavenly spheres. All this comes from the power of the name **Shaddai**. When parts change or reach their limit, it is known in every species and every part at a specific time. Our Sages of blessed memory explained the name **Shaddai** <note>He-Shin-Dalet-Yud</note> as: "He who said to His world, 'Enough!'" <note>original: "She-amar l'olamo dai"</note>. This was to prevent the world from stretching out and expanding infinitely.

In the *Book of the Boundary* <note>Sefer Ha-Gevul</note>, it is said: He moved the world, and it shrunk and gathered together so they would not vanish or exceed their limit, neither in width nor in thickness. 

Who poured out His kindness upon it? Every single detail of the living species has a limit and a measure; it shall not add nor subtract from its "nest" <note>its established place</note>. He gave it understanding and a boundary. This is hinted at by the "Palm tree that dwells over the multitude." <note>This likely refers to a Kabbalistic configuration of the Sefirot, specifically the attribute of Malchut (Kingship) or the "Crown" (Atarah) which limits the flow of divine energy.</note>

**"Upon what were its foundations sunk?"** <note>Job 38:6</note> Know that every lack on earth is influenced by the Upper Heavens. There are Twelve Columns of the Earth, and Seven Columns, and this **One Column**—the **Crown** <note>Atarah</note>—upon which the world stands. Similarly, the attribute of **Beauty** <note>Tiferet</note> stands upon columns, and there are twelve boundaries of the diagonal. This is the meaning of "He hangs the earth upon nothingness" <note>Job 26:7</note> and "The Lord by wisdom founded the earth."

**"Or who laid its cornerstone?"** <note>Job 38:6</note> This refers to the "Stone" of Beauty, which is the **Foundation Stone** <note>term: **Even Shetiyah** — In Jewish tradition, the spiritual center of the universe, located under the Holy of Holies in the Temple</note> from which the world was founded. In the lack of its opposite, all the higher qualities are for the sake of kindness... <note>The text here becomes a highly technical discussion of the "Seventy Facets" and the "Face and Back" of divine emanations.</note>

<summary>The Ra'avad explains that God acts as an "Engraver" who precisely designs the biological and physical limits of all species. He introduces the name Shaddai as the divine force that halted the infinite expansion of the universe, ensuring everything stays within its "boundary."</summary>
<keywords>Shaddai, Engraving, Sefer Ha-Gevul, Foundation Stone, Even Shetiyah, Divine Proportions, Creation Mechanics, Form and Purpose</keywords>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory
## Page 8

<header>Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory</header>
<sig>B 2</sig>
<meta>catchword: And it concludes</meta>

Its engraving and carving were done by His spirit and its doors. For just as I engraved the **Abyss** <note>original: *Tehom*, referring to the primordial depths of the world before it was ordered</note> with cloud and thick darkness, He set a boundary at its "lips" <note>the shoreline</note> through decrees. These were spread out into separations and movements, dividing until they became bound together; then the sand of the sea congealed and hardened. I measured its dimensions and prepared for it paths and structures to draw its surroundings, like a sea and a road, while leaving a space within it. This refers to the waters being split and the Abyss being prevented from flooding the entire world. This is hinted at in the verse: *"He rebukes the sea"* <note>Nahum 1:4</note>, which refers to the ancient mystery of the "weeping waters." To say that their strength was limited by the Waters of the Beginning <note>original: *Mayim Rishonim*</note>. Our Sages have already hinted at this secret in the verse: *"And God made the firmament and divided"* <note>Genesis 1:7</note>, where He extracted and set apart what was before us. Here, these matters are revealed and received through a tradition of established crowns. In the Talmudic chapter **"He who sells the ship"** <note>Bava Batra, Chapter 5, which contains many mystical legends about the sea</note>, regarding the verse: *"By His power He stilled the sea; by His wisdom He cut Rahab to pieces"* <note>Job 26:12</note>, consider this:

The language of the **Targum** <note>the Aramaic translation of the Bible</note> explains: "And I set my decree upon them, and I placed bars and doors." This means I barred and closed off its boundaries. For just as I engraved the limit for the children of men, so too did the waters have the potential to spread infinitely. Therefore, in the second stage of Divine Thought, the heart of the Almighty <note>original: *Shaddai*</note> decreed: **"Enough!"** <note>original: *Dai*</note>. He said to His world: "Enough!" This is why the commandment of the "Decree" is explained as a restriction, saying: *"Thus far you shall come, and no farther; and here your proud waves shall stop"* <note>Job 38:11</note>. This means that as soon as the waves reach their limit, they dry up and return to being dust and clay. Were it not for this salt-like boundary, the Abyss would have flooded the entire world.

Behold, the entire mystery of **Loving-kindness** <note>original: *Chesed*</note> is to allow the Abyss to be covered. This is what the Holy One, blessed be He, said to Job: *"Where were you when I laid the foundations of the earth?"* <note>Job 38:4</note>. Therefore, the translator rendered it: "And I commanded the depth, 'Do not continue, and here you shall set your waves in their colors.'"

*"Have you ever in your life commanded the morning, or told the dawn its place?"* <note>Job 38:12</note>. Our Sages of blessed memory said: In the future, every righteous person is destined to inherit **three hundred and ten worlds** <note>The Hebrew word for "substance" or "is" (*Yesh*) has the numerical value of 310</note>, as it is written: *"That I may cause those who love Me to inherit substance [Yesh]"* <note>Proverbs 8:21</note>. My intention here is the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*, the highest of the ten Siphrot or divine emanations</note>.

The secret within this collective is what is mentioned in the *Great Chapters of the Palaces* <note>original: *Heikhalot Rabbati*, an early Jewish mystical text</note> regarding the rain and **Metatron**, the Prince of the Countenance, who is called the **Lesser Lord** <note>original: *Adonai Ha-Katan*</note>. From the perspective of the "end of the heel," these spirits delay every crown. The Great Light that is with them is part of the "Small Waters." Regarding this, the Torah states: *"Be on your guard before him and obey his voice... for My name is in him"* <note>Exodus 23:21</note>. Our Sages said this refers to Metatron, whose name is numerically equivalent to his Master's name <note>both Metatron and Shaddai equal 314</note>. The philosophers call his voice the **Active Intellect** <note>original: *Sekhel Poel*</note>, which acts upon the celestial spheres, and some say it acts upon the physical elements.

Furthermore, our Sages said that in the future, the righteous will have "Holy" proclaimed before them, as it is written: *"And it shall come to pass that he who is left in Zion and remains in Jerusalem shall be called holy—everyone who is recorded for life in Jerusalem"* <note>Isaiah 4:3</note>. This is because every righteous person is recorded for an eternal, uninterrupted life—a permanence like the sand. This fulfills the verse: *"But you who held fast to the Lord your God are all alive today"* <note>Deuteronomy 4:4</note>. The secret of their labor is: *"They dwelt there with the King for His work"* <note>1 Chronicles 4:23</note>. This means they dwell with the King of Kings, participating in the actions of the righteous, which are like a king creating a world. See the Book of Genesis according to its **Sefirot** <note>divine emanations</note>; this occurs in every path and line. The fullness of the day moves from the side of the mundane to the Sabbath. From the "Lower Light"—meaning from the Sefirah of **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note> downwards—the world was created. Thus, one merits to be "Morning" in the future, as it says: *"And God said, 'Let there be light,' and there was light... and God called the light Day."* This is the secret of the verse: *"Have you ever... built the morning?"* <note>a variant of Job 38:12</note>.

"And told the dawn its place"—this refers to the Sun and the Shield of the Lord God. It does not refer to the physical sun alone, but to the entire Wheel of the **Chariot** <note>original: *Merkavah*</note> in the heavens. A quarter of a day encompasses twelve "Existences" <note>original: *Havayot*, permutations of the Divine Name</note>, corresponding to the twelve signs of the Zodiac. When you divide the number of the day and its quarter by twelve, each "Existence" receives 3 days, 3 hours, and 540 "parts" <note>original: *chalakim*, a traditional unit of time where 1080 parts equal one hour</note>. This movement is compared to the pace of a horse. However, there is also a daily movement, and we do not honor the Wheel with it at all times. This is to say that in its daily movement, one **Living Creature** <note>original: *Chayah*, a type of angel</note> moves through every hour. There are six "Existences" in a day...

## Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

The Earth, and all the Chariots <note>original: *Merkabot*, referring to the organized structures of the divine realm</note> and governing forces beneath it—they all praise the "Glory of Your Kingdom," and so forth, as will be explained later. And now, we shall explain in detail the nature of its circumference, as mentioned in the statement: *"Let the waters under the heaven be gathered unto one place, and let the dry land appear"* <note>Genesis 1:9</note>. This means that when the waters covering the **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: The Sefirah representing "Beauty" or "Harmony," often associated with the celestial "Heavens"</term> emerged via the two channels toward one place—as mentioned above—it is a proof from the side of the secret that they were all gathered toward the **Yesod** <term>Yesod: The Sefirah of "Foundation," which acts as the conduit for all divine emanations</term>.

And then the Sea was built. The two channels that reached the *Yesod* descended <unclear>...heights...</unclear> to reveal the dry land upon the foundations of the Earth. Afterward, as explained, <note>The text here contains cryptic abbreviations and OCR fragments likely referring to Job 38:8</note> it describes the bursting forth from the womb, for the "Gathering" <note>original: *Koveh*</note> takes from the upper and lower realms. *"As if it burst forth from the womb"* <note>Job 38:8</note>—this means that as the waters were drawn out, the darkness was gathered and He "shut the doors" against them, because of the decree <note>original: *Chok*</note> that He placed upon it, which is the image of the sand.

And behold, when the waters were gathered to one place at the time He shut the doors upon them so they would not turn back to cover the Earth—meaning, He set a boundary that they shall not pass, nor return to cover the Earth <note>referencing Psalm 104:9</note>. For it was not their intention to distance themselves from the Lord and His Torah, but rather to surround the "Transformations," as I explained in the Portion of *Bereshit* <note>Genesis</note>, where it says: *"The mountains rose, the valleys sank down unto the place which You established for them"* <note>Psalm 104:8</note>. And as it is said, I have placed the sand as a boundary for the sea, a perpetual decree that it cannot pass. *"Though the waves toss themselves, yet can they not prevail; though they roar, yet can they not pass over it"* <note>Jeremiah 5:22</note>.

Know that there is an **Inner Sand** and an **Outer Sand**. The secret of the "door" of *Yesod* prevents the waters from descending upon the Earth <margin>the Crown</margin>, and the door from below prevents them from returning to cover the Earth (meaning the waters that were gathered beneath the Earth). By the power of these doors, the settlement of the Earth is maintained, as hinted at in what our Sages of blessed memory said regarding **Tohu and Bohu** <note>the "Chaos and Void" of Genesis 1:2</note>:

<column-break/>

They said: **Tohu** <note>Chaos</note> is a green line that encompasses the entire world. **Bohu** <note>Void</note>, these are "slimy stones" <note>original: *avanim mefulamot*</note> sunken into the **Abyss** <note>Tehom</note>, and from them, waters emerge to the world. Behold, this flame of which it is said <unclear>...sand/stones...</unclear>

And behold, when it says "Let the waters be gathered," as the waters were gathered to rise and cover the Earth, there preceded this the existence of the Abyss as its garment <note>original: *Tehom kikesuto*</note>. For it is written: *"Cloud its garment, and thick darkness its swaddling band"* <note>Job 38:9</note>. He surrounded the Abyss with thick darkness and placed doors around it so the Abyss would not surround the entire world. This "Thick Darkness" <note>original: *Arafel*</note> is the structure of the **Firmament**.

The explanation of *"As if it burst forth from the womb"* is the power and movement of the waters to emerge from the Abyss to flood the world, just as a fetus emerges from the womb to be born. There are those who explain this in the sense of a "covering" <note>original: *Sochek*</note> of the hatch on ships <note>original: *Oniyot*</note>, emerging from the "Womb" which is *Tiferet*, and sent to the *Atarah* <term>Atarah: The "Crown," another name for the Sefirah of Malchut/Kingdom</term>, for all the "Ships" <note>The author likely uses 'ships' as a metaphor for spiritual vessels or celestial bodies</note> and the waters in the Abyss would destroy the world. This comes to teach you that the "Ships" in the Abyss also correspond to the triad of **World, Year, and Soul** <note>original: *Olam, Shanah, Nefesh*, the fundamental dimensions of reality in the Sefer Yetzirah</note>. And so, too, does the "Living Speaking Being" <note>Humanity</note> correspond. And the rest is upon me to fulfill.

# Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

Remember and understand this: just as the sun and the shield of the Lord God <note>referencing Psalm 84:12, "For the Lord God is a sun and shield"</note> provide light for the souls and a throne above them, so too the master of the "Crown" <term>Keter: The highest and most hidden of the ten Sefirot (divine emanations), representing the Divine Will</term> acts amidst the decrees. Just as the sun is chosen, the shield of the Lord God enables our souls to navigate every detail of every world, from the day the Throne was poured out from the greatness and the setting of the decrees. This continues until the whole achieves its purpose in the debate of each individual part. 

Similarly, regarding the loss of cold in exchange for heat: there is no distinction between the objects themselves; this "curtain" <note>original: *Masach*</note> is merely the absence of cold. Yet, it is a settled matter that is quite confused, for behold, the sun and the Throne stay the night in thick darkness and the depths—darkness and noise are likened to the void. Therefore, it is said that the separation of this "Curtain" <note>original: *Vilon*, the lowest of the seven heavens in Talmudic cosmology</note> stands between the coldness of the soul of the "Noise" <note>original: *Ra'ash*, possibly referring to the angelic quaking</note> and the soul itself. These are the small souls who lack eyes, for "they have eyes but do not see" <note>Psalm 115:5</note>, and like the "way of the wicked," they are in darkness and do not know what makes them stumble.

Between the "shaping of its dough"—which is like saying "to the deaths" and "to the orders"—and its movement—which is like saying "to the arm of Strength" (**Gevurah**) and its "Exaltation" (**Tiferet**)—it shall be sold. This movement is the rest of the cold. "Remembering" is the greening of power and the conflict within the pocket, while in the body, it is the scratching of your right hand, like the "daughters of the morning." Because the six days of the Beginning <note>original: *Ma'aseh Bereshit*, the act of Creation</note> include the great and thought-out Law, and the prayer, etc., they became unified. For every righteous person is sanctified in their souls during the days of Creation, as it is said: *"Yours, O Lord, is the greatness, and the power, and the glory [Tiferet], and the victory, and the majesty"* <note>1 Chronicles 29:11</note>. 

And this is the value and the merit of the morning. Now, the place of the sun's knowledge and scent (**Tiferet**) is established upon its stand, and the path of its place is where all these vessels are known. This is the place where it sinks every single day to fix its boundary by day and its boundary by night. Since the night was called to the light of day, and the darkness was called night, you should therefore interpret the "Beginning of Existences" and the "Foundation of My Place" through **Chesed** (Kindness) and the knowledge of **Din** (Judgment) <note>original: *Karin*, a play on the Aramaic/Hebrew for "Judgment" or "Horns"</note>. 

This softens the harshness to bring life to the "Gatherings of the Earth." Know that the Earth is founded upon its foundation, beneath which is a void <note>original: *Gilyon*, literally "scroll" or "blank space"</note>, and above which is the support and the oversight of what is above and what is below. So too are the "Gatherings" <note>original: *Kenisot*</note>—they enter into one another. For the incense within them will be raised up by a foundation that is unique, and the wicked shall be shaken from it. Generations of the wicked will be tossed from it unto its end. From the founded land, it will be transformed from the **Attribute of Mercy** <term>Middat Ha-Rachamim: The divine mode of compassion</term> to the **Attribute of Judgment** <term>Middat Ha-Din: The divine mode of strict justice</term>.

Out of the darkness comes light. Even the physical matter <note>original: *Homer*</note> follows the structures of the power of the "Glory" <note>original: *Kavod*</note>. These two are turned toward **Chesed** (Kindness) by the "Living Beings" <note>original: *Chayot*, a high order of angels</note>. They were not created in actual deed, but their bodies stood by the power of the soul, like a void garment or an empty vessel without clothing. 

Therefore, the translation <note>referring to the Aramaic Targum</note> for the "unification of the gatherings of the noise" <note>original: *Ra'ash*</note> is: "and he shall shake the paths of the blind from it." It was transformed when they died; their seal was transformed like their bodies, without a soul, like an empty vessel. *"The light of the wicked shall be withheld"* <note>Job 38:15</note> through the concealment of the transformations. And those who pass through it are the "Light of the Righteous," which is the light created on the first day, destined to be searched for in the future. 

As for the "shaking" of the **Gevurah** (Strength), it occurs through noise and transformations as a breakthrough. This is what the Targum translates as: "and he will be prophesied from the dedication of your righteous choices, and for the wicked, he will give strengths." Everything is made by the "Vessels of the Sea" <note>original: *Kosi Yam*</note>. 

Know that this "Vessel" (or "Cup") is prepared to receive all decrees, just as the **Hyle** <term>Hyle: A Greek term used in medieval philosophy to describe "Prime Matter"—the formless substance that underlies all physical reality</term> is prepared to receive the forms of all existing things. However, no form exists permanently in the Hyle; rather, at every moment, it strips off forms and puts on others. Since there is no permanent form, it is called the "Vessels of the Sea" <note>original: *Kosi Yam*, a play on the word *Hiyuli*</note>, because it is hidden in the foundation of the earth.

This is the meaning of the warning Rabbi Akiva gave to his companions when they entered the **Pardes** <term>Pardes: "The Orchard," a metaphor for the highest realm of mystical ascension and secret knowledge</term> with him: *"When you arrive at the stones of pure marble, do not say 'Water, water!'"* <note>Talmud, Chagigah 14b</note>. This is because the nature of the "Chariot" <note>original: *Merkabah*</note> follows this path; for why is the place of the "Beginning" <note>original: *Bereshit*</note> related to the Hyle and the Chariot? <meta>This page continues the Introduction of the Ra’avad, moving from the conceptual nature of the "Curtain" and the primordial darkness into the structured emanations of the Sefirot and how the Divine manages the world through cycles of time and judgment. # Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

The soul of all existence is unified as a whole. As our Sages of blessed memory said: every single "degree" <term>Ma'alah: A level or step in the spiritual hierarchy, often referring to the Sefirot</term> encompasses all six degrees within itself. This is the "Secret of the Sapphire" and the "Secret of the Pomegranate," as hinted in the verse: *The Lord diminishes, the Lord gathers, the Lord makes small.* <note>Original Hebrew: *Yod-Yod S'met, Yod-Yod Keltim, Yod-Yod Haktinam*. These are likely mystical permutations of the Tetragrammaton (YHVH) associated with the divine process of contraction or *Tzimtzum*.</note> Therefore, they divided all of existence into four parts corresponding to the four seasons of the year. They said: The day has twelve hours—the first three <note>hours</note> He sits and engages in the Torah (**Binah**); <term>Binah: The Sefira of "Understanding," often associated with the source of the Torah's structure</term> the second three, He sits and judges the entire world (**Tiferet**). <term>Tiferet: The Sefira of "Beauty" or "Harmony," which balances mercy and judgment</term> When He sees that the world deserves destruction, He rises from the Throne of Judgment (**Gevurah**) and sits upon the Throne of Mercy (**Gedulah**). <term>Gedulah: Another name for the Sefira of Chesed (Loving-kindness)</term> The third three, He feeds the entire world—this is called "Mercy"—until the time of the afternoon assembly. The fourth three, He sits and plays with the Leviathan (**Yesod**). <note>Reference to the Talmudic tradition (Avodah Zarah 3b) where God spends time in play; in Kabbalah, the Leviathan represents the "circling" or "connecting" light of the Sefira Yesod.</note> This refers to the One who surrounds and teaches the "Schoolchildren" (**Malchut**). <term>Malchut: "Kingship," the lowest Sefira, representing the physical world and the recipient of all divine flow</term>

Regarding these "Little Ones," it is said: "Whom shall He teach knowledge? And whom shall He make to understand the message? Them that are weaned from the milk, and drawn from the breasts." <note>Isaiah 28:9</note> They summarized this as: *The living takes, the living does, the living desires, the living is not* <note>original: *M'chai K'chai Avir Chai B'it Lita*</note>—this is like a vision. These four parts correspond to the four seasons of the year.

It is known that the "Sahar" <note>original: *Sahar*, referring to a round disk or the moon</note> is hollow within; it is like a hollow cap on one side, and a covering on the other. Whoever is within it knows the inversions of its goodness, and it weeps in accordance with its existence inside. This is the announcement to the whole Rim (**Chesed**), its place is opposite **Da'at** (Knowledge) and above it. *Chesed* is translated <note>into Aramaic</note> as *Katreg*, and the *Da'at* is the "Place of the Rim." *Da'at* is for its "Radiance" <note>original: *Likornoto*, derived from *Keren* (horn/ray)</note>, which refers to movement. 

What has been made clear to me is that all forms of the "Masculine" are sent out from the **Tiferet** to the "Smallness" <note>the lower world</note> every single day. They are given permission to exist, to live, to die, to return to exile, to appear, to rise up and up, to descend and descend, and to be cut off from their roots. This occurs through the "Sun and Shield of the Lord," as mentioned previously. Therefore, it is said: "Our bread is like the dryness of the earth, and may the wicked be restrained from it." <note>Referencing Job 38:13, "That it might take hold of the ends of the earth, and the wicked be shaken out of it."</note> This means our "bread" is in the Land of Israel, which causes the removal of the wicked and the "Transformations" <note>original: *Temurot*, often referring to spiritual exchanges or bad influences</note> from it. Thus, they translated it <note>into Aramaic</note>: *To return to the sides of the land of Israel, and the sinners will be prevented from it.*

The explanation of this from the perspective of natural philosophy is that actions are completed in the world through their uniqueness. The "wings" <note>directions/elements</note> are changed, and regarding this, the Sages of blessed memory gave a secret in the Midrash on Ecclesiastes: "A generation goes and a generation comes," for many souls are born every day and many souls die every day.

From the "Set Aside" until the name... there is a division of spirit until the "Small End." From the East, the power of "Beings" <note>original: *Havayot*, referring to manifestations of God's Name</note> gives life, and the names of Beings bring death. Three Beings serve the **Malchut** (Kingship), and certain Beings, according to the strict law, cut off those who have broken their covenants. 

Now, through their radiance from the "Seventy Crowns of Israel," look toward the "Transformations." "It is turned as clay to the seal" <note>Job 38:14</note>: their form changes, some to be raised and raised, some to live and live, some to be cut down and cut down, and some to the wilderness and the wilderness. They desire to stand within the refined and purified Kingdom (**Malchut**). The whole is "turned as clay to the seal, and they stand as a garment."

Regarding the "Garments of the Kingdom" that are separated from the class of the "Kings": these are seen from the souls of the dead and their like when they expand to descend into the world. By drawing from the four elements, they receive a sort of temporary body for a time, until they appear to those standing there in a human-like form. To the created beings, these parts of light appear as humans. Sometimes their vision is to be seen by women or by the "disturbed," as with the "Sages of Sodom" <note>the people of Sodom</note> who saw the angels, and as with the wife of Manoah and Manoah himself, and as with Abraham and Sarah. They treated the angels like guests and labored for them; this is the secret of the "Kings." 

Therefore, sorcerers and those who "inquire of the dead" <term>Dorshei el ha-metim: A biblical prohibition against necromancy</term> require incense and vapors in the air so that the "matters" sent into the atmosphere can sparkle through them. Thus, sometimes the dead are seen in their likeness. This happens when the "veils" are removed for certain Sages, such as our Holy Rabbi <note>Rabbi Judah the Prince</note> who appeared even after his passing. This is the law for every species and every individual detail found. The "whitening" or clarification of them in their descent is called a **Refined Garment**. This is the meaning of the translation: *It is turned like the clay of their seal, and they are ready like a garment and a standing.*

It touches upon the mystical significance of God's daily routine, the nature of judgment, and the mechanism behind supernatural visions.</summary>

# Introduction of Rabbi Abraham, of blessed memory

And you shall write the "Small Birth" <term>Molad Katan: A calculation for the appearance of the new moon, used here to track spiritual cycles</term> within the guidance. All its "births" <note>Referring to the moments of planetary or lunar emergence</note> are blessed; this is an addition for those who consume, and that is an addition for everyone, until they are subdued and attain merit. The Altar is compared to the "Death of the Waters" <note>A mystical reference to the cooling or stabilization of primordial forces</note> for the sake of **Kindness** (**Chesed**). The sign of the Crab <note>The zodiac sign Cancer (Sartan)</note> is within the Wisdom of the Holy One, blessed be He, in the Spirit. You shall write "three" upon the tree (**Foundation**/**Yesod**). The "Gates of Death" of the right-hand wine shall be opened for you; every pious person and every species requires a "Gate." The souls of the dead are ushered through a key belonging to him, whether by "Death at the hands of Heaven" or by physical death.

And the "Gates of Shame" <note>Possibly referring to the trials or humiliations of the physical world</note> which spread over all the "Circles" <note>Original Hebrew: *Macholot* (dances or circular cycles). This refers to the cyclical nature of life's trials.</note> are, as it were, present as a reward: whether in life, in the desert, or in all the commerce of the cycle. 

Know for the sake of wisdom that the power called "Death" is not the absence of existence for all twelve "Places." Since the Soul <term>Neshamah: The highest, intellectual level of the soul</term> dwells there with the Spirit <term>Ruach: The middle, emotional level of the soul</term> and the Vital Soul <term>Nefesh: The lowest, physical level of the soul</term>, it is her portion in life and it is the **Glory of the Lord** (**Tiferet**). Observe that those who "cling to the born" (**Tiferet**) belong to the division of the **Ten Sefirot** <note>The ten emanations of God in Kabbalah</note>, for in **Beauty** (**Tiferet**) and the **Crown** (**Atarah/Malchut**) are the foundations of the twelve holy tribes. These are the twelve "Havot" <note>Places of life or settlements</note> through which "the soul of every living thing and the spirit of all human flesh" is held in His hand. 

The souls of the year and the three spirits possess twelve "Strengths" in **Beauty** (**Tiferet**) and twelve "Strengths" in the **Crown** (**Atarah**). Within them are the souls of the twelve tribes that encompass all of Israel. Behold, from the Divine Name (**Havayah**) comes the tribe that rules the soul. They called it the "House of Life," followed by the "House of Wealth," "House of Relatives," "House of Ancestors," "House of Children," "House of Sickness," "House of Marriage," "House of Death," "House of the Way of Exile," "House of Dominion," "House of Inquiry," and "House of Debt" <note>These correspond to the twelve houses of an astrological chart, mapping every aspect of human existence from birth to death and legacy.</note>.

<meta>This page continues the introduction attributed to the Ra'avad (Rabbi Abraham ben David of Posquières), a 12th-century legalist and mystic. The text shifts from general cosmological cycles into a deep exploration of the "Abyss" (Tehom), the mystery of death, and the four types of capital punishment as spiritual or natural archetypes.</meta>

### ->Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory<-

...those who give birth and those who ascend, etc. Every action of their path is thus. The "Living Creature" <note>Hebrew: *Chayah*, referring here to a high level of the soul or the celestial beings in Ezekiel’s vision</note> is considered like one who sells an entire ship <note>A reference to the Talmudic tractate *Bava Batra*, chapter *Ha-Mocher et Ha-Sefinah* (He who sells the ship), used here as a metaphor for the soul navigating the "sea" of existence</note> and those who travel upon it within the sea. Likewise, for every "Height" of three hundred leagues <term>Parsa: An ancient unit of distance, roughly 2.5 to 3 miles</term>, it extends from there through the "Tongue," <note>In the *Sefer Yetzirah*, the "Tongue" is the organ of balance between the spiritual and physical worlds</note> for it is nurtured from the Abyss and connects the parts. 

Regarding the "Living Creature" as a vessel: some of them contain pure waters that were created during the **Work of Creation**, and within every refuge is a "Greatness." Its zodiacal sign <note>Hebrew: *Mazal*, also meaning luck or destiny</note> is like the waters that covered the world until it was completed with the Name of *Hallel* <note>Praise</note>. It pours them out, and like them, it is a source, a depth, and a hidden treasure. 

The "Living Creatures" in the assemblies are the **Permutations of the Letters** of the Great and Sublime Divine Name—that is, the "Everlasting Waters." Their power is the world, their power is the soul, as I have explained in the commentary on the **Chapter of Creation** (*Bereshit*). These are the heavens that sweeten all the "Connections" <note>Spiritual bindings</note> from above. 

"And I walked in the search of the Deep" <note>A paraphrase of Job 38:16: "Hast thou entered into the springs of the sea? or hast thou walked in the search of the depth?"</note>. For in the "search" of the lower waters—though some explain this as reaching as far as the stars of the heavens—there are parts that are neither near nor far, neither firmaments nor water. "In the search of the Deep": meaning, I walked in the very heart of the Abyss. For everything that is "Soul" has a corresponding "Lamentation" in the Abyss. It is possible for the "Gate" to be closed until my "Ranks" <note>Levels or degrees of spiritual attainment</note>, and there are "Paths" and "Plain Script" concerning the "Abysses of the Moon."

"Have the gates of death been opened unto thee? or hast thou seen the doors of the shadow of death?" <note>Job 38:17</note>. Know that death is not the end of everything; rather, it is a transition between the letter *Mem* <note>The letter *Mem* often symbolizes water or the womb</note> and the Name, until the Name of "Death" (*Mavet*) is added. That is to say, when it exceeds its limit until it adds the "Death" of one of the species. This itself is the "Life of the Nothingness." 

"In the sweat of your face shall you eat bread" <note>Genesis 3:19</note>; everything is lacking the warmth of the wise. In the "Melting" and the "Secret of the Veil" within it, the "Consolations" die. It returns to them from the living beings, and for the flesh to turn to dust without its life-force. By this matter, it becomes the "Living Being of the Place" <note>A name for God is "The Place" (*HaMakom*)</note>, and likewise for every species: whether from the animal, the plant, or the silent stone—even in the world of Angels, the Spheres, the stars, and the constellations, and likewise in the World of the **Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: The ten divine emanations or attributes in Kabbalah</term>.

Each and every one has a Name according to its station, for the higher and the lower. Concerning these levels, it is said "Gates of Death"—as in, "Have the gates of death been opened unto thee?" These are the **Four Deaths of the Court**: **Stoning, Burning, Slaying, and Strangling**. 

Regarding these, in **Tractate Sotah**, it says that even though the four deaths of the court were abolished <note>After the destruction of the Temple, the Sanhedrin lost the legal power to execute criminals</note>, their divine equivalents were not abolished. He who would have been liable for **Stoning** either falls from a roof or is trampled by a wild beast. He who would have been liable for **Burning** falls into a fire or is bitten by a snake. He who would have been liable for **Slaying** is handed over to the government or robbers who kill him. He who would have been liable for **Strangling** either drowns in a river or is choked by a "Croup" <note>Hebrew: *Luvsi*, a respiratory ailment</note>, which is to say, a blockage. 

Why do we look down from a high place and say "Under"? Because the one who falls is "Under." Let the rod of the wicked be broken; therefore, the pious man is spared. This is written upon his heart: "Be careful of the trap." Below, there are several "Levels" <note>Or stories/floors</note> similar to the **Foundation of the World** (*Yesod Olam*). 

Regarding **Burning**: one falls headfirst or into a "Biting Fire," and it strikes him until he is bent to his knees, for there is nothing to support him. His strength should be in a holy place, in purity and cleanliness, but instead, it is in a place of "Vapor" and "Blows," not in "Exchanges." 

Likewise, when discerning the "Hard Flesh" <note>Decree</note> within the walk, he wraps himself in his "Folly" <note>Or perhaps "Pain"</note>. This leads to the "Two Pipes" <note>The windpipe and esophagus</note> which provide sustenance; one pulls this way and the other pulls that way, until the "Spirit of the Living God" is severed from him. Then, his mouth is opened, and they place lead <note>This describes the ritual process of "Burning" in Jewish law: pouring molten lead down the throat</note> into it, which enters him. It is thrown into his mouth, resembling the "Fire of Supreme Might" (*Gevurah*) with the "Palm" from below. It descends and burns his innards. 

These are the "Two Outstretched Arms," and there is something similar in the "Ladder" that is between the shoulders from all sides. The general rule of the matter: from the tip of the middle finger of the right hand to the tip of the middle finger of the left hand, when the two arms are outstretched equally—this is the "World that Sustains the Body." The "Body Garment" is refined; these are the "Enduring Letters." Therefore, we do not destroy the "Vessels of Understanding" <note>The physical organs</note>, which are hollow. Everything is with her, except for the "Vessel that Receives the Burning"—the external one, the "Lamps" in the Chariot, as the Name of the **Kingdom** (*Malkhut*) performs. And the "Repairers" uproot the one who is liable, down to his knees...

<meta>catchword: And they write.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

To receive the aforementioned assemblies—this is the secret of the verse: *Have the gates of death been opened unto thee? or hast thou seen the doors of the shadow of death?* <note>original: "פוגנו לך שערי מות ושערי צלמות תראם" — a variant citation of Job 38:17</note>. Therefore, the darkness is arranged to be revealed to you from the pathways of your seal, and as the pathways of your foundations for your soul, you shall wonder. 

Surrounding all these is the "Land of Water," meaning the gates of the Garden of Eden <note>Paradise</note>, the "Sixty-Three" <note>Hebrew: ס"ג (*Sag*), referring to a specific Divine Name associated with the realm of Binah/Understanding</note> properties until the "breadth of the earth." Behold, kindness and knowledge are completed. For after he arranged for us the way to begin your wisdom toward the "Crown of Israel" <note>The Sephirah of Tiferet or Malchut</note>, the morning to the land, and explained the judgments of the wicked who surround us like a sea, and the abyss was searched, and he explained the "gates of death" and the "gates of the shadow of death"—return and explain for yourself how the attribute of Kindness and Goodness resides within the Land of the Living. 

He called that portion the "breadth of the earth" <note>Job 38:18</note> because it is a power from the "Streets of the River" <note>Genesis 36:37, often interpreted as the overflow of *Binah* (Understanding)</note>, meaning Understanding, which contains no wave or darkness or <unclear>damage</unclear>. For now, the "Broad Place" is the number ten; go and separate the power of the "Arrow." This is the secret of the "Properties" <term>Techunot: In this context, astronomical or spiritual configurations</term> from the level of Understanding (*Binah*) until the "breadth of the earth" of the Garden of Eden. Behold, kindness and knowledge are completed, meaning all the details of the "Living Creatures" of the path will be established in the white light. 

It is "sown for the righteous" <note>Psalm 97:11</note> from the light of Understanding, and over all the movements and the drawing of power from the power of Strength (*Gevurah*) and the permutations to protect its place in the land of the Garden of Eden. Behold, without doubt, it is written: return to honor, and it is written: Kindness for all. For it passes through the world-firmament and passes to the <unclear>extremities</unclear> and the eternal duration. For you shall take it to our understanding, and you shall understand within his house. 

The explanation is: when our tribe and our mighty ones are milked for the "perfect ones"—meaning, return to our understanding and the kindness to our understanding—and when you understand, you shall restore every part of them to its place, its "house." And you shall rest there and give birth to the white light through the transition, and you shall be renewed like a "new creation," and the number of your days shall be many. 

The explanation of this is that the scripture says 180 <note>original: "קפ"</note> and the sign is 79 <note>original: "ועט"</note>. Lacking from them is the white light and your trap, and the surrendering of the whole day, and the loss to all according to the judgment of all the "children of change" <note>Hebrew: *Bnei Chalof*, meaning mortals or those who pass away, from Proverbs 31:8</note>; they are all servants as it was born in our portion. Therefore, when you take it and absorb every secret and essence into the soul, to its border which is the <unclear>life</unclear> of the circle, it is lacking <unclear>desire</unclear> for the earth. For when all the voices are gathered, the limit of its action is in the power of its excellence, and <unclear>the white light</unclear> descends to death for the <unclear>transgressors</unclear> to another youth for everyone judged like the "children of change." You shall turn and distance yourself, you shall understand and arrange, for he is obligated to be liable in the circumference of 300 <note>The letter *Shin*</note>, as it is written of "His House." 

Therefore, choose for yourself, for within it is their coming. For then you shall give birth according to the judgment of the "children of change," and the number of your days shall be many according to the power of death. There are four levels to this: the "diminished" face, the "added" face, and further the "overlapping" from the change, and the "secular" from the writing—and they are the "simple" and the "established," etc. And this is what is hinted at in the verse of the "Properties" until the "breadth of the earth," behold kindness and knowledge are complete. You have been measured for me within the knowledge of my heart for the <unclear>dimensions</unclear> toward Eden; a line of white light and complete knowledge. For the day of the path, the white light shall be established, and the drawing forth of 99 <note>original: "צט"</note> to protect its place.
