<meta>This page continues the Ra'avad's exploration of the soul's journey, transitioning from the celestial "Gates of Eden" mentioned on the previous page to the "Gates of Death" and the "Permutations" that govern the soul's exit from the physical world. It uses the prayer of King Hezekiah (from Isaiah 38) as a primary mystical proof-text.</meta>

### Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

And behold, in the observation of the soul, from among the souls there are twenty-two <term>Existences (Havayot)<note>The 22 letters of the Hebrew alphabet, viewed here as the building blocks of spiritual reality.</note></term> acting as the "material" that reaches the "House of 50" (N) <note>Representing the 50 Gates of Understanding (Binah) or prayer.</note> and the "House of 40" (M) <note>Representing the realms of death or limitation.</note>, and so it is for all of them. Sometimes the soul finds rest by way of grace—Heaven forbid—they ascended to one lodging and "he lived," while others are weakened by the fear of the Lord and the lack of their exile. And for one who is subject to <term>Excision (Karet)<note>Spiritual "cutting off" from the source of life.</note></term>, they are severed from the "House of Life" <note>original: "בית חיותו"</note> when their portion arrives. 

This is the secret of the phrase "My life and my death are prepared"; it does not depend on merit, and the word *chalu* (waited/sickened) is like the word *teluya* (dependent) upon another. When there is a deficiency in the body, a decree of death is issued upon it; this is the "Excision" (Karet) of the soul. The letters *H-L-V-B* <note>Numerical play on debts or obligations.</note> and *Ch-S-V-B* are counted as a debt of the transition. A deficiency in its "writing" <note>The spiritual record of the soul</note> brings about the removal of life. These are the "reproaches" mentioned regarding all living things that are written from the <term>Explicit Name (Shem HaMeforash)<note>The sacred, four-letter name of God, the Tetragrammaton.</note></term> to the *Kattatus* <note>A mystical or angelic rank/layer.</note>, and from the *Kattatus* to the *Matpechot* (Wrappings), and from the wrapping to the "Cursed Garment." Its leading is like the "daughter of the mixture" and the <term>Sephirot<note>The ten divine emanations in Kabbalah.</note></term>. 

To engrave it is like a burning daughter above them, leading to the "Permutations" that belong to death. As it says regarding the judgment in the manuscript: the exchange of the soul in the illness of Hezekiah <note>King Hezekiah's near-death experience in Isaiah 38.</note>, as I said in my imagination: "I shall go to the gates of the grave." For I opened with the "bitterness" and "to whom shall I go?" and I was severed from the "Trees of Life" unto the "Permutations." It follows the "Gates of Speech."

<margin>
This "N" (50) is deficient, for it is lacking due to those who "strike" it.
The "M" (40) is deficient in the permutation; it is a lack that blemishes itself.
The "Adorner" is from its strength and its resting place; there is yet power in the soul to choose.
Behold, here in the "Gate of Speech," it is more severe than those who "strike" it.
</margin>

**Explanation:** The secret of the "Permutations of Life" refers to the permutations that are "behind" and in "deficiency." I received this as the answer to "I am deprived of the residue of my years" <note>Isaiah 38:10</note> by way of the *Atarah* (Crown) and the *Pele* (Wonder). My rest and my sustenance are a "connection" from the *Y-L* of the *Atarah* and the *Y-H* of the "Perfect One." Therefore I said: "I shall not see the Lord, even the Lord, in the land of the living" <note>Isaiah 38:11</note>. This teaches that all death is categorized under the "Excisions." 

The *Y-H* <note>A shortened Name of God</note> is missing in the "Land of the Living" (at the level of *Atarah* / the Crown). "I shall behold man no more with the inhabitants of the world." My dwelling is plucked up and removed from me like a shepherd's tent; for the cycles of the "threads of my life," which were fit for my palm and my hints, have already become like barley, and the cycles of my life have passed. *Dori* (My generation/dwelling) comes from the language of "light" (*Orah*), and *Masuch* (Mixture) comes from the language of "Order" (*Sedura*).

It is revealed as the "Greek Goddess" <note>Perhaps a reference to a specific philosophical or external husk/Kelipah</note> under the "Higher Interpretation." "I have reckoned until morning; as a lion, so will He break all my bones." Let the Name be upon my head. And now, as it is revealed and publicized and goes outward from the "Chamber of my Faith," it is like a shepherd's tent—it resembles the "Prince of my Life." "In a moment" resembles the "Time of my palm," for he is the "Shepherd of Trust" until he is called a "Predator." 

The "Cycle of his fabrications" in the chapter "One Does Not Expound" <note>A famous chapter in the Talmud (Chagigah) dealing with forbidden mystical studies.</note>: "I have rolled up like a weaver my life." This is because, in the value of the "proximate feeling," it is like weavers, as it is said: "Who stretcheth out the heavens like a curtain." So too the "Existences" (Havayot) which are severed; the soul of every living thing is like my weaving. The secret is that when the days end—when my days have been nested within me—they are removed, as it is said: "They saw no good." 

The distinction in this is that it was cut and descended from the "Four and the Evening" <note>Likely referring to the four lower Sephirot or directions</note>, from Wisdom, Understanding, Greatness, Power, Crown, and Foundation. Above him is a "Tag" (Crownlet), and the lower *Atarah* (Crown) is for the "Striker." When the days of his life stand for him, he shall be utterly severed from the "Hidden Two" and the "Evening." "If I have rolled up like a weaver my life," from the head they are humbled, "from day even to night wilt Thou make an end of me." 

**Explanation:** From the depths that are called "Night" (at the level of *Atarah*), and it is the *Atarah* and our sub-levels. "From day even to night wilt Thou make an end of me"—meaning, You have completed and pardoned my days, and they have already failed me. My "Crown" is a language of "diligence," which is a "redeeming from the thorns," like the sin I bore. I have approached the "House of the Lord." "I have reckoned until morning; as a lion, so will He break all my bones; from day even to night wilt Thou make an end of me." All the "paved affliction," the "hindrance," and the "death" are established as a "Remnant" that was shown. 

And so there are in the "Permutations" twelve types of permutations. Even though they are "interrupted"—meaning they have no permutation as will be seen below—nevertheless, you must understand that there are twelve types of souls and twelve levels. 

And so further: his "bed" is called his "garment." And so further: his "bed" is called "Sapphire of my Words." To each of them is the "Face of a Man." To you, it is "almost covered." And so further down: "Sapphire of my Words," *Lotan* and *Shovan*. And now, *Lotan* who is from the head... *Dishan*, *Keri*, *Imas*, *Alan*, and *Makat*, and *Eival*, *Shepo*, and *Lukhas*, *Chur*, and *Akhas*, *Dishan*, *Chamdan*, and *Eshban*, and *Yitran*, and *Beran*, *Bilhan*, and *Akan*, *Avon*, and *Chalan* <note>These are the names of the Horite chiefs from Genesis 36, used here as mystical identifiers for spiritual forces or levels of the soul.</note>. Below the "Kings," and each one causes a power over them, and the Name proves this in the "Gates of Death" and the "Gates of the Shadow of Death"—meaning the entrances of Gehenna (Hell). And for each of them, there are judgments and a place to receive them.

Two circular diagrams illustrating the balance of spiritual and physical forces. 

The circle on the right represents the "Outer Forces" or the nations of the world. Inside are the names: **Amalek**, **Ken**, **Jokshan**, **Nebaioth**, and **Kedar** (appearing twice). The circumference is inscribed with Psalm 12:9: "The wicked walk on every side, when vileness is exalted among the sons of men."

The circle on the left represents the Divine Realm. The circumference lists the **Ten Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: The ten attributes or emanations in Kabbalah through which the Infinite interacts with the universe</term> in counter-clockwise order: **Crown** (Keter), **Wisdom** (Hokhmah), **Understanding** (Binah), **Kindness** (Hesed), **Strength** (Gevurah), **Beauty** (Tiferet), **Victory** (Netzah), **Splendor** (Hod), and **Kingship** (Malkhut). Inside are terms like **Prophecy**, **Ancient**, **Foundation**, and **Holy**.
</image-desc>

<margin>
In this cycle, regarding the **Transmigration of the Garment** <note>Hebrew: *Gilgul ha-Malbush*. This refers to the soul's transition between different "garments" or physical bodies</note>, it is "strange as his garment" <note>A wordplay on Isaiah 28:21</note> regarding Esau. Then, man changes and pays his debt. Likewise, in Chapter 19 of Tractate Shabbat, where Rav Nachman bar Isaac asked: "What is written: 'When you come into the standing corn of your neighbor'?" He said to him: "If one says to you, 'I am a sword and I will kill you,' ask him, 'What time is it?'" <note>A cryptic Talmudic reference used here to discuss the timing of judgment and physical survival</note>. In the section on "Come [to Pharaoh]," Chapter 7, why are they called "Children of the East"? As it is said, "They dwelt in the land of the Children of the East." There are two "Adams" <note>Men/Archetypes</note> there; then one sees the man, and the name is removed from this.
</margin>

To receive the severed palms—this is the secret of **Reincarnation** (*Gilgul*). "Will you search out the gates of death and the gates of the shadow of death?" Therefore, you shall lattice the windows to be rolled from the heights of your sustenance. In the pathways of the Mountains of Death, they shall take my soul. Remember every "passing through" of **Haran** <note>original: *Charan*. This refers to the biblical location but is used here mystically to signify a place of "wrath" or spiritual testing</note> designated for the gates of the Garden of Eden. Observe the "breadth of Haran" until all knowledge is complete. 

**Explanation:** The book returns to the "Leftward Path"—the trust in the "Crown of Israel" which is called "Haran." It details the judgments of the wicked, which are like heat in the treasures of the sea and the source of the Abyss. He explains the "Gates of Death" and "Gates of the Shadow of Death" to you: how the attribute of Kindness and Goodness returns by extending toward the designated land. The law-giver called the "breadth of Haran" so because it represents the elevation from the **Crown** (*Keter*) to **Understanding** (*Binah*), where there is no light or grasp. It is written: "For now the Lord has made room for us, and we shall be fruitful in the land" <note>Genesis 26:22</note>. This is the secret of "Observation" (*Hitbonenut*), derived from the word for **Understanding** (*Binah*), reaching the breadths of Haran in the Garden of Eden, where kindness and knowledge are completed in every detail. 

"This is the way where light dwells"—the light known to the righteous, stemming from the light of *Binah*. All is pleasure, and the power is drawn from the power of **Strength** (*Gevurah*). The exchanges of its place are in the "Gold of the Garden of Eden." Without doubt, it is written: "Light for all" and "Darkness for all," for "some to everlasting life, and some to reproaches and everlasting abhorrence" <note>Daniel 12:2</note>. For you shall take these to their border, and you shall understand the inscriptions of his house. 

**Explanation:** When we prepare and establish it toward the boundary of its place—Light to its boundary and Darkness to its boundary—and you understand the return of each to the "inscriptions of its house," then you will know that the "Double Conception" is born. You shall be sanctified like a chosen one from the beginning, and the number of your days shall be many. 

In this unique way, the **Sun** (<term>365</term>) <note>original: *shasah*, the number of days in the solar year</note> and the **Moon** (<term>354</term>) <note>original: *shnad*, the number of days in the lunar year</note>, from which come light and darkness, govern their entire day. Their loss is according to the law of all "children of transition" <note>Hebrew: *bnei chalof*, meaning mortals or those subject to change</note>, who are recognized through the reincarnation of the "Garment." Therefore, when you take it and place the entire day and the strength of the soul at its border—which is the secret of the circle above the earth—the consolation returns in the perfection of the border of its action, according to the level of its palm. Then <unclear>it returns</unclear> and descends to death, to its trouble or to the "youth" who consumed the law of the children of transition. From there onwards, you shall understand and pursue, for it is established to blow in his bosom <note>abbreviation in original?</note>, meaning the "inscriptions of the house." 

Therefore, it will be clarified to you that when it returns to **Dumah** <note>The angel in charge of deceased souls in Jewish tradition</note>, it is then born according to the law of the "children of transition," and the number of your days will be many according to the power of strength. There are four levels in this: the few, the added, the further added, and the great. Hear and understand this. This is what is hinted at in the verse: "Have you perceived the breadth of the earth? Tell, if you know it all" <note>Job 38:18</note>. My understanding is "grace" (*Chen*) over the enticement of our wickedness, and this is "knowing it all." "Where is the way where light dwells?" (<term>485</term>) <note>The numerical value provided in the text for this concept</note> and "where is the place of darkness?" (<term>354</term>) to settle its place. For the light of God stays.

## Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

And behold, in the surrounding of the souls, it is as if they have become like clay; arriving at the Seventh House of stumbling and the Ninth House of death, and so it is for all of them. Sometimes there will be a lightening of the soul’s burden, Heaven forbid, due to a transformation <note>original: "חלוף" — a change or substitution in spiritual status</note> on account of the Glory of the Lord and the order of its transmigration <term>gilgul: the cycle of reincarnation or the "rolling" of souls through different lives</term>. And one who has become liable for spiritual excision <term>karet: a punishment where the soul is "cut off" from its divine source</term> shall be cut off from the House of Life, and this is the "Excision of Death." This is the secret of the verse, *Let them live and their vision is for her* <note>A mystical reading of Job</note>; for in her merit the matter depends, and "the matter does not depend on luck" <note>original: "לא במזלא תליא מילתא" — a reference to the Talmudic debate on whether stars/fate (mazal) or merit (zechut) governs Jewish life</note>. And in the inner chamber, when the decree of his death was decided, the spiritual excision of the year was due to a debt of the past that was missing from his stroke.

And the requirement of death refers to those "Life-forces" mentioned, which are drawn from the Explicit Name <note>original: "שם המפורש" — the specialized, potent Name of God used in Kabbalistic meditation</note> for all the fragments, and from the natures to the transformations, and from the depths to the depths, and from the voices—and it is drawn between the extended power and the *Sefirot* <term>Sefirot: the ten divine emanations through which God interacts with the world</term>. They shall strike the power of the one who cleanses them toward the changes; if one is lacking, he dies, and to the dead, one is lacking for judgment. This is because of the change regarding the illness of Hezekiah <note>The Biblical King Hezekiah, whose life was extended by fifteen years after he prayed (Isaiah 38)</note>, according to the way I kept his paths, for I transgressed in my suffering.

And a portion was made alive and cut off, and the liberation of the lives of the transformations went into the "Gates of the Dance" <note>original: "שערי מחול"</note>. The explanation is: the foundation of the transformation of lives—meaning, toward the transformations—is lacking as in a dance. And as our Sages of blessed memory received: "I was visited through the Crown <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss> <note>Atarah: another name for the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom)</note> and his fear and his merit." And I considered the connection from the twelve of the Crown <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss> and from the Lord, blessed be He, in Beauty <gloss>(Tiferet)</gloss> <note>Tiferet: the Sefirah of Beauty or Balance</note>. He calculated his attribute for a change, and the Lord's mouth was like a burning anger <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss>. The liability <gloss>(Tiferet)</gloss> teaches that all the dead are cut off, and this is the secret...

...one who is lacking in the burning anger <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss>. The liability <gloss>(Tiferet)</gloss> belongs to the new vessel <gloss>(Tiferet)</gloss>. Furthermore, the Crown shall be added to one hundred and thirty-four <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss>. If not for the one who was struck and stolen from me in the "Dance of Thoughts"—the explanation is: the transmigrations of the lines of my life were lacking. It was for my life, for my silver, and my mercies have already been stirred. And behold, the transmigrations of my lives passed, the generations of the Kingdom of the Torah <note>original: "מלכות תורה"</note> (and this is from the language of "returning" to the Assembly).

He was hidden and stolen through a "stumbling of my life" under the Higher Solution in my life, in all my foundations <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss> and there. And it shall be upon his pressure, and now it is stolen and made known, and he shall rest outside of me. The explanation: it left me in the "Dance of Thoughts." Our Sages of blessed memory said: "I was a cow" <note>A metaphor for the animal soul or a specific talmudic reference</note>, because it was almost as if my soul was silenced, for he is the "wickedness of death" upon the solution of the ladder of the light.

And in the transmigration, as in the "Impulse of the Chapter," I doubled the lines of the wisdom in the liability, for it was set in the liability of the flight. As it is written: *It is better that my name be like a curtain*—so are the lives for the solution of the flight. The soul of every living creature is at ease in the excision. And the mountain—for when the days were finished, the days of my nest were like clay. From me, it was excised from a distance; I did not see good. And in this uniqueness, for the opening ceases from all the "Heel of Thought" and the like, and transmigration, and Might, and Power, and Glory. And the "Higher Explanation" and the "Repair of the Crown" below, as the days of his life stood like clay, he shall be cut off from the Living Creatures upon the Small Impulse.

And in this arrangement, I was careful regarding the impulse of the lines of the "Dripping" <note>original: "דלף" — possibly referring to the 'dripping' of divine grace or the 'leakage' of the soul</note>, and in its suffering from day until night. *You make an end of me* <note>original: "תפלומיכי" — a corruption or variant of the biblical "תשלימני" (tashlimeni) from Isaiah 38:13</note>. The explanation: from the place called "Dripping" <gloss>(Atarah)</gloss>, and it is the Crown. "From day until night You make an end of me" — the explanation: to seal the acceptance and the forgiveness of my days, and I have already been circumcised. Is it that I became liable for good to be visited? The "Opening of the Chapter" regarding the princes because of the sin of the refuge in the middle of the law of my graves, to the House of the Lord <gloss>(to Atarah)</gloss> I was until morning. Just so, He will break all my bones; "from day until night You make an end of me," and all the escaped and stirred essence and life and the existence in that span. And so it is in the transformations: twelve types of transformations, even though the Sukkot <note>The festival of Booths, but here likely referring to "coverings" or "spiritual canopies"</note> have lives and transformations for them, as follows. Nevertheless, you must understand that there are twelve types of souls for the ears.

<margin>
This is the "Gap in my Life" which is
the return of the gap of the depth.
The "Choice of the Gap of the Depth,"
and the pleasantness of this, and my soul spread out.
This is aimed precisely at the soul.
</margin>

(Thus further, our drawing-down is like the Crown, and further from its supply is like the "Hairy Goat" <note>original: "שעיר המרי" — possibly a reference to the scapegoat or a demonic force in the wilderness</note>. And for every place, a new face for you for a moment. And yes, all below is like the Hairy Goat, and it is consumed and struck and strangled and added to the courtyard. Behold, lives are upon him, and from the strike and the cloud of his end, and he was a living creature <note>original: "חיה"</note>. Now they are judged, changed, and overturned. By the law of their transformation, and sin upon sin, and a "change" below from the Kingdom. And all that is revealed upon you... they sit in the Gates of Death and in the Shadow of Death, meaning at the entrances of Gehenna <note>Purgatory/Hell</note>. And for every one of them, there are judgments and a place to receive.)

## Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

<margin>And after</margin>After the *Composition of Infusion* <note>original: "Hibur HaNisuch" — likely a reference to a specific mystical treatise on the soul's "pouring out" or its connection to the divine.</note> came into our hands, we must choose a small portion of it by way of allegory and as a response. What is this "allegory"? It is the loss of <term>Existences (Havayot)<note>The various manifestations or permutations of the Divine Name that sustain reality.</note></term> and the "profaning of death"—God forbid! Existence is such that all composite elements, when a sin is found, have the strength to return—and it is good for the element to return to its source—until the whole returns to air, and water to water, and dust to dust. If so, regarding the soul, one must also know to say that its "loss" is actually its return to its source, toward the outside. Beyond all this, there is a division beyond it that is a pleasure and delight for the soul; more than this occurs at the time it returns to the God who gave it. Therefore, you should know that those who disagree and explain the Torah according to nature and materialism [are mistaken], for the <term>Intellect (Sechel)<note>In medieval philosophy, the "Intellect" is the highest part of the human psyche, the link to the Divine.</note></term> is like a glue to the living soul. This is merely its existence. A knowledge of the foundation of the intellect according to the truth has entered into us from among the perplexed. For the "shaking" of a sickness is like a glue to the imagination within the soul when the "thread of fullness" <note>A mystical or biological term for the life-force or a specific vessel/nerve.</note> rests upon it and shakes it. Today, it has become easier; it returns to being a bit of the <term>Elemental Heat (Chom HaYesodi)<note>The "innate heat" which medieval medicine believed was the primary engine of life in the body.</note></term> which, in the rest of the soul and the rest of the sick person, will rest in the elemental heat through the power of imagination that reached it from the shaking of the sickness and its death. And behold, the elemental heat is like glue to the living soul, and in the end, heat upon its [assigned] heat, until the "Capture" <note>The angel or force of death.</note> that descends from the "Mountain of Speech" <note>Sinai, or perhaps a reference to the throat/breath.</note> meets it and rests upon the living heat. Furthermore, the wrath of the Lord's fury shall descend upon it, and a "shaking" shall stir upon the womb of the wicked and rest there. This is also the rule for one who dies of cold: his sickness, his "veils," and his "frosts" <note>original: "dokyo ve-pispitis" — likely corrupted medical terms for cataracts or membranes.</note> shall join together from all the snow and hail [reserved] for the day of battle and war. 

The <term>Targum<note>The Aramaic translation/paraphrase of the Bible.</note></term> translates: "Where is the day of battle? For my rest, for its descent like an inn or a lodging place." 
And it is possible that in the future to come, the Holy One, blessed be He, is destined to blaze in a portion with the enemies of Israel, like a mountain that is melted, and a cloak of glory. "And I will show wonders in the heavens and on earth: blood, and fire, and pillars of smoke." 
This is also the rule regarding the "precious fruit" of snow and hail for the day of the path and the "division of the lightning-flash" (*chaziz*). The explanation of *chaziz* is "Prayer," [used] to fight; and the Shield of the Lord of Hosts is in his hand for the life of all those who are "drawn" from world to world, even to this world of ours. 

And it is known that the Hidden World is called *Kedem* (Ancient/East), as it is said: "The eternal God (*Elohei Kedem*) is a dwelling place." Therefore, the Holy Spirit (*Ruach HaKodesh*) is called from there "The Ancient/Holy Spirit" (*Ruach Kadish*). 
And it is known that the Sun and the Shield of the Lord of Hosts is the leader of the world according to twelve <term>Existences (Havayot)<note>The twelve permutations of the four-letter Name of God, corresponding to the months and zodiac.</note></term>. Every single Existence has a nature and a specific virtue for known matters. 

And behold, I open this secret to you: Know that when the Name of "The Child" <note>The specific permutation governing birth.</note> rises, life, health, and conduct will be in its hand. Behold, the Sun and the Shield of the Lord of Hosts, in its Existence, breaks over the "House of the Child." He sends life to all that is found—for the wise man, this is not in vain—but rather according to the quantity of the "Portion" alone. The number of his days of life will change: for this person, he shall die at one hundred years old; for another, "in his day"—meaning, on the day of his birth; for another, in his month. For him, it was written so in the "Holiday of the Beginning" <note>Rosh Hashanah, the New Year.</note>. And so it is with every single number. 

How much more so is there a difference and a change in the years of life between one species and another—each species of living creature. And so too among the species of plants: there is that which changes in a day, and there is that which changes in a month—it was written for it in its passing from the Heavens. And so too in the mineral realm: the time of its existence is established for every single species and for every individual among the individuals of that species. And so too in the world of the Spheres, and so too in the stars, and so too in the Intellects, and so too in the world of the Angels and the Chariots, and so too in the world of the <term>Sefirot<note>The ten divine emanations/attributes in Kabbalah.</note></term>. 

And behold, for every single life, it must have a known path through which the flow of life is stamped into that individual. And so it is for every single individual among the individuals of living existence. 
And this is what is said to him: "Life is the path and the division of the flash (*chaziz*)"—which is Prayer—for the bringing of life to all other types of decrees for every individual among the individuals of existence. And he shall scatter the "East Wind" (*Ruach Kadim*) upon the earth. 
Therefore, the Targum translates: "Behold, for the path it is divided in light, and he shall scatter the East Wind upon the earth." 
Furthermore: "He divided a channel for the torrent, and a way for the flash (*chaziz*) of the voices." The explanation is: He furthermore divided a portion for the torrent toward the Abyss—the paths of life below...

Explanation of what our Teacher, of blessed memory, wrote in this note <note>original: "pitka" — a small slip of paper or instructional note.</note>

### Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

The path of life leads upward for the wise <note>A quote from Proverbs 15:24</note>, for there is no excuse when you are taken to your border and when you understand your house as if you are inside it, to see the shrouds for the pure and the destruction of the congealed memories of the heathens. Know that for him, there is a birth and the number of your days is great. The knowledge of God is thus found in the generations, and the count of your days is like streams. It is known that you have sustained a portion of the fire; a "portion of the face" <note>An idiom for personal attention or favor</note> in your eyes is like yesterday. If so, these days are seventeen thousand years <note>original: "iz alapim"</note>. All of this is hinted at in the "exalted" letters of the calculation of eighty-two, and the number of your days is two hundred and nine <note>Based on Gematria, the Hebrew system of assigning numerical values to letters</note>. Up to three hundred and sixty, they equal the <term>Gematria<note>The kabbalistic method of interpreting words based on their numerical value.</note></term> of one thousand and twelve—meaning twelve thousand years of the cycles of **Snow** <note>original: "SLG" (Sheleg). In this mystical context, snow represents the cooling or crystallization of divine energy.</note> to make known that even in the **Attribute of Mercy** <note>original: "Midat HaChesed" — the divine quality of benevolence</note> there is a mixture of **Judgment** <note>original: "Din" — the divine quality of severity and law</note>, for it has the cycles of **Snow**.

Know that there are sixty types of **Snow**. One of them is the "division of every tree," and we shall remove the burdens and they shall be dismantled... and they were the ascent of the congealed as our kings, and their motion is like congealed milk. There is "lacking snow" which is like the appearance of the points of the sunrise and from the forces of the horn... in the place of lack, and they shall be sanctified. There are some of them that return to establish the "Pered" <note>original: "Pered" (mule/division) — perhaps a reference to the four levels of interpretation: Peshat, Remez, Derash, Sod</note>, and some that return to deviate as **Snow**. All of them rise from the earth to the canopy in the air, covering all the **Mercy** that acts upon them. Outside the Land, the vapors rising from the water and the earth are like the clouds called "Great All," and they themselves are acted upon to be the storehouses of **Snow** and the cycles of **Hail** <note>original: "KLD" (Barad). Hail represents a more severe, solidified form of Judgment than snow.</note>.

With these, the Holy One, blessed be He, takes vengeance upon the wicked, as when He "withholds His covering for the time of distress, for the day of battle and war" <note>A reference to Job 38:23</note>. For with stones of hail <note>original: "Avnei Barad"</note> He took vengeance against Pharaoh, and against the kings of the Land of Israel in the days of Joshua. So too, every single day, there is payment from the wicked, from "Halem" <note>a mystical term</note> to **Snow** and from "Haleg" to "them." Thus, the exchanges shall be cut off and the clouds shall end. They shall return to be as the book of Amos says: "under the soles of the feet" of the congealed <note>foundations</note>. For when their wickedness is upon them, they shall be "ashes under the soles of the feet" of the congealed. For it is reversed, and "the clay becomes as the seal" <note>A reference to Job 38:14</note>.

And the sound of the **Snow** beneath the **Throne of Glory** <note>original: "Kise HaKavod"</note> comes from the powers of the cycles of **Snow** and the cycles of **Hail**. This is the offspring, and later it is written: "this is the translation," and the clay is for the **Mercy** of the world... "He sends forth His snow like wool" <note>original: "kurtolei talga"</note>; "He scatters the frost like ashes" <note>original: "kedra tachmei"</note>. And in His cold, the cycles of **Hail** are the "mortgage" <note>A legal/mystical term for a debt or guarantee</note>, in the language of the wise...

<margin>
This means the congealing stands and we are tested by it and by the frost. For by this scripture, He takes vengeance upon the wicked; when He withholds His covering for the time of distress, for the day of battle and war. And the translation of "He gives snow like wool" is to awaken the cry of their children. And their frost for the day of battle, for Pharaoh and his groups... and it is necessary for all the paths to be in a circle within the **Sephira of Malchut** <note>The final Divine Emanation, representing Sovereignty and the physical world.</note> corresponding to the "fatty parts" <note>original: "Chalvei" — referring to the fats of the sacrifices or the "milky" white light of mercy</note> of the Tabernacle, and there they are... and 17 portions.
</margin>

While the previous page discussed the soul and the "cycles of snow" as a manifestation of the Attribute of Mercy, this page shifts to a physical and mystical analysis of the elements—specifically how water transforms into ice, snow, and rain through the influence of the constellations Kimah (Pleiades) and Kesil (Orion).</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

<warning>There is a brown liquid stain in the upper-middle section of the page, obscuring a few words in the first paragraph.</warning>

Let us return to observing the "Table" <note>The "Table" here refers to the structured arrangement of the physical and spiritual worlds.</note> and its order. As it is said, "the waters shall become wise." This is the explanation of the waters that surround the essences of the firmaments; during their formation, they exist in the ground, and then they return to their previous state through pressure. Out of their coagulation, they are supported and fixed into the shape of the firmaments and the structure of the **Heavenly Host** <term>Pamalia shel Ma'ala<note>The divine "entourage" or assembly of angels that governs the natural order.</note></term>. It is already known that those who "lick" <note>likely an OCR error for "those who walk"</note> in the heart of the sea... and also the letters regarding the "bricks" of ice, as we have learned elsewhere: "He turned them into bricks," <note>original: "shavi l'veinim"</note> which are concepts that will be discussed later. Behold, the waters within the ice are examined and squeezed into existence, and once they depart, the sources are joined to the "inner faces," <note>original: "panim"</note> moving and hardening into possessions. Behold: **the freezer** (a), **the frost** (b), **the snow** (c), **the ice-block** (d), **the rain** (e), and **the hail** (f)—all of these are species <unclear>of them</unclear>. These are the "Ten" <note>Referencing the ten Sephirot or divine emanations</note> which are tested through the pressure of the water. This is the meaning of "Can you bind the chains of the Pleiades <note>original: Kimah</note> or loose the cords of Orion <note>original: Kesil</note>?" <note>A quote from Job 38:31</note>.

Know that **Kimah** <note>The Pleiades constellation</note> is a name for a solid substance formed by the power of binding on the side of the water. Therefore, it possesses great coldness. Because of the moisture of the rainwater found in Kimah, it binds the fruits. Every wise person will understand the word: "chains" <note>original: ma'adanot</note> means a knot or a bond, for it binds all separated things and makes them into a single fluid.

**Kesil** <note>The constellation Orion</note> comes from the side of **Strength** <term>Gevurah<note>The divine attribute of Judgment, Power, and Restriction.</note></term>, and therefore there is great heat within it. Out of the heat of Orion, the fruits are ripened and opened. If so, "the cords of Orion you shall open." And there are "chains of the Pleiades"—meaning, can you stop the fruits associated with Pleiades from resting, or the small ones from being poured out? For in His great kindness, He joins together to restore everything, and from the rain, the fruits and the righteous are benefited. Whereas Orion is specifically designated to ripen the fruits and to purify the changes until they are made... <note>The text here becomes highly elliptical/symbolic</note>. Therefore, out of the accompaniment of all the souls, you can "loosen the cords of Orion," for you are able to untie them. Behold, Pleiades is that which binds all accidental matter, plants, and joints; it is the binder. Orion, however, is that which melts, cuts off, and consumes.

Thus, the "chains" <note>or "delights"</note> are attributed to Pleiades because it provides the "delights of a king." But Orion "draws" its strength and gives things even in judgment and law. This is what you should consider: how far the wisdom of the Founder reached regarding His secret. He placed it in a row and associated Orion with the **Scorpion** <note>The zodiac sign Scorpio</note>. You already know the secret of the twelve essences and the thirty-two constellations. Since Orion's place is in the **Strength** <note>Gevurah</note> of the "Dragon," <term>Teli<note>The "Teli" is a mystical concept in the Sefer Yetzirah, often interpreted as the axis of the universe or the constellation Draco, which directs the movements of the spheres.</note></term> in the path of the "hollow and bitter," gold comes from it... a trust in materiality. Therefore, the distance of the star called Orion is toward the south... and it "kills the camels" if they follow it.

Regarding "the chains of the Pleiades," the **Aramaic Translation** <term>Targum<note>The ancient Aramaic translation of the Bible.</note></term> says: "The knots of the cluster of Pleiades." <note>original: "katrei moleh"</note>... and for "the cords of Orion you shall open," they translate Orion as "the giant" <note>original: "nephila"</note> in the language of the Talmud.

There is also in "the chains of the Pleiades" the weight of the Upper Essences which hover over the path of the **Dragon** <note>Teli</note>... and behold, the one born of matter, his soul follows the paths of the spheres. Therefore, "the cords of Orion you shall open" refers to those supported by the "tail of the Dragon" and the five essences below. For the five essences are the upper ones in form... and in kindness, the world is called out of all judgment. The influence of Pleiades is numbered from the **Supreme Crown** <term>Keter Elyon<note>The highest of the Sephirot, representing the Divine Will.</note></term>... to renew the worlds. It is a "face" <note>Partzuf</note> that participates in it, for it is like a "mother hen" <note>A traditional Jewish identification for the Pleiades cluster</note> that takes the moisture... and all pass through the judgment and law of the Above. This is the meaning of the Targum: "The knots of the cluster."

<meta>stamp at bottom: Sefer Yetzirah</meta>

<summary>This page provides a Kabbalistic and astrological interpretation of Job 38:31, contrasting the constellation Pleiades (Kimah), which binds and solidifies matter through cold, with Orion (Kesil), which ripens and dissolves matter through the attribute of Judgment (Gevurah). The Ra'avad links these celestial bodies to the "Teli" (cosmic axis) and the transformative states of water, ice, and snow.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the Ra'avad’s cosmological discourse. While the previous page discussed the soul and the "cycles of snow" as a manifestation of the Attribute of Mercy, this page shifts to a physical and mystical analysis of the elements—specifically how water transforms into ice, snow, and rain through the influence of the divine attributes (Sefirot) and the scriptural descriptions found in the Book of Job.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

He portioned out for good; do not spare the paths he brought up on high. And "who divided paths for the clouds," <note>original: "mi phileg netivot le-ananim"</note> observing the waters in the sounds, and their height toward the bolts of the firmaments and in the depths. And "there is He who divided a channel for the overflowing water" <note>Job 38:25</note>; who divided channels and paths for the flood, which comes from the attribute of **Lovingkindness** <term>Chesed; the divine attribute of expansive benevolence</term>. Some of these paths are drawn toward every living species and every individual detail of the fundamental elements, and some are drawn toward the explanations for the scripture concerning the "striking of waters": for frozen waters, for hot waters, for salty ones, for surplus waters, and for salt-sea waters.

He also divided the paths that are drawn from the attribute of **Severity** <term>Gevurah; the divine attribute of judgment, restraint, and power</term> toward the inner realms. For Severity is like the winter lightning in its freezing clarity, seen in its numerical value as two paths. Some are drawn toward the foundation <note>the element of Yesod</note> and some are drawn toward the transformations. And this is the Aramaic translation of the verse: "Who prepared and leveled the path for the flood to descend from above, and a path for the sound of the thunder's lightning." <note>original Aramaic: "tatar'ges ve-chamar..."</note> Because the waters are influenced by the power of Lovingkindness, while the great saltiness comes from the side of Severity, it is from this interaction that the sound of thunder and lightning is produced. "And who divided a channel for the overflowing water"—as if to say: "I have created many hairs, and each and every hair has its own follicle and is written by itself; for if even two hairs were to draw from a single follicle, they would destroy and darken the eye of a man." <note>This is a famous Talmudic teaching from Bava Batra 16a, where God explains the precision of creation to Job.</note> And regarding "a way for the lightning": "I have created many drops in the thick cloud, and each and every drop has its own perforation and is sent out by itself; for if even two drops were to descend from a single perforation, they would wash away the entire world." Similarly, "I have created many lightnings, and for each and every lightning I created its own path; for if two lightnings were to emerge from one place, they would destroy the entire world." All these were made with Knowledge, Understanding, and Wisdom, so that it might be "on the earth, where no man is; on the wilderness, wherein there is no man" <note>Job 38:26</note>.

Those "windows" and "wildernesses" that were hidden at their creation from human beings—I have protected them <note>The Hebrew root "chasiti" is interpreted here as protection or concealment</note>, meaning the power of the strength for human beings; and for their life, I have blotted out the [harsh] threads for the wise of the "enclosure" <note>original: "machtur"</note> so as to elevate them toward Lovingkindness and toward humanity. And this is its translation: "To bring rain upon the land where there is no man, and the wilderness wherein there is no son of man." "To satisfy the desolate and waste ground; and to cause the bud of the tender herb to spring forth" <note>Job 38:27</note>. It is known that the "desolate and waste" are prepared for the wicked, but the dew of light shall be lost like the perfect, and the wicked shall be removed when it comes. For a moment, their forgiveness is spoken of in a place of glory, a good rule, and do not let it be in trial here, as is the way with "him," and "all of them like a wasteland where no man passed and no man dwelt there." Therefore, He causes it to rain upon all the earth to satisfy the most vigorous of all species and to cause the source of the grasses to sprout; and through them, they shall be elevated from the plant to the living, to the [celestial] spheres, to the angels, and to the constellations. Therefore the Aramaic translation says: "To satisfy the parched and thirsty land, and to make the buds of the grass grow." For satisfying the creatures of the earth is for the purpose of elevating the plants.

"Hath the rain a father?" <note>Job 38:28</note>. The explanation is that the rain which descends comes from the Lovingkindness of the Lord and His Severity. Its substance and the goodness of its spirit—this is water. And all of these are examined from the power of **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah; the first flash of divine intellect</term> which is in its frozen heights, a sea on the right.

"Or who hath begotten the drops of dew?" <note>Job 38:28</note>. For the rain descends by weight, but the drops of dew are born through the power of **Understanding** <term>Binah; the analytical or maternal aspect of divine intellect</term> that is within Lovingkindness. "And may God give thee from the dew of the heavens." Therefore the Aramaic translates: "Is there a father for the rain? Or who begot the droplets of the dew?"

"Out of whose womb came the ice?" <note>Job 38:29</note> "And the hoary frost of heaven, who hath gendered it?" The explanation is that from the power of the **Crown** <term>Atarah; often referring to the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingship) as it receives from above</term>, in which is the Understanding that is in the clarity of the Lord, the ice comes—like the appearance of a terrible ice-crystal. From it, the firmaments were formed, rising to the level of the "frozen ones." Behold, their secret is the frost, and the secret of "crystal" and "flour" <note>possible reference to the white appearance of frost</note>. The Lovingkindness touches the matter of the dew, but it is not included in the "Back" <note>Gevurah/Severity</note>, for not all of its blessing was congealed. The translation says: "From whose womb did the ice come out? And the frost of the heavens, who gave birth to it?"

<summary>The Ra'avad provides a mystical commentary on Job 38, explaining how rain, dew, ice, and lightning are governed by specific Sefirot (divine attributes). He emphasizes the precision of creation, noting that every hair and raindrop has its own unique path to prevent the destruction of the world.</summary>

<keywords>Ra'avad, Job 38, Sefirot, Chesed, Gevurah, Binah, Chokhmah, Rain, Dew, Ice, Cosmology, Targum, Divine Providence</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the Ra'avad’s mystical introduction, moving from the cosmological movements of the heavens to the internal "heavens" of the human soul and body. It transitions from discussing the divine attributes (Sefirot) in the macrocosm to their manifestation in human anatomy, particularly the kidneys and the heart.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

Regarding the soul, whose beginning is in the firmaments <note>original: "beraqim"—likely a transcription error for "reqi'im" (firmaments)</note>, let them go and learn, for they have understood you. Every bride is perfected and known; how she shall serve and be taught for His love. And "know My Name," for they called My Name "to the Lord," <note>original: "l'Hashem"</note> meaning "God," for he knew My Name in the **Secret of Knowledge** <term>Sod ha-Da'at; the mystical bridge between the upper and lower intellects</term> <gloss>Tiferet</gloss>. 
Observe the glory of my father, and my sigh is with him—the wise of the soul—to solve the arrangement of the firmaments, that they should dwell and watch over you. "Who has put wisdom in the inward parts? Or who has given understanding to the heart?" <note>Job 38:36</note>. The explanation is: "Who has put in the kidneys," which are the covered parts <note>original: "tuchot"</note> and the secret of **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah; the first spark of divine intellect</term>, for the kidneys provide counsel to a person. "Who has given to the heart" <gloss>Tiferet</gloss> "that which joins with **Understanding**" <term>Binah; the analytical faculty of the divine intellect</term>. For the Lord, we are crowned and prayed to, those who receive from Understanding. 

It is known that the "pocket" <note>likely referring to the vessel or containment</note>, the place for all things, is composed of branches. There, the sensation is greatest, for the Sustainer is not divided from the portion called the "Tree" by way of the paths called "branches." Regarding this secret, it is said: "I will bless the Lord who has given me counsel; my kidneys also instruct me in the night seasons" <note>Psalm 16:7</note>. Because Wisdom requires arrangement in its existence and is related to physical action, therefore all Wisdom and Understanding are "simple intellects," and **Counsel** <note>Etzah</note> is what introduces order into Wisdom and Understanding until it reaches physical action. This is the secret of "With the Lord" <gloss>Binah</gloss> "He took counsel, and He instructed Him" <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. 

The reason for this is that the intellects are surrounded on every side like salt <note>perhaps referring to preservation or permeation</note> and are sealed within them. Therefore, they are called *Tuchot* (inward parts), which means "covered," from the language of "plastering a house" <note>Hebrew: "tach et ha-bayit"</note>. Regarding this secret, it hinted and said: "Who <note>original: "Mi"—a mystical name for the attribute of Binah</note> has put wisdom in the inward parts?" for the star is called "Who," as it is said, "With whom <note>Mi</note> did He take counsel, that he might instruct Him?" She receives from the sparks of Wisdom and spreads through the "harp" <note>likely a metaphor for the heart or the Sefirah of Yesod</note> toward the inward parts the flow of Wisdom that comes upon it. "Or who gave understanding to the rooster?" <note>Job 38:36</note>, providing fruit to the **Foundation of the World** <term>Yesod; the attribute of generation and connection</term>, and Understanding to the order of the **Kingdom** <term>Malkhut; the final attribute, representing the manifest world</term>. From them, Wisdom exists in the kidneys, and is refined for Understanding <gloss>Atarah/The Crown</gloss>. 

Know that the power of procreation has three centers: the **Brain** for the seed, the **Heart** for the spirit, and the **Liver** for the boundary <note>likely referring to the physical maintenance/nutrients</note>. From there, the liver sends the power of the boundary to the kidneys, and it flows through the kidneys. After the power of the kidneys has been explained to you, and the transformations of the boundary which "cooks" <note>metabolizes or prepares</note> through the action of the "three thirties" <note>a cryptic reference to mystical numerical divisions</note> toward male and female; and because the "cooking" is drawn from **Knowledge** <note>Da'at</note> in the heart, it is therefore said: "And Adam knew Eve his wife" <note>Genesis 4:1</note>. Behold, through their action in the "branch" of the kidneys, in the action of the "Root" which arouses nature and Wisdom, etc. This is because, in the value of the kidneys, they receive from everything that is above them; they are the sources of reception for the **Crown** <note>Atarah/Malkhut</note>, and thus the soul is born, which is included from "Being" <note>Yesh</note>.

There is also one "Who put wisdom in the inward parts (the kidneys)," or "Who gave the rooster <note>original: "sekwi"</note> understanding" to distinguish between day <gloss>Tiferet</gloss> and night <gloss>Atarah</gloss>. Know that just as there are four seasons <note>Tekufot</note> in the year through the movement of the sun, so too there are four seasons in the kidneys and in the movement of the day. Just as the sun is prevented from returning from the four corners... the Will, in its daily movement, when it moves to encompass these four corners, creates a "season." This is the secret: the four corners are between the divisions of the night and the twilight of the day, and in the middle of the night and the middle of the day. Behold, in these four times—and the time of each and every one of these—the **Heavenly Host** <term>Pamalia shel Ma'ala; the celestial retinue of angels</term> returns each to the labor they were made to do. 

Therefore, when the "understanding" of one of these four times reaches the spirit of the rooster—whose root is in the Foundation of the World—it understands the strike of its root. Immediately, it claps its wings like its root and gives strength to the intellect. It stands at the time they are receiving dominion, each one an official over his labor. Therefore, the Masters of the Talmud, when they wished to punish a person, would act at these known times, as is explained in **Tractate Berakhot** <note>a volume of the Talmud dealing with prayers and blessings</note>. 

And behold, I will tell you one story in the Holy Tongue, where the name of the deed is mentioned: a certain heretic <note>original: "min"</note> used to trouble **Rabbi Joshua ben Levi** excessively. The Rabbi set his mind to punish him. He tied a rooster to the leg of his bed and watched it, waiting for the hour of the "oath" <note>the specific moment of divine judgment, when the rooster's comb turns white</note>...

<summary>This page explores mystical anatomy, linking the kidneys and heart to the divine attributes of Wisdom and Understanding. It explains the spiritual significance of the rooster as a creature that perceives cosmic "seasons" or turning points throughout the day, concluding with a Talmudic story about using these spiritual timings.</summary>
<keywords>Kabbalah, Kidneys, Rooster (Sekwi), Rabbi Joshua ben Levi, Sefirot, Wisdom (Chokhmah), Understanding (Binah), Knowledge (Da'at), Mystical Anatomy, Tractate Berakhot</keywords>

# This is the Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

[A circular kabbalistic diagram occupying the top left quadrant of the page. It consists of several concentric circles divided into radial segments. The center contains a small square (resembling a door or the letter 'dalet'). The radial sectors contain Hebrew words, many being names of the Sefirot (e.g., Greatness, Strength, Beauty, Victory, Splendor, Foundation, Kingship) or divine names and combinations. Around the outermost circle, additional Hebrew words are written in each segment (e.g., Yahweh, Wisdom, Understanding, Crown). There are also small letter combinations like "Yah" and "El" repeated.]

<margin>
Through your knowledge and return...
Thus you shall remember.
The gathering of the assembly and the intention...
Regarding the flowering...
For this is the secret of the intentions...
In the world...
To all the intentions.
"Do you know the laws of the heavens? Can you establish their dominion on the earth?" <note>Job 38:33</note>
For you have no business with anything below that does not have a "prince" <term>Sar; an angelic governor or spiritual force</term> above that strikes it and says to it, "Grow!" For the laws of the heavens <gloss>Beauty</gloss> are all-encompassing.
</margin>

...to him from the hidden intentions <term>Kavannot; the meditative focuses required for prayer or mystical acts</term>. And after they reach the power of **Kindness** <term>Chesed; the Sefirah representing divine love and expansion</term> to **Beauty** <term>Tiferet; the central Sefirah that balances the system</term> by the power of equality, and after—
the waters mentioned (in the hidden place), their dominion and their rule on the earth <gloss>The Crown</gloss> <note>Atarah, literally "Crown," is a common kabbalistic name for Malchut, the final Sefirah that represents the physical manifest world</note>. And from the Crown to each and every world
below. He is found in His day with the word, in the chapter "Yod" <note>The letter Yod, representing the initial point of creation</note>, God, He was, He is, He shall be. Within the head, and He is there for you,
from the secret of the back, for He gazes and watches over all generations. And afterwards, from the head, from the side of Kindness and Goodness.
In the conclusion of the chapter, like the master: "Yod," L-L-D <note>mystical permutations of a divine name</note>, within the name **Almighty** <term>Shaddai</term>. He is the one appointed to say to His world, "Enough!" <note>The Ra'avad refers to the traditional etymology where Shaddai is "She-dai," the One who set limits to the expanding universe</note>. This is according to the path of
the Torah, according to the limit of the time of their standing. And it says: "Do you know the laws of the heavens?" Why? Because if you knew
the commandments regarding this, you would know the decree of the Heavens <gloss>Beauty</gloss> that are worthy for you to establish their dominion in
the revealed world on the earth <gloss>The Crown</gloss>. For it is possible that by knowing the decree of the heavens, you will not compare the dominion of the "revolutions" <term>Gilgulim; the cycles of the celestial spheres or the transmigration of souls</term> 
upon the earth <gloss>The Crown</gloss>. For by the way of their revolution, their decree is strengthened and gathered to be high and low,
and the sunken rises, and thus all of them. "Can you lift up your voice to the clouds?" <gloss>The Crown</gloss> <note>Job 38:34</note>. Why is this written at the beginning?
When you lift up your voice <gloss>The Crown</gloss> to the cloud <note>the source of emanation</note>, the flow of water—and they appear to you from Kindness—
by the request for water for the ways of the Crown. And the explanation of "lifting up": when the connection is lifted <gloss>The Crown</gloss> as it is written
<gloss>Beauty</gloss> "behold, a spirit for him" <gloss>Beauty</gloss>. "Lift up your voice to praise," and the flow of water will turn toward you. 
"Will the lightning bolts go" <note>Job 38:35</note> in the firmament from the side of **Strength** <term>Gevurah; the Sefirah of judgment and restriction</term>, and they will go to the place where they are completed. And they shall say to you: "Here we are," ready to do 
your will. From the side of the heavens, for from here they enter the righteous ones. And the secret of the Three is handed over—
the keys to water, the keys to birth. And it is because of this he had the power to inform you: that if a person does not know the laws of 
heaven and earth, who is appointed from the entities over the water, and who is appointed from the entities over the land, and so on 
regarding all the laws of the heavens, he will not be able to guide the power that is over the land. As it is written: "Do you know the laws of 
heavens? Can you establish their dominion on the earth?" Or: "Can you lift up your voice to the clouds?" This is the abundance of water, meaning a multitude of water.

<summary>This page provides a kabbalistic commentary on Job 38, explaining that the "laws of heaven" are the interactions between the Sefirot (divine attributes). It posits that a mystic who understands these spiritual laws can influence the physical world, specifically the "keys" to rain and life.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the Ra'avad’s mystical introduction, specifically exploring the "Sod Ha-Tinnur" (The Secret of the Oven/Cycle) and how divine justice is mirrored in the natural world through the food chain and the transmigration of souls.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

**Strengthen yourself:** Interpretation of "The World" <gloss>original: Atarah; the Sefirah of Kingship</gloss>. They were created through His thought <gloss>original: Tiferet; the Sefirah of Beauty</gloss>. For just as in the thought of the Writing and the Crown—the General Teacher—the **Greatness** <term>Chesed; Loving-kindness</term> and **Might** <term>Gevurah; Strength</term> are found in potential, in the "Point" <note>likely referring to the Primordial Point or Keter</note> and the **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term>, as they are cleaved there.

Likewise, we find in the chapter of the "Heifer whose neck is broken" <note>Deuteronomy 21, a ritual for an unsolved murder</note>, in the scroll of the *Sotah* <note>the ritual for a woman suspected of adultery</note>, and in the "Salt of the bread of a slain man," that a person's "matter" <note>thought or spiritual essence</note> might be in one place while their body is in another. A person's thought leads the body, like a rope leads the hoof <note>original: Parsas; often used in this text to refer to the boundary of the physical realm or the Sefirah of Kingship</note>, for the primary root of the body is from the "Hoof" <gloss>Atarah</gloss>. Alternatively, the thought leads the "Hoof" to the rest of the body, for its primary root is from the Heart <gloss>Tiferet</gloss>. 

From the "Salt of his choosers" <note>difficult phrase; likely referring to divine selection</note> to test and examine—the translation of "chosen" is "selected"—why did He allow all these elements of "dust" <note>materiality</note> to sustain the "Wise One" <note>the soul</note>? 

Consider this: Just as a mortal king requires "Wise Men of Kindness and Mercy" for the administration of the kingdom's subjects, and also requires "Wise Men of Might," warriors, and merchants for the "Foundation of the Hoof" <note>the infrastructure and defense of the realm</note>, and through this his kingdom is established; **so too in the Upper Order.** The King who guides the world requires the Attribute of **Kindness** and **Mercy** to do good for all those close to Him through the beautiful ways of the Kingdom. Yet He also requires the Attribute of "Merchants" <note>referring to those who manage "exchange" or trade</note> and the Attribute of **Death** and **Punishment** to discipline those who rebel against the King’s decree. 

Just as with a mortal king, some are granted a life of ease and luxury, with their needs provided for from the King's house. Some eat at his table, some from the royal stores, some from the city, some from the village, some from the fields and vineyards—and some are sustained from the "fines" paid by those who transgress the King's law. 

So it is in the place of the **Supreme King** <gloss>Tiferet</gloss>. Therefore, He created human beings and every "Person" <note>likely referring to the 'faces' in Ezekiel's vision</note>: the **Face of the Lion** representing all pure and impure wild beasts; the **Face of the Ox** representing all domestic animals; and the **Face of the Eagle** representing all pure and impure birds. 

Among the birds and beasts, some are sustained by prepared food, and some are sustained by "theft" <note>predation</note>, such as lions, leopards, and wolves. If there were no "fined ones" <note>those being punished</note> among the transgressors of the King's law, His kingdom would not be established. Similarly, if there were no predatory beasts to seize [the transgressors], the Kingdom would not be sustained. 

This is the secret of what our Sages of blessed memory said: *"No wild beast overcomes a man unless he appears to it like a beast"* <note>Babylonian Talmud, Shabbat 151b</note>. This means that when the awe of the Name [God] is removed from him, the "Awe of God" departs from the predators, and he is consequently abandoned to all.

Know that all the Works of Creation follow the "Law of Exchange" <term>Kavvei Halof; a Kabbalistic term for the transmigration of souls or the recycling of energy</term>. Some change through eating and procreation, and some through perfection. Therefore, everything consumed is divided according to this Law of Exchange. This is the **Secret of the Oven** <term>Sod Ha-Tinnur; refers to the "re-firing" or recycling of souls in the afterlife or through reincarnation</term>.

The interpretation of *"He goes about to hunt prey"* <note>cf. Job 38:39</note> is that he [the predator] must pursue it until he brings the prey to the species destined to eat it, according to what is fitting for both the eater and the eaten. "Prey" refers to food, as in *"The young lions roar for their prey"* <note>Psalm 104:21</note>. The food of the lions will be "filled"—meaning it will suffice to provide for them according to the "Law of Exchange" between the eater and the eaten. 

All this is written as truth and justice. Regarding "sustenance," the translation is "scattered" <note>Aramaic: chafzar</note>, for you shall dwell to provide the animal its food and sustenance. *"They crouch in their dens and lie in wait in the thicket"* <note>Job 38:40</note>. They satisfy their high stature in their dwellings—meaning in their dens—where they lie and wait. When they sit in the thicket of the woods and forests, they direct their entire consciousness to destroy their prey. This is translated as: *"They lie in their dwellings to ambush their food."*

For the sake of "shattering the souls," they are enclosed and hidden in these dwellings. This is the "Law of Exchange," where the "Face of Man" is turned to the "Face of Edom" <note>likely referring to a soul falling from a human level to a lower or more animalistic state</note>. Behold, the soul that is now "woven" <note>trapped</note> in the lion’s den longs for the "thicket" <note>the security of its source</note> and for the "house" which it once chose. Its soul is shattered because of its "harlotry" <note>sinful deviations</note>, and therefore it is "caught in the net" to be destroyed. Perhaps there is some benefit in the soul being preyed upon or eaten by animals, for through the act of being eaten, the soul reaches an "elevation." If the eating is done in purity, it achieves a higher level through pure things. If the eating is inferior...

<meta>catchword: will be born (teled)</meta>

<summary>This page explains the divine necessity of predators and punishment as a mirror of a king's justice system. It introduces the "Secret of the Oven" and the "Law of Exchange," suggesting that the food chain is a mechanism for the transmigration and elevation of souls.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the Ra'avad’s mystical introduction. It moves from the internal ethics of the human body and "matter" discussed on the previous page toward the cosmic order, using the "rooster" (Sekhvi) as a primary symbol for the transition between divine wisdom and physical time.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

God, for whom the rooster stands on one leg with its comb upon its head—almost like a kind of white thread—and his strength and time <note>The text likely refers to the "Sekhvi," the rooster/heart mentioned in Job 38:36, which in Jewish tradition distinguishes between night and day.</note>. He receives his portion and looks out upon the world and its companions, cleaving without ceasing to holiness. From the action of the **Voice** <gloss>Tiferet</gloss>, he distinguishes between the darkness of time and the time of peace. It is good for him, and he hastens to occupy himself with "matter," yet he hastens to delay his investigation <note>This suggests a tension between spiritual perception and physical constraints.</note>. For the "portion of evil" is not good, and it is written: *Evil shall not dwell with You* <note>Psalm 5:5</note>. Thus it is explained that he sets his mind to gaze upon the hidden treasuries. He flows toward the "Bed" <gloss>the Shekhinah/Atarah</gloss>, for his bed is hidden within the clay to align the "matter" from the four cycles <term>Tekufot; the four seasons</term> of the year. Therefore, all those who possess a natural form require support from the power of the "Master of the Assembly of the Offering," who was before the Lord. Every "Master" is like a blow to his neighbor, and he hastens for the morrow from their power to derive pleasure from the Torah.

And behold, the time appointed for the "Call of the Rooster" <note>Kriat HaGever; the dawn</note> is drawn from the four cycles of the year. This is what is meant by: "He gives from his glory to those who fear Him." He gives his wisdom to him; He gives to the rooster the strength of the dove <note>A mystical comparison between the rooster's alert nature and the dove's loyalty</note> to hasten toward the morning.

"Who can number the clouds by wisdom?" <note>Job 38:37</note>. Who can record? For when the entire **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term> is clarified for the **Crown** <term>Atarah; the 10th Sefirah</term>, He gives wisdom to the flow that provides "Splendor" <gloss>Hod</gloss> to her, for **Understanding** <term>Binah</term> and the Heat. For he who is Understanding and occupies himself with the surrounding **Sefirot** <term>Divine emanations</term>—they allude to **Endurance** <term>Netzach</term> and **Splendor** <term>Hod</term>. The wise ones say: **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah</term>, the Supernal, is in the Crown. This means that the Name gives them their clarity, and the "Generalities of Heaven" <note>likely a reading of 'Nivlei Shamayim' or 'Bottles of Heaven' from Job 38:37</note> represent the exchanges of the heavens—who shall write and arrange their place? 

This means: who can count and number the lightness of the stars of the heavens? For the heavens are "laws" <note>Original: *Gezarot*</note> corresponding to the **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term>. That which is vacant of them in his world comes from the movers. The "Generalities of Heaven" refers to the matters of the heavens which are nullified before their lights; who shall arrange them? Behold, they shall gush forth, and behold, they shall turn, and they shall pour out their bread in perfection. Therefore, he sings to them and says: He gave the timings of the stars of heaven by His wisdom. The matters of all the seasonal cycles are likened to "precise measurements" given to the wise. For we already know that all existing things are found through the "Eye of the Eye" <note>The Primal Source</note>. Know that Wisdom belongs to the wise, for He taught all existing things and improved them toward the Supernal Wisdom, as it is written: *In Wisdom.*

And behold, the "Place-Makers"—which are the firmaments—who is the "Voice" <gloss>Tiferet</gloss> that can give them a number? He who understands shall answer you under the Supernal Wisdom. "And the Generalities of Heaven—who can record them?" The things joined to the heavens—who shall arrange them? Therefore, he sings and says: He gave the timings of the celestial bodies by His wisdom, and their power is adjacent. 

The interpretation: When the heavens reign, they live and become wise in their place, alluding to the "Appointed Ones." And "The Generalities of Heaven—who can record them?" It is known that everything that emerges from the **First Cause** <note>the Divine Source</note> only lingers and is hidden due to the distance. This is the "Line" <gloss>original: Kav</gloss> in the world of the Sea. Therefore, it was at the ultimate thickness according to what was drawn from them. According to its "Taste" and the "Beauty" <gloss>Tiferet</gloss> of its surroundings, it is drawn from the source of the **Investigation** <gloss>Binah</gloss>. The Beauty <gloss>Tiferet</gloss> merited to gaze upon the power of the Supernal Machine, of blessed memory—which is Binah— and the "Line" was, as it were, uplifted. Therefore, the "Line" was "unformed and void" <note>Tohu va-Vohu; Genesis 1:2</note> and was born and sought the good of its primordial state, looking back to its birth.

Therefore, "And the Generalities of Heaven—who can record them?" The interpretation is: "Who can quiet them?" <note>as in 'to be still'</note>. It is like the cooling of the "Wings of the Dust" <note>original: Kanfei Afar</note> for rest. The interpretation: When it is complete in the mind of the **Crown** <term>Atarah</term>—which is called "Dust"—to rest, it is like water as it was before the Creation, being close to **Understanding** <term>Binah</term>. For the *Spirit of God* <gloss>Binah</gloss> *hovered over the face of the waters.* It entered and was pressed until the Holy One, blessed be He, stirred and attached the indwelling. Just as one might say: *He established the Earth* <gloss>Atarah</gloss> *upon its foundations* <gloss>Binah and the Voice</gloss>, *that it should not collapse forever* <note>Psalm 104:5</note>. This implies that it was in its mind to collapse until He attached the indwelling. This was the "Request of the Dust to Rest" when the Dust was appointed to be in the hand of the works of Heaven, to be gathered like the "Form of the Transition" <note>difficult mystical phrase</note>... and it was entered and pressed...

<summary>This page explores the mystical relationship between the Rooster (symbolizing the transition to wisdom), the Sefirot (particularly Binah and Atarah), and the act of creation. It interprets verses from Job to explain how the celestial order is "recorded" and how the physical "Dust" (the lowest Sefirah) seeks to return to its divine rest.</summary>
