<meta>Catchword: "And as found" (וכנמצא)</meta>

<summary>This page details the mystical attributes of the 8th through 17th "Paths of Wisdom," describing them as spiritual "Intelligences" that govern divine emanations, angelic structures like the Cherubim, and the path to the Garden of Eden. The text emphasizes the transition from abstract divine thought into the structured reality of the spiritual "Veil" and the celestial Chariot.</summary>
<keywords>32 Paths of Wisdom, Sefirot, Binah, Tiferet, Pargod, Merkavah, Cherubim, Chashmal, Araphel, Garden of Eden, Kabbalah</keywords>

--- Page 41 ---
<meta>This page continues the Ra’avad’s commentary on the 32 Paths of Wisdom, though the internal numbering in this manuscript remains non-linear, jumping between the 32nd, 29th, 4th, 8th, 5th, 6th, and 7th paths. This reflects a thematic rather than numerical arrangement in this specific section of the manuscript.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

It is known that one may bring down the power of these **Thirty-Two Paths** <note>original: "לב נתיבות" (Lamed-Bet Netivot)</note> to become wise and to understand the hidden secrets of the strength of **Amalek** <note>In Kabbalah, Amalek often represents the "shadow" or the forces of doubt and ego that oppose holiness</note>. This writing was entirely pure with the writing of the **Aleph** <note>The first letter of the Hebrew alphabet, representing Divine Unity</note>. Therefore, they said in *Bereshit Rabbah* that the letter **Lamed** is referred to as a "purified oak" <note>original: "אלון מזוכה" (Alon Mezukkah); a play on words between the name of the letter Lamed and trees mentioned in the Midrash</note>. Look there: "Oak in the secret," "Oak of Palanzar." This writing is called **Lamed**, the Prince of Righteousness.

The **29th Path** <note>original: "הזמן התשיעי (כט)" — likely referring to the 29th path in a specific sequence</note> (see *Bereshit Rabbah*) is called the **Foundation of the Vessel** and the establishment of the Holy One, Blessed be He, of **Binah** <term>Binah: The Sefirah of "Understanding" or "Constructive Intelligence"</term>, as it is said: "The Lord by **Wisdom** founded the earth; by **Understanding** <note>original: "בתבונה" (bi-tevunah)</note> He established the heavens" (Proverbs 3:19). This means that **Beginning** <note>Bereshit</note> refers to Wisdom, and "God created" refers to Understanding. This writing is called the **First** because from it the firstness is included and dwells there.

The **4th Path** is called the **Fixed Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל קבוע" (Sekhel Kavua)</note>. It is called so because from it all powers are emanated, for all powers are fixed within it and "planted like nails" <note>A reference to Ecclesiastes 12:11</note>. Therefore, all spiritual powers extend from it like the thinness of the air, in the manner of a building's design within fine garments. Thus, all the forms of the "Building" <note>The structure of the Sefirot</note> which are within it are all weakened versions of the powers dwelling in the garments of every kind, from it and not from another. Hear this: the form of the "Lodging" <note>original: "מלון" (Malun) — likely a metaphor for the physical world as a temporary dwelling</note> which is within the garments is what brings Wisdom into actualized residence.

However, there is no other pioneer in that which is born, just as a bird depicted on a wall has no power to lodge within the drawing of the bird. Therefore they said that these are emanated—meaning they are a "species" from it in the drawing of **Abkah** <note>Numerical value (Gematria) equivalent to "powder" or "dust," often used to describe fine spiritual essence</note>. For just as the river carries the crossing to the Creator, so is the "Crown" <note>original: "נזר" (Nezar)</note> from the lap of all. And behold, the "Writing" was in the power of the **Will**, which is the first writing; it is the chamber of all types of vitalities.

The **8th Path** is called the **Mediated Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל נסרס" (Sekhel Nesaras) — often meaning an intelligence that is arranged or "castrated" of its infinite potential to become finite</note>. It is called so because it is the cause for its specific verse, for through it all the emanated beings from it and below are formed. There is a difference between this writing and the **3rd Path**, which is the **Splendor of the Equal Progeny** <note>original: "וזהר התולדות השוה" (Ve-Zohar ha-Toldot ha-Shaveh)</note>, because that is the standing-up of the ruling powers in an equal unity, until there is no difference for him between left and right, for He is He, and they incline to His power to plant in His garden that dwells above.

But the **5th Path** consists of things spreading out from it to the right, left, front, back, to **Judgment** <note>Din</note>, and to **Mercy** <note>Rachamim</note>. All of them are rooted from one simple rock, which is the **2nd Path**. They are of this nature: the metal that is made, which joins and divides branches into several simple laws. In it are the chosen "dovecotes," for when various powers are heard within it, they are proven; through it, they have ends for the harvest of darkness or the desired light.

The **5th Path** is for all expansion above the "points" <note>term: Nikudot (Vowel points, representing a stage of emanation below the letters)</note>, and certainly above the "Foundation Letters." However, in one power of the sun <unclear>mi-bah</unclear>, and that power in life was a simple power without any foundation, without multiplicity or change. So should one understand the **5th Path**: that within it are these laws from which change, substitution, and transformation spread. Take this as a comparison for the mind: the difference between the **5th Path** and the **3rd Path** is that the **3rd Path** is like the **Soul** <note>Nefesh</note>, while the **5th Path** is its generations according to a simple unity.

The **6th Path** is called the **All-Inclusive Flow** <note>original: "שפע נכלל" (Shefa Nichlal)</note>. It is known that "Flow" <note>Shefa</note> is a language of dripping and abundance, as in "an abundance of water shall bless you." Behold, the **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> and the **Preparation** which flow from the **7th Path** are literally in the proportion of the sun's light; it is a flow that can descend to the many. Furthermore, the sun's light flows upon all existing things. From the **6th Path**, an all-inclusive flow emanates; the difference between the **6th Path** and the "Pool" or the "Unified Source" is in the proportion of the 6th Path itself.

The **7th Path** is called the **Hidden Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל נסתר" (Sekhel Nistar)</note> because it is hidden from everything besides it. Therefore, it abides only in its own balanced intelligence. This is the understanding between the **7th Path** and the **Occult Intelligence** <note>original: "כתיב מופלא" (Kativ Mufla)</note>, for the Occult one is a gift of the mind. This **6th Path** <note>returning to the previous subject</note> dwells for the wise who are in the **Sefirot**, and also for the thoughts and reflections of the common people.
<meta>catchword: Called (הנקרא)</meta>

<summary>This page analyzes several of the 32 Paths of Wisdom, focusing on the Fixed, Mediated, All-Inclusive, and Hidden Intelligences. It explores how Divine power flows from unity into multiplicity, specifically through the concepts of Wisdom (Chokhmah) and Understanding (Binah).</summary>

<keywords>32 Paths of Wisdom, Binah, Fixed Intelligence, Hidden Intelligence, All-Inclusive Flow, Sefirot, Ra'avad, Kabbalah, Amalek, Medieval Philosophy</keywords>

--- Page 42 ---
<meta>This page continues the Ra’avad’s commentary on the Thirty-Two Paths of Wisdom, specifically detailing the qualities and functions of Paths 8 through 17. It maintains the philosophical and mystical exploration of how Divine light descends through these "intelligences" (Sekhalim) to structure the universe and the human soul.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

->Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory<-

**The 8th Path:** The 8th Path is called the **Learned and Comprehensive Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל מלומד וכלול" (Sekhel Melummad ve-Khalul)</note>. It is named according to your intelligence, for when it reaches its end, the paths are completed and comprehensive. It is the bridge to standing upon the ultimate purpose of this path, without exception, and one must be careful. Therefore, it is called "Intelligence." Do not be mistaken in this matter, for this doubt exists among all the wise who are able to grasp it. He who gazes upon this will find himself at its "window" <note>original: "בחלונו"; perhaps referring to a point of observation or a spiritual aperture</note> to the **Crown** <note>original: "עט'" (Atarah)</note>; he is alive to stand upon it. It is missing from the general category of "fishes" <note>The Hebrew "דגים" (fish) here might be a metaphor for souls or a specific astrological reference</note> but is part of the category of **Wisdom** <term>Wisdom: Chokhmah, the first flash of divine inspiration</term>, for it is alive and moving, yet it does not change its place. It is fixed and driven into its portion. All the power to understand the depths of this path is blessed through inquiry, and the **Greatness** <note>original: "הגדולה" (Ha-Gedulah), often synonymous with the Sefirah of Chesed</note> that is comprised within it is magnificent.

**The 9th Path:** The 9th Path is called the **Pure Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל טהור" (Sekhel Tahor)</note>. It is called so because it purifies and cleanses the decree of the "Examinations" and the **Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: The ten creative powers or attributes of God in Kabbalah</term> from the side of **Kindness** <term>Kindness: Chesed</term> and the highest elevations. All good stands in contrast to the "Permutations" <note>original: "תמורות" (Temurot); the forces of exchange or imbalance</note>. This path guides, cleanses, purifies, and upholds the decree of their purpose, removing the permutations from them. Because it understands the **Unity**, it acts to unify without any separation or division.

**The 10th Path:** The 10th Path is called the **Scintillating Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל מתנוצץ" (Sekhel Mitnotzetz)</note>. It is named so because the "Whole" <note>The totality of the emanations</note> ascends and sits upon the **Throne of Understanding** <term>Understanding: Binah, the Sefirah of structure and comprehension</term> through a supreme power. It sends its light to the **Flame** <note>original: "להלהב"</note> and the **Crown** and all the luminaries. It pours out a flow of blessings to the **Prince of the Face** <note>Metatron, the highest angel in Jewish mysticism</note>. It is called "Scintillating" because it receives the sparks of splendor from **Understanding** (*Binah*).

**The 11th Path:** The 11th Path is called the **Resplendent Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל מצוחצח" (Sekhel Metzuchtzach)</note>. It is called so because it is the "Giant of the Veil" <note>original: "ענק הפרגוד" (Anak Ha-Pargod)</note> and the secret of the **Curtain** <note>original: "הפרוכת" (Ha-Parochet)</note> which arranges the order of the array of the upper paths and all the paths below. It is the strength of the paths to stand before the **Cause of Causes** <term>Cause of Causes: The Ultimate Divine Source</term>, as it is said: "And the Greatness is written for you between the Holy and the **Holy of Holies**." It enters at all times into the Holy place within the **Veil** <note>original: "הפרגוד" (Ha-Pargod); the cosmic curtain separating the Divine from the lower realms</note>. Corresponding to this Veil is the **diaphragm** <note>original: "חגר הכבד"; literally 'the belt of the liver'</note> in a living being, which separates the vessels of the **Soul** <note>original: "נשמה" (Neshamah)</note> from the vessels of the brain.

**The 12th Path:** The 12th Path is called the **Clear Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל בהיר" (Sekhel Bahir)</note>. It is called so because it unites all existences to **Kindness** (*Chesed*), as well as the offspring of the **Chariot** <term>Chariot: Merkavah, the realm of Divine palaces and angelic beings</term> and the connected paths. It is called the "Stronghold of Greatness" because in its division are included upper and lower beings and all the "tracks." This is the secret of "they shall take in their mouths and their bosoms" <note>A poetic reference to internalizing spiritual light</note>. It is called **Gatherings** <note>original: "מקוות" (Mikvaot)</note> after the phrase "gathering of the waters."

**The 13th Path:** The 13th Path is called the **Guiding Intelligence in Unity** <note>original: "שכל מנהיג באחדות"</note>. This is the essence of the **Glory** <note>original: "הכבוד" (Ha-Kavod)</note> that receives from all the paths. Within it is the completion and end of the spiritual, pleasant, and unique "Gifts" in the supreme Unity. From its gift, one may say that all sweetness is made sweet through this path. Therefore, it is called the "Kindness of the House of **Ya[h]**" <note>original: "כגית ינ" - likely a scribal shorthand for "House of the Lord" or a numerical reference</note>.

**The 14th Path:** The 14th Path is called the **Intelligence of the Luminary** <note>original: "שכל המאור"</note>. It is named so because it teaches the secret and hidden things of the Supreme to all who are separated from it, and it enlightens their eyes in the secrets of the Law. This is the essence of the **Chashmal** <term>Chashmal: The "speaking silence" or "electrum" seen in Ezekiel's vision</term>. It holds all the hidden things of the heights. It reveals the secret of the Holy and their form, to learn the Holy which is for the "witness" and the form of the Holy.

**The 15th Path:** The 15th Path is called the **Establishing Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל מעמיד" (Sekhel Ma'amid)</note>. It is named so because it establishes the essence of the **Cherubim** <note>Angelic beings supporting the Divine throne</note> in the **Thick Clouds of Purity** <note>original: "ערפלי טוהר" (Arpilei Tohar)</note>. According to the scripture: "He brings the thick cloud near" and establishes it upon a proper base to receive the engravings of the upper paths by all necessity. The strengthening of the cloud occurs through this verse; it was in the darkness of the thick cloud to receive the necessary image. Concerning it, it is said: "Whose garment was a cloud, and thick darkness its swaddling band" (Job 38:9).

**The 16th Path:** The 16th Path is called the **Guarded Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל נשמר" (Sekhel Nishmar)</note>. It is prepared for anyone who purifies his inclination and his transformation. It is the secret of "delight upon the **Glory**," for in this path the **Crown** (*Atarah*) receives pleasure and delight through the portions of the Lord. It is called the **Garden of Eden**, which is prepared for the righteous.

**The 17th Path:** The 17th Path is called the **Intelligence of Feeling** <note>original: "שכל ההרגש" (Sekhel Ha-Hergesh)</note>. Know that at the time of prayer, all the lights are hidden in the Name above, and they have a house and understanding <meta>catchword: "ובינה" (and understanding)</meta>...

<summary>This page describes Paths 8 through 17 of the Thirty-Two Paths of Wisdom, detailing how each "Intelligence" functions to purify, guide, and establish the Divine flow from the higher Sefirot down to the lower realms, including the Garden of Eden and the human soul.</summary>
<keywords>32 Paths of Wisdom, Ra'avad, Intelligence, Binah, Chesed, Pargod, Cherubim, Garden of Eden, Chashmal, Sefirot, Kabbalah</keywords>

<meta>This page appears to return to the beginning of the list of the "Thirty-Two Paths of Wisdom," specifically detailing Paths 4 through 7. While the previous page discussed Paths 8 through 17, this section provides the foundational commentary on the earlier paths and an introductory passage regarding the nature of faith and the struggle against spiritual opposition.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory

->Introduction of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory<-

It is worthy to mention the power of words toward faith <note>original: "עט'" (Atarah/Crown). The author links the power of faith to the highest Sefirah, the Crown</note> to become wise and to understand the secret of the Prince of the World, **Amalek** <note>In mystical literature, Amalek represents the spiritual force of doubt and the primary opponent of faith</note>. This scripture was a drawing force along with the text of the verses, and therefore it was said in the Midrash *Genesis Rabbah*: "Why is the craftsman <note>original: "אמון" (Amon); based on Proverbs 8:30, referring to the Torah or Wisdom as God's architectural tool</note> covered and hidden?" See there regarding "the hidden craftsman" and "the craftsman of greatness." This scripture is called **Levi** <note>The name Levi implies "attachment" or "joining"</note>, as in the verse: "As a nursing father <note>original: "אומן" (Oman), a wordplay on "Amon" (craftsman)</note> carries a suckling child" <note>Numbers 11:12</note>. See *Genesis Rabbah* on this. This scripture is also called **Grace** <note>original: "חון" (Chon)</note> for it is the tool of the craftsmanship of the Holy One, blessed be He. As it says: "With **Wisdom** He founded the earth" <note>Proverbs 3:19</note> and "He established the heavens" refers to **Understanding** <term>Understanding: Binah, the analytical power of the Divine mind</term>. It also says: "**In the beginning** <note>interpreted here as Chokhmah/Wisdom</note> **God created** <note>interpreted here as Binah/Understanding</note>." This scripture is called the **Father of Faith** <note>original: "אב האמונה"</note> because from it comes complete faith, and it dwells therein.

**The 4th Path:** The 4th Path is called the **Fixed Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל קבוע" (Sekhel Kavu'a)</note>. It is called so because from it all powers emanate, for all powers are themselves fixed and "like well-fastened nails" <note>Ecclesiastes 12:11</note>. Therefore, all spiritual powers emanated from it in their divine subtlety, just as the form of a building exists within the tools in the subtlety of its conceptualization. Consequently, all the forms of the building that are in the physical structure are divided from the powers in the conceptualization within the tool—each according to its kind and not from another. The matter is this: the form of a window in the tool’s conceptualization is the cause for the actual window in the building. Because the power of "no window" in the tool's design surrounds the bird depicted on the wall of the house, the conceptualization of the window does not exist within the conceptualization of the bird. Thus they said that these differ from one another, meaning each is of its own kind. In the conceptualization of "Day," its numerical value <note>Gematria</note> is three, for the **Soul of Absence** <note>original: "נפש העדר" (Nefesh Ha-Heder). "Absence" or "Privation" is a philosophical term for the state before a form is realized</note> is the absence of the preceding form; thus, "absence" is a power for **Being** <note>original: "הויה" (Havayah)</note>. And behold, this scripture possesses the ancient power of the first scripture, and it emanates every kind of light.

**The 5th Path:** The 5th Path is called the **Rooted Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל נשרש" (Sekhel Nishrash)</note>. It is called so because it is the cause for the "End of the Primordial," for in it all things that emanate from it downwards become equal. There is a difference between this scripture and the second scripture, which is the "One of that Unity," for that is the unfolding of the Sefirot found in that Unity to the point where there is no difference between its left and its right—it is prepared and does good even to the wicked, as explained above. But this scripture—the things that spread from it to the right and left, to merit and guilt, to judgment and mercy—all of them are rooted in a single, simple root. Contemplate this by the example of a simple metal that receives the sanctification of the water through a power of simple unity within it. Understand from here that in the **Soul** <term>Soul: Nefesh, the lowest level of the soul, associated with vitality</term> there are dwelling powers: a power that merits and a power that melts... <unclear>לו טיסיס</unclear> in the soul dwells the <unclear>התבכס</unclear> then the Divine goes out and provides for every shoulder above the spheres, and certainly it is above the "quality" of the elements. For by the power of the single "cubit" of water within it, its stay in the soul is a simple power without any element, without quality or the secret of change. Thus one should understand regarding this scripture, that in it is the "confession of the end" from which the "children of God" spread, as well as substitution and change. You may take as an illustration the difference between this scripture and the second: the second scripture is like the **Soul** <note>Nefesh</note> and this scripture is like the **Spirit** <note>Ruach</note> in the explanation of that Unity.

**The 6th Path:** The 6th Path is called the **Universal Influence** <note>original: "שפע כללי" (Shefa Klali)</note>. It is known that "Influence" <note>Shefa</note> is a term for flowing and abundance. The second word "Influence" <unclear>תנכך</unclear> means an abundance like the flowing of water. Behold, the scripture and the abundance which flows from the 7th Path is in the category of "sweetening the darkness" and "influence," and it can pour into the "Garment." Furthermore, it is opened from the vowel **Kometz** <note>A Hebrew vowel ( ָ ) representing gathering or contraction</note> which points to all existing things. So, from the 6th Path, influence flows into everything, separating from the 6th Path until the "Blessings" and "Sources" which branch out from the 6th Path.

**The 7th Path:** The 7th Path is the **Hidden Intelligence** <note>original: "שכל נסתר" (Sekhel Nistar)</note> because it is hidden from all that is outside of it, and it cannot be grasped except by the "intellectuals of the Sefirot." This is the difference between the 7th Path and the **Wondrous Intelligence** <note>The 1st Path</note>; for the "Wondrous" is among the objects of the intellect <unclear>מפספסו</unclear>, but the 7th Path is grasped by the wise who are in [a state of] simplicity, and also by the thought and reflection of the moment.

<summary>This page details the 4th through 7th Paths of Wisdom, using metaphors of architecture, metalwork, and flowing water to explain how Divine influence (Shefa) and specific "intelligences" (Sekhalim) structure the spiritual and physical worlds. It emphasizes the "Fixed" nature of spiritual powers and the "Rooted" origin of judgment and mercy.</summary>
<keywords>Fixed Intelligence, Rooted Intelligence, Universal Influence, Hidden Intelligence, Amalek, Sefirot, Conceptualization, Soul, Spirit, Shefa, Architecture metaphor</keywords>

<meta>The previous page concluded a brief mystical analysis of the early "Paths of Wisdom" and the nature of the craftsman (Amon). This page transitions into the formal introduction by Moses Botarel to his famous commentary on the Sefer Yetzirah (The Book of Formation), shifting from the Ra'avad's perspective to Botarel’s philosophical and pedagogical approach to the text.</meta>

# Introduction of Rabbi Moses Botarel, of blessed memory

->Introduction of Rabbi Moses Botarel, of blessed memory<-

**Said** the one who strives and is aforementioned, to fulfill the desire of the aforementioned wise and pious man, may his memory be for a blessing in the life of the world to come. Every person of understanding and wisdom will grasp the superiority of wisdom and the value of perfection, which characterizes God, may He be blessed, in all heights. It is the cause for the human race to be unified with the world at all times, and to delight in the pleasure of the soul forever as a reward for its actions along the path of intellectual concepts. 

And in what shall a person trust in his exile? For he weakens his powers <note>OCR reads "kchatulotaihu" (like cats), but the context suggests "kohotaihu" (his powers)</note> with thoughts of living for His glory, may He be blessed. And if he is a human, his thoughts should not be merely for his physical mechanisms, but a ladder above all lowly levels <note>original: "z'l" (zulat) - meaning "other than" or "besides"</note> by the power of unique virtues. And if a mortal flies like a dove to every place and at every time, the secret of creation will be good for him, for it is a pearl within a shell, existing and faithful. 

And it is even greater that he should trouble himself with the ultimate praise in the birth of the sciences. For there he will produce pearls in the practical wisdoms, the moral virtues, and the intellectual virtues—to search the "lute of his body" for the glory of a wise man. And therefore, as we have seen <note>OCR reads "k'halomeinu," likely "k'mo shera'inu"</note> in the grace of the kings of kindness, it is more correct and more beneficial for a man. And from the clarity of his mind regarding the scales of the intellect and the preciousness of what is captured, he will know how to examine the pearl from the many facets of its face. 

All the things that were written and spoken here, which circulate throughout his treasures, are simple to publicize and go out into the world, as a permanent bond under the providence of the Eternal God <note>original: "El Olam"</note>. For he is a righteous and perfect person, and a pious man <note>OCR reads "chazir" (pig), clearly a typo for "chasid" (pious)</note> whose fruit is among the famous sages. According to the flow of the lips, he investigated until the influence of the ultimate purpose and the fruitful sun. Included in this are all the powers of the sciences, especially focused on the practical application: the good secret and the foundation of taste.

And I saw further that because of the variation of the parts, and due to his great diligence regarding the precision for strengthening the decrees for Divine matters, I said in my heart that it is time to fulfill our desire. For he is the one who was chosen <note>unclear reading in OCR</note> to make a commentary on the *Sefer Yetzirah* <term>Sefer Yetzirah: The Book of Formation, the oldest known Hebrew text on cosmology and linguistics</term>. And because its tablets are a great cessation in your Divine eyes, I did not consider it as vain words, and I did not refuse to take the discipline. For it is not proper to do so to this language. To everyone, the preparation was from me for its creation, to take the words according to the established order. For such is the way of those precious individuals of unique virtue who are counted among the established ones—not to play with our values, but in their greatness, this will open our eyes with the understanding of ethics. 

For whoever does not know wisdom knows only in his imagination, like one who does not know how to drink the "passing waters" <note>a metaphor for deep or spiritual knowledge</note>. Therefore, he should not force himself to swallow without the water of unique virtues in his study, for he will not be able to endure in a lasting state. And further, regarding the gaze of his eye, I said of him: "The secret of the Lord is for those who fear Him" <note>Psalm 25:14</note>. And upon this I intended, and my deeds were desirable, so that they might be extracted from my thought and integrated. 

And I decided—though I did not swear—to explain to him a verse or a matter from the words of our Sages of blessed memory that contains an argument regarding the nature of this wisdom. And I have recounted that this book includes the "Work of Creation" <note>original: Ma'aseh Bereshit</note>, meaning natural science, and the "Work of the Chariot" <note>original: Ma'aseh Merkavah</note>, meaning Divine science, which is the hidden wisdom <term>Divine Science: Metaphysics or the study of God's essence and the celestial realms</term>. 

This is not like the work of a commoner, but like the work of a wise man and a matter from his knowledge. In the gathering of this variation... the Sages of blessed memory said: "Rabbi Yochanan ben Zakkai <note>OCR reads "R. Chasan," a likely error</note> said to Rabbi Eleazar: 'Come, and I will teach you the Work of the Chariot.' He said to him: 'I am not yet old enough.' When he grew old... he had the wisdom of the Kabbalah and did not withhold himself." And furthermore, regarding this wisdom, it is said and the mouth is able to speak, as clarified by Rabbi Abraham bar Isaac of Narbonne <note>referred to as the Ra'avad II</note> in the *Sefer HaBerit* (Book of the Covenant). 

And these are his words in Chapter 13 on the nature of the sciences: "And there shall be faith of your times, a store of salvations, wisdom, and knowledge; the fear of the Lord is his treasure" <note>Isaiah 33:6</note>. 
*   "Faith" is a hint to the tithing <note>Ma'aser</note>.
*   "Your times" is a hint to the sciences <note>Chokhmot</note>.
*   "Store" <note>Hosen</note> is a hint to the writings <note>the Mishnah or recorded traditions</note>.
*   "Salvations" is a hint to the order of the structure... and "take it from me" as a son of old age, take the matter and let the hand of the Lord be upon him.
*   "Wisdom" is a hint to the virtues <note>Middot</note>.
*   "Knowledge" is a hint to nature <note>Teva</note>.
*   "The fear of the Lord is his treasure" is a hint to the wisdom of the Kabbalah <term>Kabbalah: The received mystical tradition of Judaism</term>. 

As our Sages of blessed memory said: "One does not hand over the main points <note>rashei perakim</note> except to a person who is concerned and whose heart is anxious within him." This refers to a state of being touched and moved, like "and he saw and was afraid." And regarding this wisdom it is said: "For the devious person is an abomination to the Lord, but His secret is with the upright" <note>Proverbs 3:32</note>. For a person must be in the ultimate state of uprightness and perfection when learning this Kabbalah, as the Guide wrote in Part 1, Chapter 34. And the work...

<meta>catchword: Bereshit (In the beginning)</meta>

<summary>Moses Botarel introduces his commentary on Sefer Yetzirah, defining it as a synthesis of natural science (Ma'aseh Bereshit) and metaphysics (Ma'aseh Merkavah). He emphasizes the moral and intellectual prerequisites for studying Kabbalah, citing Maimonides and Isaiah 33:6 as prooftexts for the hierarchy of knowledge.</summary>

<meta>The previous page introduced Moses Botarel’s preface. However, this page begins with a heading attributing the following section to "the Ra'avad" (Rabbi Abraham ben David of Posquières), a 12th-century scholar. This specific text describes the 32 "Paths of Wisdom" (Paths 18 through 32), which are foundational concepts in the Sefer Yetzirah (Book of Formation), a primary text of early Jewish mysticism.</meta>

# Introduction of the Ra'avad, of blessed memory

...and a throne. Therefore, all the souls that ascend above it towards its elevation are like a covering, to indicate the freedom within the associated freedom <note>original: "deror b'deror" — likely referring to a state of spiritual liberation or the Jubilee</note> (levels); the sources of mercy (crowns) accompanied them, crouching together like one of the masters of their ministry who are in **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: The Sefirah or divine attribute associated with Beauty, Balance, and the Holy Spirit</term> through the Holy Spirit which is there in the "Secret of the Kingdom" <note>Sod HaMalkhut</note>. He is within the fire and their strength is this:

**The 18th Path** is called the **Intelligence of the House of Abundance**. It is called this because from its source, individuals can draw from it. Likewise, those who are trained in its revelation through the investigation of its Creator can draw from it. All those who cling to it can draw a unique influence from it. The difference between this path and the 10th Path is that the 10th Path is called "Separated Abundance" because from it, the separated abundance flows to all sources to give life and provide abundance to all; but this path [the 18th] provides abundance for the matter of study, wisdom, and the wisdom of one's actions. Thus, it is a source of sustenance.

**The 19th Path** is called the **Intelligence of the Secret of Activities**. It is called this because through it, the paths of the abundance that descends from the "Upper Blessing" <note>likely referring to the Sefirah of Binah or the source of divine flow</note> ascend to the Master of all the **Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: The ten creative powers or attributes of God in Kabbalah</term>. Through this path, the abundance is distributed to all the depths of the divine spheres.

**The 20th Path** is called the **Intelligence of Will**. It is called this because it is the path that prepares the disposition of all creatures within **Wisdom** <note>Chokhmah</note>. Through this intelligence, all the obligations of the "Primordial Wisdom," the Upper One, are known. This is the general rule: for it is written "The hand of the Lord was upon me," for with this, He makes known to them the secrets of wisdom.

**The 21st Path** is called the **Intelligence of the Desired Object**. It is called this because through this path, all created beings receive abundance from the Recipient above, in order to bestow it upon everyone.

**The 22nd Path** is called the **Faithful Intelligence**. Through it, spiritual "vessels" are deposited and they grow through it so as to be close to the "Processions" <note>the movement of divine forces</note> in every part. Through it, the soul ascends above its own elevation.

**The 23rd Path** is called the **Sustaining Intelligence**. It is called this because through this path, the power of existence is given to all the **Sefirot**, to establish them from the spirit of His holiness. Because this path sustains all the spheres, it is called the Sustaining Intelligence.

**The 24th Path** is called the **Imaginative Intelligence**. It is called this because it gives likeness and form to all "imaginations" <note>mental or spiritual forms</note> that are created in His spirit, each according to their perception in judgment—such as the various types of imaginations, the types of animal life, and the types of plant life. To each one, it gives a specific character, and to every heavenly intelligence and all existing things, it gives them their specific traits through His spirit.

**The 25th Path** is called the **Testing Power**, for in it is the Wisdom of God which tests all existing things.

**The 26th Path** is called the **Renewing Intelligence**, because it is the intelligence that renews the forms for all the established things that are renewed in His spirit. It shall be from the praise of God which endures forever.

**The 27th Path** is called the **Sensory Intelligence**. Within it is the substance of the intelligence of all created things beneath the "Highest Sphere" and their sustenance.

**The 28th Path** is called the **Natural Intelligence**, because through it the nature of every existing thing beneath the "Encompassing Sphere" is completed in perfection, which is its abundance. It is called "The House of Nature."

**The 29th Path** is called the **Fountain Intelligence**, because every soul beneath all the spheres and species receives its "voice" <note>its vital force or resonance</note> for resurrection from beneath the spheres and within their borders.

**The 30th Path** is called the **Universal Intelligence**, for it includes the laws of all the spheres, the species, the forms, and their judgments.

**The 31st Path** is called the **Constant Intelligence**, for it is that which draws out the watches and the cycles of the stars, and orders each one within its sphere, and gives to all those that are joined to the arrangement of the Zodiac signs and forms.

**The 32nd Path** is called the **Served Intelligence**, for it serves to rotate the stars and the constellations forever.

## Introduction of Rabbi Moses Botarel, of blessed memory

Know, every reader, that everything has its reason beside it, and one should cling to doing His will. In this fullness, it arose and existed. I have paved within me the language of the philosopher: to explain this wondrous statement, there is no end. And because I have paved for myself the way of change and walked in my own path, I did not wish to reveal the secrets <note>original: "nistarim" — hidden mystical truths</note> as long as the second assembly is tied in bonds. 

Even if all the wealth in the world were allocated to me, I would not desire to reveal them. I intended well to build, to do the will of Rabbi Benjamin and Rabbi Jacob of Paris <note>Rabbi Jacob of Marvège (13th century), a French Kabbalist famous for "Responsa from Heaven," whom Botarel claims as a teacher</note>, whose goodness and traditions <term>Kabbalot: Received mystical traditions, often passed orally from teacher to student</term> continue. His voice and word, their stature and protection, are the power of their existence: to reveal a secret matter to one who is wise and discerning. 

Despite all this, I have a goal to elevate the receivers. They offered about a thousand thousand gold dinars, but I did not accept them from the whole world. I heard His will over me: to do kindness from the cup of my masters. By this merit, their service ran the celestial spheres in their chain like the essence of their foundation. If so, what benefit is it to me to lose the World to Come <term>Olam Ha-Ba: The afterlife or the spiritual realm of reward</term> for the sake of the passing world? Therefore, my heart was not filled to reveal what is forbidden. I did not transgress His commandments, nor did I forget, nor did I shorten; nor was I a transgressor by adding.

And this writing is his tradition, blossoming in its strength, and the verse: "It is a tree of life to those who hold fast to it." They paved their paths. The heart of the great Rabbi, the right-hand pillar, my master and teacher, Rabbi Jacob of Paris, of blessed memory, placed oaths upon me not to reveal the hidden secret within his heart, not even for a worldly gift. I did not call for charity; I hid it from all his ways and edges. It was never lacking. You paved with the harp and lyre, and the physical vessel for the horse and chariot to the life of the World to Come... to produce with the white coverings, with taste and wisdom and goodness, to produce the powers of the traditions. And for those before the boats of my work, not to pass to him the keys to the door of wisdom; not to mention in His essence... a forbidden sacrifice.

Therefore, Plato said: "Do not hand over wisdom to one who does not know its value, for wisdom is more precious than pearls." <note>Botarel frequently attributes such ethical maxims to Greek philosophers to bridge the gap between human reason and divine revelation</note> And Aristotle the philosopher also said: "Do not hide the lineages; do not hand over wisdom to one who is not worthy of it, lest it be snatched away." Our intention is to say that it should not be snatched, as in "lest you turn away the face of the master." This is because if you use the "dust of wisdom" for one who is not worthy of it, the wisdom is belittled in the eyes of the foolish, as it is said: "Speak not in the ears of a fool, for he will despise the wisdom of your words" <note>Proverbs 23:9</note>. 

And the fool pursues the contempt of the philosopher Aristotle... and I have guarded it and revealed it to my children to give... the strength of wisdom is incumbent upon you. I wanted to reveal it to him according to your knowledge...

->A beauty from the depth of a virgin, it is like a great shroud<-
->Before the inner faces, it turned: and was awakened<-
->The whole intention: to return to him<-
->Lacking and finished:<-

# The Introduction of Rabbi Moses Botarel, of blessed memory

He dived into the deep waters of the wisdom of contacting wondrous things concerning the *Book of the Queen’s Seal* <note>original: "Sefer Chotam HaMalkah" — likely a reference to a mystical work or a specific stage of divine emanation</note> in wisdom, may its memory be for a blessing. All of it stands as a vision to the observer. They brought this verse to show that the sphere has power <note>original: "galgal koach"</note> for the strong, and the secret of the foundations, all surrounding the good. For the **Work of Creation** <term>Ma'aseh Bereshit: The secrets of the physical origins of the universe</term> is investigated in the Middle World, which is the world of the celestial spheres, as we explained in our introduction to the *Book of Philosophy*. These spheres revolve the Work of Creation. Since this is found there, we shall mount it <note>interpretive: "narkivah" — a play on "Merkavah" (Chariot), suggesting the integration of concepts</note>, for God placed the four honorable **Living Creatures** <term>Hayyot: The four-faced angelic beings seen in Ezekiel's vision of the Divine Chariot</term> within the **Work of the Chariot** <term>Ma'aseh Merkavah: The highest form of Jewish mysticism, involving the vision of the Divine Throne</term>:

*   **The Face of a Man** refers to the Sphere of the Fixed Stars, which looks down upon the foundation of holiness and is suspended in a dream to awaken the holy heart.
*   **The Face of a Lion** refers to the Sphere of the Zodiac <note>original: "Galgal HaMazalot"</note>, which looks inward at the foundation of the Earth, like a beast prepared with modesty, its strength like a leopard from its source.
*   **The Face of an Ox** refers to the Sphere of the Waters, which looks toward the foundation of Fire. Furthermore, this "ox" provides luxury for the dreamer so that he might prevail—that is, through the stars in the constellation of Taurus.
*   **The Face of an Eagle** refers to the Sphere of the Air, which looks toward the foundation of the Water, as the sages of research <note>referring to medieval rationalist philosophers</note> have written: that every living creature is divided and pursued by coldness and moisture, and the foundation of water is cold and moist.

This foundation prevails when placed in wisdom. He returned the grace of the engraver in everything; therefore, we relied on the sign of the king. Thus, the investigation of the Work of the Chariot is composed of the vision of the one receiving the structure, based on the change in the construction of the Chariot, which is like the four animals. Just as matter, form, and body are composed of structures that are like silver and gold in action and word, so too is the image and law of the Chariot like the four Living Creatures and the four Wheels <note>original: "Ofanim"</note>. It is born with the secret of the spheres, the secret of the accents <note>likely referring to the musical tropes of the Torah</note>, and the Chariot and the Throne. One is called *levul* in the logic of the mind, appearing like the "colors of the electrum" <note>original: "chashmal" — the glowing substance from Ezekiel's vision</note> from which they mount the sections of the heart to the blessing of Isaac of the pomegranate of action <note>likely a metaphorical reference to a specific teacher or the "Pomegranate" (Pardes) of wisdom</note>.

**Moses Botarel says:** I have much to write concerning the words of this Rabbi. This palace <note>original: "heikhal" — often used to describe sections of mystical texts</note> is the connection between the wisdom of Kabbalah and Divine Service—the wisdom of interpretation. The Kingdom appears in the world through the "Key of Kabbalah" and also the "Wisdom of the Talisman." He who habituates himself in its practice will achieve my attainment and recognize my wisdom from this book, for I have spoken in the language of a thing. By my life, I have seen; I did not "wise up" to the secret of the book of incidents that I did not engrave. Therefore, I have not written my words except for one who is designated in the ranks of Kabbalah. At the end of his transformation, the secret of "letter-combination" <term>Tzeruf: A meditative technique involving the permuting of Hebrew letters</term> that I have not hinted at will not be counted. One who strives to be among those who "weigh with the scales" <note>a metaphor for philosophical or mystical judgment</note> must act in the way of humility and wisdom. He must forgive the provocations that arise from the heart, so that the source of Wisdom and Understanding <note>Chokhmah and Binah</note> is not cut off within him.

But outside the holy faith, the "surrounding lights" will not dwell upon him. For the Chosen One of the world <note>the Messiah</note> did not want to rest upon anyone for whom humility was not a covering. This is the secret of "and peace" <note>Hebrew: Shalom</note>—the matter of a proverb for the whole world. Regarding the strength of his virtues, his lot is beautiful. Therefore, look with trembling at research and semantics in holiness. For when the philosopher knows the righteousness of humility, various traits and flaws will distance themselves from him—such as arrogance, haughtiness, and the "evil tongue" <note>Lashon HaRa: Gossip or slander</note>, and other lowly traits. This is because humility includes many types of perfection. Therefore, the coming of the King Messiah is characterized by the trait of humility, as it is said: "Behold, your king comes to you... humble and riding on a donkey" <note>Zechariah 9:9</note>. And it is explained that "poor" <note>Ani</note> here means "humble" <note>Anav</note>, for it is not possible that he would be literally poor when the whole world is in his possession; rather, it is in the trait of humility that he rides upon the donkey. 

Is it not so that the observer must look to complete the preparation of the "Foundation of Glory" to guard it? When a person errs and completes his fall, he becomes immersed in committing transgressions, for "one transgression drags another transgression" <note>a famous quote from Ethics of the Fathers (Pirkei Avot 2:1)</note>. In the measure of the path written in its height, by the teacher of the wise, evil traits will diminish the "divine flow" <note>Shefa: The emanation of divine energy</term> written in the knowledge of the "Heel of Jacob" <note>metaphor for the Jewish people or the lowest level of emanation</note>. If so, in times of anger, a visitation is required.

**Moses Botarel says:** Friends, little by little you shall guard it, and thus we have nested among the wise. Many evils have been spent upon you. And it is hard; for it was written by Maimonides <note>original: "Rambam"</note>, who is the head of the philosophers... <note>The text continues to discuss Maimonides' refinement of philosophical thought in relation to divine action</note> 
 
and he shall return <note>catchword for the next page: "ve-yashiv"</note>

# Introduction of Rabbi Moses Botarel, of blessed memory

Those who know the thirty-six <note>original: "L"V" — referring to the hidden righteous ones (Lamed Vav Tzadikim) who sustain the world</note>, even all the wise men, know that every matter and reason in the Scripture belongs to Him, may He be blessed, to do His will and the gathering of His power according to the capacity. And I have paved a path for them all in the language of the Philosopher <note>Referring to Aristotle, often cited by medieval Jewish scholars as "The Philosopher"</note>; and to examine this wonderful discourse, there is no end. It was proper that I struggled within myself regarding the measure of change, and I walked in the "Deep Way," for I did not want to reveal their secrets as long as I was enslaved by the "bond of bonds." Even if all the money in the world were allocated to me, I would not have merited to reveal them. My intention was well-directed to do the will of the complete Rabbi, Rabbi Jacob of Farli <note>A scholar to whom Botarel dedicated his work</note>, of blessed memory, who dwells in the character of patience. His voice and words of hope make me tremble; I have the strength of their existence to reveal the secret of a matter to every wise and honorable person. Despite all this, it is my ultimate goal to increase the healing; and since I am like a thousand thousands of teachers and good, I shall not be comforted for all the world. I have assisted for my own sake to make oil for the ways of my teacher, the Levite, and I have deepened this intention from our matters into a language within the essence of their foundation. 

Is it not so, that what benefit is there for him to lose the World to Come for the sake of a world of merchandise? Therefore, to reveal the hidden things of my heart is not [done] in a state of freshness <note>or "lightly"</note>. I did not transgress his portions, nor did I open [them]—Heaven forbid I should lack—nor for a fence of destruction. And I placed in the heart of the womb of the Philosopher a clear explanation, and my hope is good to establish us, to wash our palms, more than our kingdoms, to support our dwellings. The heart of the great Rabbi, the "Right-Hand Pillar" <note>An honorific for a great scholar</note>, my lord, my teacher, and my master, Rabbi Jacob of Farli, of blessed memory, agreed through me not to reveal the secret hidden within my heart; and I did not give my secret. And when I gave to him, I cut [a covenant]; for all his ways and portions I have hidden, and never shall they be lacking. You have paved [a path] like those who are dead and preserved. They have a small body, but their power before the world is small; behold, it is a world of subversion. And to add to the written things, like the ecclesiastical ones <note>original: "k'klasiyot" — likely a transliteration of the Latin *ecclesia* or referring to formal assemblies</note>, secure and like oil and secure partial tones, smooth portions. 

And for the sake of the children of Radiance <note>Zohar</note>, for my portion, I shall not pass him the keys and the Tablet of Wisdom. Heaven forbid that I should depict in its essence a physical nature outside of the action of the Impression <note>Reshima: the spiritual trace left behind after the withdrawal of Divine light</term>. And therefore Plato <note>original: "Platono"</note> said: "Do not hand over wisdom to one who does not know its value, for wisdom is more precious than pearls." And the Philosopher also said: "Do not be like the Greeks <note>original: "HaYevanim" — perhaps a reference to the untrustworthy or those who treat wisdom as mere rhetoric</note>; do not hand over wisdom to one who is not worthy of it, lest he pillage it." His intention was to say that he should not pillage it as one might pillage the village of Sakes <note>unclear place name</note>. This is because when a person acts wisely and teaches wisdom to one who is not fit for it, the wisdom is corrupted and it is avenged, as in the statement I wrote in my heart: "Lest he despise your folly." 

And for strength and profit to you, this is the holy thing from the Master <note>original: "Machiystro" (Maestro)</note> and it shall atone as a crown of hearing. I have desired it and for my heart I have revealed it, to give what this cheapening of wisdom is—it is incumbent upon you. And I wanted to reveal my soul to the Merciful One; and behold, our Sages of blessed memory said:

# The Preface of Rabbi Moses Botarel, of blessed memory

We require the wisdom of nature <note>original: "chokhmat ha-teva" — natural science or physics</note> based upon the words of our Sages, of blessed memory. We do not distance ourselves from it, for it is the gateway and the ladder to the wisdom of divinity <note>original: "chokhmat ha-elohut" — metaphysics or the study of the Divine</note> and the foundation of the Torah and its commandments. This is because a person cannot recognize their Creator except through His works and actions, as it is written: "From my flesh I shall behold God" <note>Job 19:26; a central proof-text in Jewish philosophy suggesting that by studying the physical human body or the natural world, one can understand the Creator</note>. Within this framework, we shall strive to organize everything that the Holy One, Blessed be He, created in the celestial spheres and on the earth, the four elements <term>Elements: Earth, Water, Air, and Fire—the basic building blocks of the universe in medieval science</term>, the nature of the beings composed of them, the definition of each one, the force acting within them, how perfection is necessitated through their making, and the ultimate purpose of their intent. This is the language of the wondrous sage:

->Isaac the Blessed of Narbonne, Spain:<-
<note>Likely referring to Isaac the Blind, a foundational figure in Kabbalah who lived in Provence/Narbonne</note>

## Moses Botarel said:
Because you have asked me, I must write the general principles of this wisdom that I received from the wondrous Rabbi Hasdai <note>Possibly Hasdai Crescas (c. 1340–1410), a famous philosopher and theologian</note>, who was the head of all the sages of his generation, of blessed memory. This wisdom is known in the language of the Sages as the **Work of Creation** <term>Ma'aseh Bereshit: The mystical and philosophical study of how the physical world was formed</term>. Whoever accustoms themselves to this wisdom will truly recognize the wisdom of its Maker and the strength of our knowledge in this matter. This is because this wisdom is the foundation of the Torah upon which the world stands; it is a ladder for the enlightened person to ascend to the level of prophecy and the Holy Spirit.

I have labored to draw these truths from the mouths of the true masters of Tradition <note>original: "chakhme ha-kabbalah" — the Kabbalists</note> and to sift their words like fine flour from the bran. You should know that this wisdom is like a single, powerful point within another point, and it has no end. It is necessary to warn anyone who engages in it to be pure of thought, free from all worldly distractions, God-fearing, and pious. 

Whoever is worthy of attaining that "point" will attain knowledge of their Maker according to their capacity. Indeed, they did not refrain from writing that this is a ladder to ascend toward God. Anyone who merits it becomes like the ministering angels. This is the secret of the verse: *From my flesh I shall behold God*. Upon this path are the levels of prophecy. Therefore, look into our book, which points toward this "point." For when you wish to attain the trait of humility, you must distance yourself from ten base and despicable traits, such as pride, anger, mockery, falsehood, and other disgraceful qualities. Through this wisdom, you will achieve everything. 

Therefore, the Creator—exalted be His name—brought forth this wisdom for us so that we might recognize the greatness of His power and might. Everyone who diligently studies it will merit to attain the true attribute of the Master of Mercy. Furthermore, a person must strive to attain intellect and a settled mind. Upon reaching that state, his soul will find rest and be quieted from all the worries of this world. For the humble person, through the trait of humility, will merit two worlds <note>this world and the World to Come</note>, and through this wisdom, he will attain precious traits and the perfection of the soul. This is the secret of the verse: *From my flesh I shall behold God*, in the same way that a builder builds a tall and well-ordered structure.

## Moses Botarel said:
Our Rabbi Saadia <note>Saadia Gaon (882–942), one of the most influential early medieval Jewish philosophers</note>, of blessed memory, said that this is why our Sages called the Day of Remembrance <note>Rosh Hashanah, the Jewish New Year</note> "Remembrances." The Master, our Rabbi Saadia, wrote in his book of commentaries as follows: 

"Every single year, the Holy One, Blessed be He, judges the world; He visits and remembers the deeds of all the human beings He has created. The intellect can tolerate the idea that He calculates and counts the deeds and shall obligate..."

<summary>In this preface, Moses Botarel argues that studying the natural world (science) is a necessary religious duty that leads to the "Divine Wisdom" of Kabbalah. He emphasizes that mystical attainment requires moral purification, specifically the removal of pride and anger.</summary>
<keywords>Ma'aseh Bereshit, Natural Wisdom, Divine Wisdom, Moses Botarel, Saadia Gaon, Isaac of Narbonne, ethics, humility, ladder of prophecy</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the introductory commentaries on Sefer Yetzirah (The Book of Formation), shifting from Moses Botarel's own words to the specific interpretations of two giants of medieval Jewish thought: Nachmanides (Ramban) and Abraham ben David (Ra'avad). It focuses on the mechanics of creation, specifically the "Seal" of the First Cause and the anatomical-cosmological parallels between the human brain and the divine throne.</meta>

### Nachmanides, of blessed memory

His intent in the introduction, with this balanced purpose, is that the First Actor <note>original: "ha-po'el ha-rishon" — a philosophical term for God as the Primal Cause</note>, may He be blessed, acts with goodness and kindness. Thus, this "stroke" <note>original: "makkah" — here meaning an impression, like a seal being struck into wax</note> is sealed in two languages: the word that explains and seals that there is an ancient, supernal world, for it does not change. He argued against his fellow dissenters who would grant peace, and the matter will be decided, for the intent of this is not regarding the earthly kingdom. Therefore, this "stroke" is the decree of judgment in three ancient scrolls. The explanatory word seals the dominion, as well as the complete market <note>interpretive: possibly a reference to the "totality" of existence</note>, and it is the word for all that exists and perishes. 

A portion of His wisdom was mentioned in His praise for one of the circuits of this stroke; therefore, there were "three days of darkness" <note>Exodus 10:22</note> in all the wrath of Egypt. Had Pharaoh himself observed with the eye of dominion, everything would have been like the labor pains of the first stroke. Had Pharaoh possessed the root—without a staff and without lack—his heart would have said, "Let us serve the Lord," and not "Who is the Lord?" <note>referencing Pharaoh’s denial of God in Exodus 5:2</note>. For he thought that day that a distant shadow had risen over the sun. This is the language of our Rabbis, which is found in abundance in the book called *The White of the Philosophers*. 

It was said additionally in the section as was explained to me: these "strokes" <note>plagues</note> of ours were for a blessing. Philosophers have excused their dreams of sovereignty to cancel what is sought in the world for which they toiled. Everything is refined according to their opinion: that the world is primordial, and it never ceased and will never cease, for everything is within the state of the "Dream of the All" <note>original: "cholam mikol"</note>. Therefore, it is incumbent upon us to recognize the sickness of these opinions and their lack; they were destructive to the ultimate degree. 

For the explanation of our internal content, this book is called **The Book of Formation** <term>Sefer Yetzirah: The foundational text of Jewish mysticism</term>. He began his book with twenty-two [letters], in the way that knowledge bequeaths in the broad places. And my King settled in the paths; they are the **Ten Sefirot** <term>Divine emanations or attributes through which God interacts with the universe</term> and **Twenty-Two Letters**. The end of the writing concerns the "Wonders of Wisdom" <note>original: "Pli'ot Chokhmah" — the opening words of Sefer Yetzirah</note>, of blessed memory. They intended their "stroke" to be like one who cleaves <note>original: "poteach"</note>, as the translator renders it. He hinted by this to the "Assembled Frost" <note>a mystical reference to the crystallization of divine light</note> and the support, as Nachmanides wrote, of blessed memory.

-> in the Kingdom <-

---

### Ra'avad, of blessed memory

Through the kindness shown, he wrote a hint as the distance of kindness <note>original: "chesed"</note> upon him from above. Just as the **unformed matter** <term>Golem: Referring here to "Hyle" or prime matter, the substance of the universe before it takes shape</term> receives all types of forms, so the foundation of the simple matter receives all types of engravings. Just as the matter is spiritual, like the matter [of the body], there are thirty places and thirty wisdoms. So this foundation receives thirty places corresponding to thirty wisdoms. 

Just as this matter surrounds it from outside the firmament—similar to the recognition of lots—so this foundation surrounds it like a thin ladder above, with the power of the moon, guarding all the stars. So too in the void, it surrounds thirty merits <note>or "justifications"</note>, lacking within it thirty levels. Our center is the thin leaf above, and within it is the proof of death. This is the **secret of the two membranes of the brain** in the head: one adheres to the bone of the skull, and the second is close to the brain itself. 

Corresponding to this, in the "Higher Yod" <note>The letter Yod, representing a point of divine consciousness</term>, there is a hidden firmament. Just as the matter is lacking below the lower firmament, we would have no existence without the firmament adhering to it. Thus, there is no existence for the void, nor for all the powers of the brain, without the "thinness" <note>original: "duk" — the fine membrane</note> and the garment that surrounds and adheres to the brain. Therefore, we do not consider the lower part "torn" <note>original: "treifah" — ritually unfit/fatally wounded</note>. 

Just as there is above the firmament, which is over the heads of the **Living Creatures**, a distance like a sapphire stone in the "likeness of a throne," so these two membranes contain a clear and thin moisture above, until it is a mediator between air and water. For it is not truly air, nor truly moisture. It is an example of the **Spirit of God** (as it is said) "hovering over the face of the waters" <note>Genesis 1:2</note>. (And because it is said) and they shall be **Sefirot**, of blessed memory, for she includes all that is distant. In herself, she has no form, but she is fit to receive every form. She is like a throne for the body of the "Who" <note>original: "miyah" — potentially a mystical name or a contraction of "water" and "divine name"</note> which surrounds the equal throne, returning to "and above the firmament which is over their heads was the likeness of a throne, as the appearance of a sapphire stone" <note>Ezekiel 1:26</note>. And the likeness is as the distance of kindness upon him from above. It is engraved (in the standing) mentioned. Know that this "Who" <note>Miyah</note> that sits upon the throne is—

-> Book of Formation &nbsp;&nbsp; v 6 e &nbsp;&nbsp; the Wisdom <-

**With thirty-two** wondrous paths of wisdom. One must know that everything the Holy One, blessed be He, created in His world, He created correspondingly within Man, as it is said: "Let us make Man in our image, after our likeness" <note>Genesis 1:26</note>. All this was done so that you might remember, from within your own inner being, the gateways of wisdom that were arranged. "My skin... they have taken this" <note>Job 19:26</note> and "blackness to sow light" <note>original: "shchorot lizroa or"</note>, meaning that Wisdom is lacking in its order. To say "my skin," and in the rectifications of "this," it is like a crown to him, until all its details and generalities were engraved in Man. Through the power of the strong Leviathan <note>original: "Levi Chazak," likely a mystical allusion to strength or the tribe of Levi</note>, for the hand of blackness was like a dwarf, a vessel of light. The secret of the "dwarf" is in the House of Wisdom, and therefore it is hidden in the "circular reversal" for the spirit of all imaginings and for the holes of all things. He is in everything and hidden from everything. If He desired to rejoice with joy in any of these things, it would be to say "her mouth is her mercy," along with all his movements and his companionship. And in the intensity of their strength, if He desired a sickness without pressure before, and for a refuge in part... <unclear>illegible fragment</unclear>
</margin>

<margin>
**With thirty-two** paths of wisdom, wisdom, wisdom. Now he has written in this book the secret of the "sickness of wisdom" <note>referring to those who become overwhelmed by deep study</note> who enter through the cessation of contemplation. And my wisdom is like the matter of "fatness," and he called it "wrapped." Now, this stood against the wise men of his generation. And what are "statutes"? Regarding the matter of divisions in lack. Our Rabbi Saadia [Gaon], of blessed memory, wrote in the *Book of Formation* regarding wisdom and language. The Sages of the Kabbalah <note>original: "Hachmei ha-Kabbalah"</note> say the secret of "statutes" refers to the company [of the Sephirot]. The Sages of the Kabbalah say there are fifty classes—the class of "Broad Matter"—the secret of the world that... from His holiness. Each one is a benefit of the reversal of lack, to give the form of the body and to give the form of destruction; in the action of evil, a good action will not come. And so they taught that the star of Pharaoh was like his "donkey," for "evil is before your faces" <note>Exodus 10:10</note>, meaning this miracle that you rebelled against. And He called God "Good Statute" and "Evil."
</margin>

# With Thirty-Two
->Wondrous paths of wisdom: Yah, the Lord of Hosts, the God of Israel, the Living God and King of the Universe, El, Compassionate and Gracious, Exalted and Uplifted, Dwelling in Eternity, whose Name is Holy—He engraved and created His universe by three "scrolls": by Book (*Sefer*), and Scribe (*Sfar*), and Story (*Sippur*).<-

## Commentary of the Ramban <note>Nachmanides</note>, of blessed memory
**With thirty-two** etc. The explanation is [found] in Babylon, between the drops. For in Babylon is the end where the "high height" is suspended—powers within Wisdom. And from Wisdom come the thirty paths in languages. And because they are the ones designated in Wisdom, the "high head," from the biblical language of "Plus," from the expression "if it be hidden" <note>original: "ki yiphla" — play on the word 'Pli'ot' (wondrous/hidden)</note>... for their consolation they are established. And because there is a difference between one script and another, I have examined likewise the "wonders" (*pli'ot*), which is an expression of "He shall set apart" <note>original: "ki yaphlich"</note>, to remember the "rectification" from the expression of "separation." Every single script is drawn from Wisdom until it reaches the depths. Every single script is within you, a general principle, and the "drawing of wisdom is more than pearls" <note>Job 28:18</note>.

**He engraved** etc. This is like the Cause of Causes, the *Ein Sof* <note>The Infinite/Boundless God</note>, for this is the Supernal Crown (*Keter*), the height of the universe. And the Holy One, blessed be He, engraved them in the precious soul. **Yah** <note>A name of God composed of the first two letters of the Tetragrammaton</note> is the secret of the "Daughter of Equality." And from the power of understanding the matter, this entire matter... knowledge in His three "books" with three pens. I have mentioned my Torah in the name of those mixed within them. Thus, "Book, Scribe, and Story" are the three letters of the Name. And this is the "Book and Scribe" in which everything is included, until the end of the principle that the Holy One, blessed be He, names—for His name is the Name. And he hinted at three, for "With Yah He created the worlds" <note>Isaiah 26:4</note>; "With Yah" refers to these [worlds], and the explanation of "three" refers to the three letters of *Yah*.

<vocab>Sefer Yetzirah, wondrous paths of wisdom, engraved Yah, Ra'avad, Ramban, Ein Sof, His name is the Name, Book Scribe and Story</vocab>

<meta>This page continues the synthesis of Kabbalistic commentaries on the Sefer Yetzirah (The Book of Formation). While the previous page introduced Moshe Botril’s own introductory thoughts and his use of "Exchange" (metaphorical permutations), this page transitions into specific commentaries attributed to the Radbaz and the Ramban, focusing on the visual and structural nature of divine emanations.</meta>

<header>The Radbaz, of blessed memory — Chapter 1 — The Ramban, of blessed memory</header>

### The Radbaz, of blessed memory
<note>Rabbi David ben Solomon ibn Abi Zimra (1479–1573). This commentary explores the visual metaphors of the Merkabah (Chariot) mysticism.</note>

**Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah; the second Sefirah, representing the flash of intuition</term> is like the **Electrum** <term>Chashmal; a mysterious celestial glow mentioned in the Book of Ezekiel</term> (a garment) surrounding the letter *Mem* <note>original: "M"י"</note>, and the Electrum is a "voice, voice" <note>referring to the double nature of divine sound</note> appearing as a vision to her. This means he mentioned the "left side" <note>the side of Judgment or Gevurah</note> regarding Wisdom, for the Name includes many hues, both subtle and thick. This light extends from the "expanse of his loins and upward," and from the "expanse of his loins and downward" it appears as a vision of fire. From the Name, a brilliance and a glow spread out.

From the expanse of **Understanding** <term>Binah; the third Sefirah, which processes intuition into thought</term>, something like the likeness of a rainbow appears; it is the "appearance of the likeness of the glory of the Lord" <note>original: "Mar'eh demut kevod YHVH"</note>. This is because from the upper firmament (**Beauty** and **Understanding**) <note>Tiferet and Binah</note>, many paths spread out from above the decree. The Throne is missing <note>perhaps suggesting it is hidden or beyond grasp</note>, yet it reveals the power of Understanding and Intellect to every companion and his vision.

Know that no sacrifice or favor will be found, nor the paths of the Name, unless you look closely at what I have written here before you; in it, you will find the secret of the **Three Mothers** <note>original: "Gimmel Immot"; the letters Aleph, Mem, and Shin which represent the elements in Sefer Yetzirah</note>, according to what I have said: namely, **Air**, **Water**, and **Fire**. Therefore, it is necessary that they spread out from there—meaning that the paths should be like woven veils, where the three "mother" elements are doubled. Consequently, they spread out from them as paths, which are the **Mighty Deeds** <note>Gevurah</note> of the "Mighty God" <note>Elohim</note> that spreads out across the face of the sea toward the spirit of the **Zodiac** <term>Mazzal; also implying "destiny" or "flow"</term>.

For there is engraving and carving in that world. This is to say that from that Mighty One unto the Name, a "harp" was sent forth to prove the "spirit of pleasantness" for the Levite. For the scent mingles with the air, and life comes from the power of the Name—spirit and brilliance—mingling with the living creatures through the power of the **Incense** <term>Ketoret</term> and the connection to the Spirit. For "the Spirit of Life hovers over the face of the waters" <note>Genesis 1:2</note>. Therefore, this Name is called "The House of our Hand" <note>original: "Beit Yadan"</note>, as the Aramaic translation says: "all the living things," and the living thing merited to exist, and its life is for our foundation, the **House** <note>the letter Bet</note>.

And if He does not give—using the language of "and He placed His Presence" <note>original: "Shekhinato"</note>—the **Sefirot** <term>the ten divine attributes or emanations</term> are from the womb. And there, the attribute is called "Chance" <note>Keri</note>, which acts like a fool to all His creatures. If one is God-fearing, it is well; if not, it is "precious to the Lord." Therefore, the Incense atones for **Anger** <note>original: "Af"; also meaning "nose," where the incense is smelled</note>.

In another [version], a path-letter emerges from the hole of the leaf and moves to the beginning of the line; then it divides, as it is written, into two ways.

---

### The Ramban, of blessed memory
<note>Rabbi Moshe ben Nachman (1194–1270). This commentary, likely part of the pseudo-Ramban tradition on Sefer Yetzirah, focuses on the linguistic foundations of creation.</note>

"In the beginning <note>Bereishit</note> God of Life created..."—the **Beginning** is a portion. There is no "Beginning" other than the **Crown** <term>Keter; the highest Sefirah</term>. "Beginning" refers to your movement, and "Beginning" is as it is written: "The beginning of His glory is the law of your path." And now... from the verse that says, "And the moon is a beginning for him." 

This is symbolized in the word *Bereishit* <note>In the Beginning</note>, which can be rearranged to spell "Tammuz" <note>a month, but here likely a permutation code</note>, for the Sefirot ascended and hinted at the Sefirah called **What** <term>Mah; a name for the Sefirah of Malchut or the essence of Chokhmah</term>, which is the foundation of the whole matter. As it is written: "The Lord by Wisdom <note>Chokhmah</note> founded the earth" <note>Proverbs 3:19</note>. This is the **Heave-offering** <term>Terumah</term>, and it is "Holy to the Lord." This is the measure for the minority of the necessary preparations. 

When one counts the "Holy," there are **Ten Attributes**, and when one investigates the "Ten," it is a hint to the **Ten Sefirot**, may He be blessed. The Sages dealt with tens and spoke of the **Ten Plagues**, and their unification is in the "Ten." [The word *Bereishit*] can also be read as "Thorn of the Pomegranate" <note>original: "Tarmon"; likely a mystical play on the word</note>. These are called "Beginning of Beginnings" in the Assembly of Israel, which is like the **Row of Rows** <note>original: "Tur ha-Turim"</note> for all of them. The scripture calls them "Exiles." 

This has already been interpreted as a **Crown** <note>Atarah</note> that his mother crowned him with. In various places, "and the moon is a beginning for him" refers to Moses. They say of him that our teacher Moses looked through a **Luminous Mirror** <term>Aspaklaria ha-Me’ira; a clear prophetic vision</term> and saw the "Beginning" that belonged to him. Therefore, he merited the Torah, for every star... <unclear>end of quote</unclear>. Thus far are the words of the Ramban, of blessed memory.

And he has **Thirty-Two Paths**, meaning thirty-two ways and matters, as will be chosen later. These are the **Paths of Wisdom**, and they are proven below. 

The "**32 Wondrous Paths of Wisdom**" mean a Wisdom that is hidden and wondrous... and it hints that the Torah descends in three [parts], and every wise man is sent a "Triad." Hinting at this matter, which is wondrous, the Psalmist said: "As for His ordinances, they have not known them" <note>Psalm 147:20</note>. 

Regarding this, the word **Heart** <term>original: Lev; the numerical value of which is 32</term> is used. The "Lulav" <note>palm branch</note> also suggests "Lo Lev" (To Him, the Heart). This refers to the **Great King**, who is in the center, like a decision-maker: five on this side and five on that side, and the "Covenant of Five" <note>a reference to the Sefer Yetzirah's description of the ten Sefirot divided like fingers</note>. This is the dwelling place of the Intellect and Understanding. Regarding this glorious secret, the Holy Torah said: "Hear, for You, O Lord, are in the midst of this people."

<-He said->

It uses the visual metaphor of "Electrum" and the "Rainbow" to describe the emanation of divine light through the Sefirotic structure.</summary>

# The Rambam <note>Maimonides</note>, Chapter 1; The Raavad <note>Abraham ben David of Posquières</note>, etc.

### The Right Column: On the Mystical Beginning

Since their standing is as those who are close, and in every condition...
This cord <note>referring to the thread of divine influence</note> descends and its power is as I have determined for it...
And explore his word, which is divided like the word **Bereshit** <term>In the beginning; the first word of Genesis</term>,
as the Kabbalist wrote... Rabbi I.T. <note>likely Rabbi Isaac the Blind, a 12th-century mystic</note>, of blessed memory,
in the book called *Me'at Tet* <note>meaning "A Little Bit"</note>, of blessed memory: **Bereshit**.
The days of standing <note>the foundations of the world</note> ceased into the Orders: **Zeraim** (Seeds), **Moed** (Festivals),
**Nashim** (Women), **Nezikin** (Damages), **Kodashim** (Holy Things), and **Tohorot** (Purities) <note>These are the six orders of the Mishnah, the foundational text of Jewish law. The author is likely using the word "Bereshit" (consisting of six letters in Hebrew) as an acronym or symbolic pointer to these six orders</note>.
They are the ends of the rings called the "External Threads"
in the chapter... **Bereshit** in combination with the secret
of the numbering in the Torah, and "He created," as it is written: "The Lord possessed me at the **beginning** <note>Hebrew: *Reshit*</note> of His way" <note>Proverbs 8:22</note>. Because of this, one is exempt from bringing the spirit

And regarding this, our Sages of blessed memory said: "**Israel is not [subject] to a constellation**" <term>Ain Mazal Le-Yisrael; the idea that the Jewish people are governed by direct Divine Providence rather than astrology</term>, but
rather [they are] after God, by way of concealment, by way of the hiddenness
of its kind, whose meaning is "treasures and possessions." And especially
we can enter the destiny of the "constellation" through our prayer. And so said our Sages of blessed memory:
