# Chapter 1: The Ramban <note>original: הרמב זל; referring to Rabbi Moses ben Nachman, though this text may be a later commentary attributed to him or based on his teachings</note>, of Blessed Memory

But the **Crown** (*Keter*) which is called "the Innermost of the Inner" <note>original: לפני לפנים (lifne lifnim), a term used for the Holy of Holies, here meaning the most transcendent realm</note>. We shall not grow weary in finding our intention: the first Sefirah is the **Supreme Crown** (*Keter Elyon*), because all the Sefirot included within it spread out from the Supreme Crown. And the Name and its letters <note>original: וה' ויה'; abbreviations for the Tetragrammaton</note> are hinted at by that which spreads out from the power of **Wisdom** (*Hokhmah*), which is in the second Sefirah. This Wisdom is called "as a Book, a Scribe, and a Story" <note>original: כספר וספר ספר; a reference to the opening words of Sefer Yetzirah: "be-sefer, ve-sapel, ve-sippur"</note>, as we shall explain through the words of the sages and their nuances. Thus, in the second Sefirah called Wisdom, there are **32 Paths** <note>The 32 "Paths of Wisdom" consisting of the 22 letters of the Hebrew alphabet and the 10 Sefirot</note>, which are the essence of everything. The philosophers called them by the name: **The Encompassing Sphere** <note>original: הגלגל המקיף; in medieval cosmology, this is the outermost celestial sphere that moves all others</note>.

The third Sefirah, **Understanding** (*Binah*), is called "in a Book and a Scribe as a Book." For it is the **Scribe** who outlines and sets down the solution to the mysteries, and the "Book" itself. Thus, this Sefirah called Understanding "counts" <note>A wordplay on *Sofer* (Scribe) and *Soper* (Counter/Counterpart)</note> the actualized paths of Wisdom. This Sefirah was contained within Wisdom, holding all the nuances and subtle likenesses, and was written from the impressions like a book and like counting. Regarding this, it was said that "He was building worlds and destroying them" in His thought <note>A famous Midrashic concept (Bereshit Rabbah 3:7) explaining that God experimented with creation until finding the right balance of mercy and judgment</note>, showing that the world would not endure unless it were perfect through these three.

And these three are the Sefirot of **Kindness** (*Hesed*), **Judgment** (*Din*), and **Mercy** (*Rahamim*). As it is said: "For the Lord is our Mercy," for they are the Supreme Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding. These are the upper Sefirot called **Height** (*Rum*) and "Truth," and also the **Cause of Causes**. And the Name <note>original: וה' ויה'</note> began with them.

The rest of the remaining Sefirot are those called "in the Book, and he shall tell the Story." This "Story" <note>original: ספור (sippur)</note> refers to the manifestation, meaning the existence of the other Sefirot, which are **Kindness, Judgment, and Mercy**. They are like the **Body**, while the Supreme Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding are like the **Soul** to them. For according to their word, one must lead and act through the Sefirah of **Kindness** (*Hesed*). They are like the body, and Kindness is the **Right Arm**, acting as the part that brings forth the radiance. And as David said, "Yours, O Lord, is the **Greatness**" <note>1 Chronicles 29:11</note>; regarding this Sefirah, he said it, for Kindness is called "Greatness." This attribute is refined only for Kindness <margin>unto our father Abraham, peace be upon him</margin>. It is like snow, or like white wool. Concerning this, the scripture says: "His garment was white as snow, and the hair of his head like pure wool" <note>Daniel 7:9</note>. 

And just as the elbow is large, and the arm is larger than the forearm, and the hand is larger than the fingers, and one finger of Kindness is larger than its fellow—and just as the elbow comes to greatness more than the arm—so you will find that in the "Kindness of the Holy Place" <note>original: מקד"ס (Mikdash)</note> there is something even greater than this. Let the wise man understand.

And **Judgment** (*Din*), the attribute of... is the **Left Arm**. It is called "Might" (*Gevurah*). In this Sefirah, it is refined for Isaac, peace be upon him. Concerning this Sefirah, Isaac said: "The Fear of Isaac was with me" <note>Genesis 31:42; original: פחד יצחק</note>. The word "Judgment" (*Din*) refers to... <unclear>illegible</unclear>... attributes in spiritual realms and physical dimensions. The attribute of Judgment is called a **Knot** <note>original: קשר (kesher)</note>, and it has several colors and likenesses, like a scarlet worm. All this is to teach how many attributes there are, for the portion of Judgment is a strong foundation. And who can tell of it? For its essence is beyond counting. And the "elbow" of Judgment is "knotted" because it is close to **Mercy** (*Rahamim*). For Mercy is like a leaning-staff <note>original: כהשתענעל (ke-hishtanel?)</note> that moves away from the harshness of Judgment toward the flow of compassion. Therefore, Judgment is "knotted" at its beginning. Thus the verse says: "My beloved is white and ruddy" <note>Song of Songs 5:10; white representing Kindness and ruddy/red representing Judgment</note>. 

Also, regarding the attribute of Mercy... and Kindness... a great chariot corresponding to the attribute of Kindness, which leads his banner of chariots with Kindness. Thus said the Great Rabbi **Bahya ben Asher of Toledo**, of blessed memory, in the book called "Before the Inner" <note>original: לפני לפנים; likely referring to his commentary on the Torah or a specific kabbalistic treatise</note>.

**Moses Botril** <note>A 14th-15th century Spanish commentator on Sefer Yetzirah</note>, of blessed memory, said: This secret was hinted at by the Sages when they said: "Three books are opened before Him on Rosh Hashanah..." and so on. Whoever investigates this wisdom... <unclear>illegible</unclear>... will find this from its source. Let him labor and not turn away from the womb of my wisdom, and he shall add strength. One should not let the transience of his intellect and his danger... for he should not have... the danger of folly...

The collected matters of the subject... and the Rabbi **Eleazar Ha-Kalir** <note>A famous 6th-7th century liturgical poet (Paytan)</note> unified in the book called *The Glory of the Lord*: "Book, Scribe, and Story," the three existences, of blessed memory, according to the opinion of the author... in the three "Book, Scribe..."

<summary>This page maps the mystical concepts of Sefer Yetzirah—the Book, Scribe, and Story—onto the ten Sefirot, specifically Keter, Hokhmah, and Binah as the "soul" and the lower Sefirot as the "body." It provides physical and biblical symbolism for Hesed (the white right arm of Abraham) and Din (the red left arm of Isaac).</summary>

# Commentary of the Ramban <note>original: הרמב"ן; Rabbi Moses ben Nachman, 13th-century scholar</note> — Chapter 1, Mishnah 2 — Commentary of the Ra'avad <note>original: הראב"ד; Rabbi Abraham ben David, 12th-century scholar</note>

In another way—and these are his words—our father Abraham spoke in this book in secret concerning the "handful of inclusions" <note>original: חופן הכלולים (chofen ha-kelulim); a cryptic phrase referring to the concentrated essence of all things held within the divine hand</note>. These are the levels held in secret above all that is taught. For to our father Abraham, he spoke of the **Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: the ten divine emanations or "vessels" through which God interacts with the world</term> as being like **sapphire** <note>original: ספיריס (sappiris); the author links the word Sefirah to the Hebrew 'sappir' (sapphire) because of its clarity and ability to reflect light</note>. Just as the sapphire receives light and color, so too will be explained here how they receive all the divine decrees, all the "blessings," and the "elevations." 

All of this is to demonstrate that the Name <note>God</note>, blessed be He, is **One**. Within Him, the **Attribute of Mercy** (*Rachamim*) is mixed with the **Attribute of Judgment** (*Din*), and the Attribute of Judgment clothes itself within the Attribute of Mercy. This is the secret behind the verse: "The Lord our God, the Lord is One" <note>original: סמ"ז refers to the numerical value or a shorthand for the Shema prayer</note>. To this, the sages of blessed memory alluded when discussing the Divine Chariot: "And the Lord passed before his face" <note>Exodus 34:6</note>.

After the heavens were separated from one another, they appeared as **ten circles**, one inside the other. In the very center, there remained a simple globe, the image of **Hyle** <term>Hyle: a Greek philosophical term for "prime matter," the formless substance from which the world was created</term> and the image of the **Land of Life**. He called these "Sefirot"—which are lights, powers, and appearances. Just as the sapphire is the image of the Divine Throne, you must understand from here that everything explained previously regarding the "Encompassing Spheres" within **Wisdom** (*Chokhmah*) was entirely envisioned and engraved in every single heaven. These are the Sefirot, and in each one of them are inscriptions, names, letters, and numbers. This is their form:

<column-break/>

You must know that just as the image of the heavens and all their branches are divided into ten, and so on until the end, there is no end to the number of their natures, as we explained above. Likewise, the power within **Wisdom** extends sometimes into the form of a land, or mountains, or trees. Because their Sefirot are round, these spheres are inside one another, like a ship or a wind and similar things. Each individual, according to their own investigation of the "General Form" that encompasses them, [will see] that these lights are...

<column-break/>

The uppermost heaven, which encompasses all, is a sphere like an **opaque Yod** <note>original: יוד אטומה (Yod atumah); the letter Yod is a single point, representing the beginning of creation before it expands</note>. It stands apart. This is the secret of that which "has already been, its name is called, and it is known that it is **Adam**" <note>referring to Adam Kadmon, the Primordial Man/Universal Form</note>, who is able to contend with that which is mightier than him. These are the...

The names of the ten Sefirot are written vertically through the rings: **Crown** (*Keter*), **Wisdom** (*Chokhmah*), **Understanding** (*Binah*), **Mercy** (*Chesed*), **Strength** (*Gevurah*), **Beauty** (*Tiferet*), **Victory** (*Netzach*), **Splendor** (*Hod*), **Foundation** (*Yesod*), and **Kingdom** (*Malkhut*). The center ring is labeled "The Creatures." Inscriptions around the rings include biblical verses such as "The lion has roared, who will not fear?"] <-

<summary>This page explains the linguistic and mystical origin of the Sefirot, linking them to the clarity of sapphire and the union of Mercy and Judgment. It provides a cosmological model of ten concentric circles, identifying the highest point of creation with the Hebrew letter Yod and the primordial form of Adam.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical commentary on Sefer Yetzirah, shifting from the general introduction of the "32 paths" to a specific analysis of Mishnah 2. It transitions into the definitions of the Ten Sefirot and the division of the Hebrew alphabet into three categories.</meta>

# Commentary of the Ra’avad, of blessed memory — Chapter 1, Mishnah 2 — The Ramban, of blessed memory
<meta>catchword: for its language</meta>

And because he mentioned "writings" and the form of the three "books"—*Sefer, Sefar,* and *Sippur* <note>original: ספר, ספר, וספור; these are the three modes of creation: the text/scroll, the scribe/number, and the story/speech</note>—he returned and included them in the number of the
**Ten Sefirot**, from the language of "counting" (*sippur*) and their derivation:
**Ten Sefirot of nothingness** <term>Beli mah: literally "without anything." This term describes the Sefirot as abstract, non-physical entities</term> **twenty-two**
<gloss>6</gloss>
**letters**
<gloss>P</gloss>
**and foundation letters**
<gloss>P</gloss>
**simple [letters], and so on.** For
the entire book is ordered
according to thirty-two paths
within the World, the Soul, and the Year. And since the first
Mishnah mentioned the order of the World’s arrangement,
he mentions here the order of the Year’s arrangement. And so it is for the entire
*Book of Formation* (*Sefer Yetzirah*); nevertheless, in every single
Mishnah, he hints at these three arrangements.
And it says: **Ten Sefirot of nothingness:** This is a hint
that the supernal foundation, *Chesed* <note>Kindness/Mercy</note>, was encompassing all
the extremities which were created by the "strong hand" <note>a reference to God's power</note>, adjacent
to their collective whole—the ten firmaments, one within the other.
The foundation of *Chesed* was within it "without anything" (*beli mah*), meaning from the Crown <note>Keter</note>
for which there is no hope [of comprehension], and from which there is a boundary and limit and desire.
Therefore it is called *Chokhmah* <note>Wisdom</note>, which is to say, "the palate of what" <note>original: חך מה (chekh mah); a pun on Chokhmah (חכמה), suggesting it is the first taste of existence that asks "What is this?"</note>. And everything
mentioned in the Torah, the Prophets, and the Writings
hints at this Wisdom, as in: "How manifold are your works,
O Lord! You have made them all in Wisdom; the earth is full of
your possessions" <note>Psalm 104:24</note>, to hint that all created acquisitions are
numbered, and Wisdom is as we hinted above.
To everything created, it is called "without value," meaning—and this is
"without anything" (*beli mah*)—from the language of "not yet" and "anything." It is a *Sefirah* of the "closing
of the mouth," for there is no permission to speak or even to reflect upon it.
And it says:

---

**Ten Sefirot**, meaning "countings" <note>original: כפירות (kephirot), perhaps a variant of counting or a play on 'denials' or 'coverings'</note> in a language where one cannot
count more than ten; for when one reaches ten, one returns
to the *Aleph* <note>The first letter, representing the number one and divine unity</note>. The master and Great Rabbi, Isaac son of
Samuel, explained in the book *Keter Shem Tov* <note>original: כח"ס; likely an abbreviation for Keter Shem Tov, "The Crown of a Good Name"</note> regarding these
Ten Sefirot of nothingness:
**Ten Sefirot of nothingness, twenty**
**and two foundation letters: three**
**Mothers, seven Doubles, and twelve**
**Simples.**
In the manner of matter and form,
for the sake of the birth of the latter
portion of the Cherubim.
[They are] like "Sapphires" <note>original: כפורות, but linked to Saphir/Sapphire</note> and crowned
from the matter of sapphire and diamond <note>original: ספיר ויהלום</note>,
almost to the understanding of the stories (*sippurim*) and their derivation.
And in the *Zohar* <note>The Book of Splendor</note>, they are therefore called from "fullness" to "eye,"
according to their placement. And to hide this, it is the secret of the doubling of matter
for the creation of *Binah* <note>Understanding</note>. *Sefirot* means "opening the tapestry."
And in the way of matter upon matter, they are countings, entirely adhering to
the "measure" <note>original: פל"ח; often refers to a scale or measurement</note> on the balance of the Sanctuary of the Lord, the height of the wine,
tapestry and hidden, weakened to enlarge them and to enclose them.
And so our faces shall no longer be shamed, and we shall pray without
weakness, and it was relaxed to be exemplified, and so from the fountain of
the eye... I was pushed to be exemplified.
That is to say, concerning the secret of the verse: "He determined the cut" <note>referring to divine decrees</note> over
everything. Which is to say: regarding the "hope," just as in the "כתן" <note>unclear technical term, possibly a scribal abbreviation</note> in the ten
Sefirot, you must not reflect on its investigation, because all
this is "nothing" (*ayin*), a simple matter in a simple form.
And their secret is the Torah, like this from one side. And this is a hint
of the secret: "Do not seek what is too wondrous for you, do not investigate
what is hidden from you; you have no business with secret things" <note>A famous quote from Ben Sira 3:21, often cited in the Talmud to limit mystical speculation</note>. These are the words of the Rabbi,
Isaac son of Rabbi Samuel, of blessed memory, in the book *Keter Shem Tov*, page 87.
And the Rabbi, Rabbi Bahya, of blessed memory, explained in the *Book of Kabbalah*
regarding the "field of the festival": "Ten Sefirot of nothingness,"
in the manner of...

---

->### Commentary of the Ramban, of blessed memory<-

The explanation of **Ten Sefirot of nothingness** is: two "writings" are there, the letter *Vav* for the combinations and the letter *Yod* for the solutions, as the collective whole is in ten. And in the Kabbalah,
he explained that the "storehouse of all" is for everything that is within the border of ten. That is to say: because every opening is the *Ein Sof* <term>Ein Sof: "The Infinite," God as He exists prior to any self-manifestation</term> and it has no investigation, it has no "what" (*mah*)
and it is the secret of "silence" <note>original: כלימה (kelimah), usually "shame," but used here as a play on 'Beli mah' to suggest a silencing of the intellect</note>. The Kabbalah says: **Twenty-two, etc.** We have explained the collective whole of ten as being divided into three parts:
which are **three Mothers, seven Doubles, and twelve Simples.** And in the way of the 32 "writings," there are 32 letters, and all are included in the collective of ten.

<summary>This page analyzes the second Mishnah of Sefer Yetzirah, defining the Ten Sefirot as "nothingness" (Beli Mah) and introducing the 22 Hebrew letters. The commentators warn against over-investigating the divine essence while explaining the linguistic and mathematical origins of the Sefirot.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical commentary on Sefer Yetzirah, shifting from the general introduction of the "32 paths" to a specific analysis of Mishnah 2. It transitions into the definitions of the Ten Sefirot and the division of the Hebrew alphabet into three categories.</meta>

# Maimonides, of blessed memory — Chapter 1, Mishnah 2 — The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

<note>The header identifies this as a composite commentary featuring the insights of Maimonides (Rambam) and Abraham ben David (Ra'avad), two of the most significant medieval Jewish scholars.</note>

<warning>Bleed-through from the reverse side of the folio is visible, particularly on the left side of the page, but the text remains largely legible.</warning>

Another explanation: that it should be in his power to understand it beforehand, so that he might grasp it like this writing, of blessed memory. And therefore the **Teacher** <note>original: המורה; referring to Maimonides</note> of blessed memory wrote in the **Guide for the Perplexed** <note>original: נב' - נבוכים</note>: "If you strive to attain what is above your grasp, do not begin to fix matters for which there is no proof regarding their excess or doubt; perhaps then you will fall into a distant clarification and arrive at weak arguments," until the end of his words. 

**Moshe Botril** <note>A 14th-century Spanish scholar known for his extensive commentary on Sefer Yetzirah</note> said: The language of "the Teacher" is found [to apply to] the one who denies the **Kabbalists** <note>original: המקובלים; those who study the hidden mystical traditions</note>, who did not wish to learn. And this denial is proven like philosophy to all; their entire aim is to faint within the depths of wisdom, which is not possible except for one whose intellect is pure and based on reason.

And for this reason, I have written concerning some of the reception of natural things—the erroneous philosophies—and a ladder is needed for those who wish to faint according to the intention of wisdom, so that the manner of its wisdom will be established for him, so that he does not produce the opposite and cause their Torah to fail. 

And a great principle was written by **Rabbi Moshe Zacuto** <note>original: רמ"ז; though the OCR reads "Ramz Gadusi," it likely refers to a contemporary authority Botril is citing</note>, of blessed memory, so as not to bring the intention of permission to become weak at your end—the entanglements at the source. Do not begin any matter beforehand until the "silver of the faith of weaknesses" comes to you, for they are a great secret according to all the Kabbalists. And this is the secret: "The daughter of a secret <note>original: בת רז; a poetic way of saying 'a hidden matter'</note> does not cover herself for anyone except a husband," according to the words of the great Kabbalist. 

**Moshe Botril** said: I already knew this account, and because of this, I dreamt a dream. And therefore **Abu-Hamid** <note>original: מוחללה; likely Al-Ghazali, the Persian philosopher often cited by Botril</note> wrote, and so too the scholar **Al-Farabi** <note>original: חל' פון; referring to the philosopher Al-Farabi</note> in his *Book of the Soul*, as follows: "Be pure so that you do not judge the legal decisors; turn your face from covering yourself in order to mock," until the end. 

And I, **Moshe Botril**, through my portion in the wisdom of judgment <note>astrology or law</note>, knew in the ordered manner that this war is righteous and not empty. We are in its company because it is the material, according to its power, to cover the **epiphany** <note>original: פפונטית; likely a corrupted transliteration of a philosophical term like "epiphany" or "hypothesis"</note> in the wisdom of judgment. They will not understand it so much unless they merit to see the name of God <note>original: בה"י</note> and that you shall limit [it], he will understand and there will be no defect.
<meta>End</meta>

---

**Elements** <note>original: אלמנטיס; from Latin/Greek</note>, and they are four opposites. Just as all compounds require a foundation from two opposites—which are heat and cold, and moisture and dryness—one standing against the other; for example, fire is the opposite of water in its death. And behold, the Earth is composed of four opposites. And it is known that the **Material** <note>original: חמר; Hyle or Prime Matter</note> is the **Golem** <term>Golem: In this context, the raw, unformed substance of the universe before it takes specific shape</term>, which Kabbalistic wisdom receives as a power that is the end of merits, and for cold, and for moisture and dryness.

And let us take, for example, that from it comes the quality of cold in its change to become moisture. And so, between moisture and dryness, these are the four simple qualities appropriate for the simple matter called **Golas** <note>likely a variation of Hyle/Matter</note>, and the secret of the "Salt" <note>original: נמ"ל; possibly an acronym</note>. And the cause is the **Spirit of God** <note>original: רוח אלהים</note> which is good wisdom for them. And according to the refinement of the qualities was the order of their establishment: for the **Air** which is simple in the ultimate simplicity is the highest and most honorable of the collective whole. And the **Spiritual Waters** are the foundation of these waters, like the "chaos" (*tohu*) from the heavens that were strengthened within them. "Let there be a firmament in the midst of the waters," and some of it was investigated, and the heavens were formed from below. And these are the **Elements** <term>Strichitis: A transliteration of the Greek 'stoicheia,' meaning the fundamental elements of nature</term>, and this is their order according to the order of the "H-K" <note>original: הה"כ; possibly referring to a specific arrangement of spheres</note>.

And these are his words: for the waters that are close to the Earth are by nature like the nature of the waters that are close to the Air. For those close to the Fire will receive its nature from the nature of heat—a bit of heat and dryness—and the waters which are close...

# Page 16
# The Ra’avad, of blessed memory — Chapter 1, Mishnah 2 — The Rambam, of blessed memory

### The Ra’avad, of blessed memory
<note>Abraham ben David of Posquières, a 12th-century critic of Maimonides and a foundational figure in early Kabbalah.</note>

And these shall all be living, moving with a motion in their powers, and in each and every one of them is a **living soul** and motion. From Him they return and are strengthened. And all the encompassing spheres surround it, with the stars inside them, and they have a motion of freedom <note>original: תנועת חירות; likely referring to the independent orbits of the planets</note> around their shelters, one from another. And some of them are in one sphere, and some in more. 

"Let there be light, and there was light" <note>Genesis 1:3</note>; and it is simple and a shield <note>original: מגן ודוד; a reference to the Shield of David/Magen David imagery</note> in darkness. It includes all the stars, and it is that which revolves in a circle, etc. From the waters of blessings... a secret, and the secret is Yours, O Lord, for eternal kindness. And the Encompasser is a hidden secret—He is the **Holy One, Blessed be He**, of blessed memory. All are ordered according to the "heart of His life." Every detail of every species of plant is within it, because it exists in the secret of "thousands upon thousands, myriads upon myriads" <note>Aramaic: חלף חלפי רבוא רבון; referring to the infinite complexity of the Sefirot</note> of their spheres. 

Above, they are like every individual sphere. Between every *Chayah* <note>term: Chayah (singular) / Chayot (plural): The "Living Creatures" from Ezekiel’s chariot vision, here associated with the celestial intelligences.</note> and *Chayah* among them is the "General" <note>the totality</note>. And the *Chayot* are living, and they "run and return" <note>Ezekiel 1:14</note> and fly from the paths of the Script; they ascend until the **Infinite** <note>original: אין סוף - Ein Sof; the Kabbalistic term for God's essence before manifestation</note>. Because of this, their response and the return of the encompassing heavens come from those that return, which distinguish between one *Chayah* and another, from the recognition of the *Chayot* until the end. 

The priests and the living ones are myriads, and "good are the children of the disciples of the wise," who are the *Chayot*, for it is through them that these spheres and their motion exist within the *Chayot*. This continues until every tongue, every speech, and all **613 Commandments** <note>original: תריג מצות; the total number of Torah laws</note> were refined from them. This is the **Torah** which preceded the world—**Understanding** <note>original: בינה - Binah; the third Sefirah</note>—and it is the Name of the Holy One, Blessed be He. There is no cessation or separation in it, "in My Name," for they are all holy. Their writing is *Y-H-W-H* <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>, and there is nothing more to it than this. 

Therefore, the Rabbi <note>original: אמ"ר ז"ל; referring to the author of Sefer Yetzirah</note> of blessed memory said that the motion was half the holiness of "Hear, O Israel" <note>The Shema prayer</note>, for they are all the **Throne of the Lord**. He said "perfect humors," meaning the foundations <note>the four elements</note>. He said that the sections of the zodiac are... for the Earth is composed of pressure and friction, a simple element. And so it is proper, for it assembled from the cause of life...

---

### The Rambam, of blessed memory
<note>Moses Maimonides, the preeminent medieval Jewish philosopher. This section applies his rationalist philosophy to the mystical text.</note>

"And he called," as Rabbi Yochanan said: "Were it not written in Scripture, it would be impossible to say it." This teaches that the Holy One, Blessed be He, wrapped Himself like a **prayer leader** <note>original: שליח ציבור; one who leads the congregation in a tallit</note> and wrapped His Name; He is at the ultimate end, and He is One and Unique... in this verse: "For the Lord your God is a **consuming fire**" <note>Deuteronomy 4:24</note>. All that is written is included in Him, like a cantor, like the light of the eye... from the humors within the eye. 

His voice—of which there is no portion in the intellect to describe His service—is in complete "moisture" with the God of the pool... meaning to say: "bridle your mouth from speaking" <note>A quote from Mishnah 1:8 of Sefer Yetzirah, urging silence regarding the Divine</note>. The Torah... meaning the world does not exist except by the "Whole" <note>the collective</note>. And all of them are a language of "closing," like one who returns to **Nothingness** <note>term: Belimah: Literally "bridling," used in Sefer Yetzirah to describe the Sefirot as "of nothingness."</note>. 

Man is the "complete voice" in creation; the voice hears from the depths and does not respond, but rather returns and is silent. Therefore, he said *Belimah* <note>bridling/nothingness</note>, but rather he shall gather as fragments... 

And this is what the author wrote: that the heart divides... the researcher of this mystery <note>original: רז - Raz</note> should know that the soul is called "Intellectual Properties" <note>original: סגולות השכליות</note> because it is suspended in matter. Why do the simple properties come first? He said: The intention of the **Teacher** <note>original: המורה; Maimonides</note> was that the Intellectual Properties have no dependence on simple matter, for every secondary intellect has a dependence on matter from the perspective of its evaluation... if it were not so, it would be like the **Separate Intellect** <note>term: Separate Intellect: In medieval philosophy, an intellect that exists independently of a physical body/matter.</note> which is removed from all. 

All simple properties have a complete dependence on matter. And so wrote the master of the logic... and this shall be called by him "the movement of the researcher." It shall be called so to assist the intellect, like one who uses a tool... and if it were otherwise, it would be the Lord always... and regarding the stars, he of blessed memory wrote: Let no man diminish in his thought... to understand.

<meta>catchword: דכר (Remember/Male)</meta>

<summary>This page features a dual commentary on the second Mishnah of Sefer Yetzirah. The Ra'avad provides a mystical view of the celestial spheres and Chayot as the machinery of the divine Name, while the Rambam (Maimonides) offers a philosophical interpretation of God’s unity and the relationship between the intellect and physical matter.</summary>

# The Ra’avad, of blessed memory — Chapter 1, Mishnah 2

And they themselves are higher and lower, from the mud and the foundation of "bread" <note>original: לחם (Lechem). In this context, it may refer to the material sustenance or a permutation of the word for "war" (Milchamah) mentioned later</note>. And we say that each one of the **Sefirot** <note>term: Sefirot (plural) / Sefirah (singular): The ten divine emanations or attributes in Kabbalah through which the Infinite interacts with the universe</note> has a nature of its own, and likewise each of the **Elements**. And there is a garment that clothes the element of matter from the "Chair" <note>referring to the Throne of Glory</note> and below, according to the moisture of L-CH-M <note>original: לח"ם; likely an acronym used here for technical permutations</note>, white and 7 and 9 complete. 

The **Seven Doubles** <note>term: Seven Doubles: The seven letters of the Hebrew alphabet (Bet, Gimel, Dalet, Kaf, Peh, Resh, Tav) that possess two sounds (hard and soft), representing dualities in human experience</note> refers to the number seven which includes their fullness. For the Air contains the power of Water and Darkness; the Water contains the power of Air and Darkness; and the Darkness contains the power of Water and Air. The collection of all these results in the Seven Doubles. And because they are doubled, like male and female, and subject to change <note>original: תמורה (Temurah); refers to the mystical system of letter substitution and the concept of "change" or "exchange"</note>, they are called "Doubles."

### The Rambam, of blessed memory
<note>While the text says Ramba"m (Maimonides), the content involving "the Rabbi in Gerona" and the "camps of the Shekhinah" strongly identifies this as the Ramba"n (Nachmanides).</note>

The contemplation of the Primordial Air is that Wisdom is the Spirit of God. And behold, I have arranged for you the twenty-eight camps of the **Shekhinah** <note>term: Shekhinah: The Divine Presence or the feminine aspect of the Divine that dwells within the world</note>, through which the Presence walks by the hand of the camps to the right. And understanding is Spirit from Spirit; and the snow is Water from the Spirit of the Shekhinah. These are the words of the Ramba"n, of blessed memory. **Metatron** <note>The highest angel in Jewish mystical tradition, often serving as the celestial scribe or mediator</note> said, etc.

His intention was that the Ramba"n, of blessed memory, disagrees on this and says regarding the "Chair" that it was Water from Spirit. For he says that the **Supreme Crown** <note>Keter Elyon; the highest and most hidden of the Sefirot</note>, which is primordial, is the Spirit of the Living God. And Wisdom was Spirit from Spirit, and the Chair was Water from Spirit, and the Snow was Water from Water. Metatron said: The Rabbi in Gerona <note>Nachmanides lived and taught in Gerona, Spain</note> wrote this.

<summary>This page explores the "Seven Double Letters" of the Sefer Yetzirah, mapping them to dualities like Life/Death and Wisdom/Folly, and aligning them with the "times" mentioned in Ecclesiastes. It also includes a technical Kabbalistic debate regarding the primordial elements—Air, Water, and Spirit—between various medieval authorities.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical analysis of the composition of the universe, moving from the Seven Double Letters to the formation of the Four Elements. It contrasts the views of the "Philosophers" (likely Aristotelians) with the Kabbalistic tradition regarding the nature of the celestial spheres. It also introduces a section attributed to the Rambam (Maimonides) regarding the "Cause of Causes" and the first emanation, the Crown.</meta>

## The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

They are near to the worthy portion and received what was worthy from them. There are some difficulties, but he observed the wisdom and the consensus regarding the simple [elements] in the mixtures of water <note>original: בחיכות המים (be-chichot ha-mayim). "Chichot" is likely a scribal variant for "Ichuyot," meaning unions or mixtures</note>. When they mingled, death did not occur <note>A common philosophical theme where the "death" or dissolution of one form allows for the "birth" of a new mixture</note> in these mixtures. 

Through their cause, some parts separate from others and intermingle with one another, and thus the fourth mixture is formed, which is called **Dust** <note>term: Dust (Afar): In medieval physics, this refers to the element of Earth, the densest and lowest of the four physical building blocks</note>. Below it is the moisture and the dampness, for the sphere of matter is... and the "Snow" <note>original: סלג (Seleg/Sheleg). Likely referring to a primordial state of matter mentioned in the Sefer Yetzirah: "He made them like a trough, He set them up like a wall, He covered them like a ceiling."</note> comes from the cold and the air. It is the "snow" from the desolation of His **Throne of Glory**. 

And here the **Philosophers** <note>term: Philosophers (Pilosofim): Usually referring to the followers of Aristotle, whose scientific views often clashed with Kabbalistic tradition regarding the divinity of the celestial bodies</note> erred; they said that the elements are as described by the philosophers beneath the sphere of the Moon. They claim that matter is the foundation for the cities and the cause for the existence of the four simple mixtures, and that they depend on the existence of the composite mixtures and their powers. 

They also erred in the number of the elements, counting only four. Thus, they wrongly denied his matter. They placed **Fire** above, followed by **Air**, followed by **Water**, and followed by **Earth**. They further erred in their desire to say that the **Spheres** <note>term: Spheres (Galgalim): The concentric celestial orbits that medieval science believed carried the planets and stars around the Earth</note> are simple and not composed of the elements. 

But according to the Sages of blessed memory, the order of matter in the **Upper World** (the World of the **Sefirot**) is the beginning of all things. In the **Middle World** (the World of the **Angels**), this same order holds. And in the **Lower World** (the World of the **Spheres**), the order of matter is reversed, as will be explained. For the logic is turned about... like clay under a seal. Therefore, He who ordered this world in His wisdom placed fire above, followed by air, and water below. Their error stemmed from what they said: that the spheres are not made of the elements. It is clarified in the "Wisdom of Lines" <note>referring to geometry or astronomy</note> that they are related—snow to earth, and always distant as fire is to water.

---

## The Rambam, of blessed memory

Moses said: <note>Referring to Moses Maimonides (Rambam), the famous 12th-century philosopher and jurist</note>
Before I begin to bequeath these chronicles of the worlds of God, the Wise One, my pen took no delight until He graced me. This is engraved as an explanation written upon the floor of a desired object, and the cause of it all is received from the Great One in his book called the **Book of Knowledge** <note>original: Sefer HaMadda. This is the first book of Maimonides' Mishneh Torah, dealing with the foundations of faith</note>, may his memory be a blessing.

Blessed be the name of the Creator, who is living and existing forever and for all eternity. He is the beginning of all things; He is the first of all and the most ancient of all. He is hidden from all, and He is the **Cause of Causes** <note>term: Cause of Causes (Eilat Ha-Illot): A philosophical term for God as the ultimate source of all existence</note>. One cannot say of Him that He has "parts" or "division," for He is hidden even from the angels, as well as from those who "dwell in houses of clay" <note>A biblical idiom for mortal humans</note>. And regarding Him it is said: *“Do not seek out things that are too wondrous for you.”* 

We know that everything depends on Him. We are not holy, but to us and for us all things were innovated/created. He, blessed and be He blessed, has no change of desire nor change of will. The philosophers call Him the **Essence of Thought**, and He is the "Grasper" <note>The Intellect</note>, and some of them call Him the **Active Intellect** <note>term: Active Intellect (Sekhel HaPoel): In medieval philosophy, the lowest of the celestial intellects that mediates between the divine and the human mind</note>, for everything comes from Him. 

Behold, it "arose in His thought" to extend and to emanate <note>term: Emanate (Le-atzil): The process by which divine light flows from the Source into creation</note>. The meaning of "revelation" is like the language of "purity," clear and pure. What is a "Vessel of Thought"? He is the **Cause of Causes**, blessed be He, who is an absolute perception such that we cannot grasp Him in our thought. 

There is also **Restraint** <note>original: Belimah; a term from Sefer Yetzirah usually meaning 'nothingness' or 'closed,' here interpreted as restraining the mouth from speaking</note>, like restraining your mouth from speaking. Therefore, our Sages of blessed memory returned to the phrase "it arose in His thought," for His thoughts are not like our thoughts. Thus it is said because man requires the power of thought; with the material of the senses, one person can descend into the depths of another's mind. But regarding the **Cause of Causes**, thought must receive from Him. Therefore, the Sages used the term "arose" and the phrase "arose in His thought" to domesticate the language to what the human ear can hear. At that moment... it arose in thought; therefore, Thought is called **Crown** <note>term: Crown (Keter): The highest of the ten Sefirot, representing the first emergence of Divine Will</note>.

## The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

Thus far are the words of the Master of Tradition [Kabbalah]. Thus far are the words of the Master of Tradition in the book called *Losi Ha-Mus* <note>original: לוזי המוס. This likely refers to a philosophical or logical work cited by Botarel, possibly a corruption of "Logica" or a pseudo-epigraphical work attributed to a Greek philosopher</note>. Moses Botarel <note>Moses Botarel (c. 1350–1410) was a Spanish scholar famous for his commentary on the *Sefer Yetzirah*, often citing obscure or lost sources</note> said: In honor of the Sage with whom I have associated; he and all his wisdom are like unto Ptolemy <note>term: Ptolemy (Plunmi): The 2nd-century Greco-Egyptian astronomer and mathematician whose works were foundational to medieval science</note>. ### The Ra’avad, of blessed memory: Chapter 1, Mishnah 2, and the Ramban, of blessed memory

And all thoughts are below, and from them come all the judgments of the pathways of <term>Chesed</term> <note>original: חסד; the attribute of Lovingkindness or Mercy</note> which seeks the welfare of all creatures. 

It is known that the name of my purifier's portion is like the name of the generation whose judgment is the L-RD <note>original: ידוד; a common substitution for the Tetragrammaton Y-H-V-H</note>. Behold, they directed their intent within it; they directed the L-RD into our hollows <note>original: חללינו; possibly referring to the internal cavities of the body or the "vacant space" of creation</note>, the L-RD at our right hand, and they directed their intent within it to our left. I have sent, and the King, the L-RD, is King at your right hand. And from these emanate all the judgments of the birth of children from the side of Lovingkindness, or their death from the side of <term>Din</term> <note>original: הדין; the attribute of Stern Judgment</note>, and the planting of souls in the Tree of Life—for it is to him like a "tree of the field"—or the uprooting of that which is planted from the Tree of Life; whether to kill or to heal from the "land of the living," or to break down and to build up the Ladder of Existence.

**"A time to weep"** refers to the departures of Lovingkindness when it departs to take back a soul and to sustain its life. **"A time to mourn"** refers to poverty, for he is cast out from his sustenance. **"A time to gather"** refers to the body <note>original: פג; literally "corpse" or "unripe fruit"</note> to bring the dust back to the side of <term>Gevurah</term> <note>Strength/Judgment</note>, for from the dust he came and to it he shall search. And its replacement is poverty. 

**"A time to seek"** after life and wealth. **"A time to lose"** all through poverty and change. 

**"A time to laugh"** from the side of the <gloss>tooth</gloss> <note>The Hebrew letter Shin (ש) resembles a tooth (Shen) and is associated in this context with 'Sivug' or spiritual coupling</note> of union. **"A time to refrain from embracing"** from the side of nullification. 

**"A time to keep"** from the side of the Manna. **"A time to throw away"** the spindle. 

**"A time to take refuge"** from the side of Wisdom (<term>Chokhmah</term>), for even regarding a silent army, "a wise man shall become wiser." **"A time to speak"** with a voice like water, in a spirit of words, and a mouth that stumbles from looking toward him. 

**"A time to sew"** together the parts of a dream for the "children of change" through Wisdom. **"A time to tear"** from the side of transformation. 

**"A time of war and a time of peace"** from the side of the Foundation (<term>Yesod</term>). **"A time to love"** from the side of peace. **"A time to hate"** from the side of transformations. 

**"A time of release"** from all servitude. 

**"A time to dance"** from the side of Dominion. For the "thirty-two paths" <note>The 32 paths of wisdom mentioned in the Sefer Yetzirah</note> are like a horse to the heart's power; the camps of the six directions surround every single person. Thus, for the Kingdom (<term>Malkhut</term>), there is the power of the camps where the Divine Presence dwells, stripping away its power until the covering of the world. And these are nearly revealed according to the law of the thirty-six <note>original: ל"ו (36); likely a reference to the 36 hidden righteous ones or a numerical division of the celestial spheres</note>, as it is written, and with the doubling of the letters of the Name.

---

As I wrote in the *Book of the Chrysolite* <note>original: ספר התרסיס (Sefer Ha-Tarsis); a work by Moses Botarel</note>, the word <term>Sefirot</term> is like "I do not know the numbers" <note>Psalm 71:15</note> or "Who can number the clouds by wisdom?" <note>Job 38:37</note>, for it is above the sapphire. That is to say, this is not written, Heaven forbid, for the physical senses to behold, but rather it is the permutation of the Name. 

**Twenty-six letters.** 
The explanation is that they are twenty-two letters plus the four letters of the Name <note>The Tetragrammaton Y-H-V-H has 4 letters, which when added to the 22 letters of the Hebrew alphabet, equals 26—the numerical value (Gematria) of the Name itself</note>. The Master of Tradition wrote in the book called *Treasury of Life* <note>original: אוצר חיים (Otzer Chaim)</note>: Regarding the house of the letter *Kaph* and the house of the letter *Het*, when you look at their fullness, rest in the secret of the "House of Beginning" <note>original: בית בראשית (Beit Bereshit)</note>, for there is no "first" except for the Torah, as it is said: "The L-RD possessed me at the beginning of His way" <note>Proverbs 8:22</note>. 

The Torah is like the "Assembly of Life" to those who study it and engage with it holistically, as it will bring light to our eyes. This means the Torah will witness for him and be his aid. A hint to this is found in the acronyms of the start of the five books of the Torah: **B**ereshit, **V**e-Eleh, **V**ayikra, **V**ayidaber, **V**e-Eleh. Through the secret of the calculation, it equals "for the lap," and the suffixes of the words together equal the calculation of "Torah," and it brings forth the word "poor" with the students of Torah.

Therefore, the Sages of blessed memory said: Rabbi Judah said that every night, **Metatron, the Prince of the Countenance**, seals all the yearnings of the disciples of the wise who engage in the Torah to delight them, and he brings them before the Holy One, Blessed be He. The Ministering Angels hint at the study of Torah according to the literal meaning along with the souls of the righteous. It hints at how He favored us with His spirit in the land, for those who engage in the Torah for its own sake.

The **Bent Kaph** <note>The letter כ as written in the middle of a word, rather than the straight final ך</note> hints at the **Attribute of Judgment**. This hints that the Holy One, Blessed be He, does not speak to a person with the Attribute of Mercy except when, in his sleep, he accepts upon himself the yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven, and then a Crown is given to him. 

Therefore, our Sages of blessed memory said: "A person is obligated to bless for the evil just as he is obligated to bless for the good," as it is said: "I will sing of lovingkindness and judgment" <note>Psalm 101:1</note>. If it is lovingkindness, I will sing; and if it is judgment, I will sing. And when you are reconciled and cleave to the Blessed Name, doing the will of our Father in Heaven, the Attribute of Judgment is transformed into the Attribute of Mercy, and it receives the "Countenance" with an outstretched hand, like...

# The Ramban, of blessed memory: Chapter 1, Mishnah 2; The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

And all of us are within the power of the Creator <note>original: היכולת; literally "the Power," a common rabbinic metonym for God</note> entirely, for He is the Knower and the Witness, for He is in all and He is everything, and everything is in Him and from Him. He knows [everything] down to the finest detail, and this is the secret of "For the L-RD knows the way of the righteous" <note>Psalm 1:6</note>. He causes His goodness to pass before the person <note>original: קלדם; likely a corruption of "Adam" (man)</note>, which is the secret of the flight <note>original: הבריחה; referring to the soul's movement or divine emanation</note>. Therefore, the one who keeps the Torah is guarded from sin. Thus it is written in the *Shi'ur Qomah* <note>term: Shi'ur Qomah; an early Jewish mystical text describing the cosmic dimensions of the divine "body"</note> that the L-RD infuses every body, and everything is distinct from every body, and the "conquering of the body" <note>original: הכביש מסגוף</note> is a matter of chance.

Rabbi Ishmael said: Anyone who knows the purification <note>original: נירופו; likely "Tzeruf," the mystical permutation of letters</note> of the Creator in its breadth is assured that he is a child of the World to Come. And Rabbi Akiva agrees with this matter. This is achieved through the occupation of the soul <note>original: סלס; likely "Nefesh"</note> with our strength; for anyone who does not cling to the breadth of the intellect will certainly be like a shortened branch.

Therefore, it is said that the "breadth" of the seven [double letters] consists of seven faces... <note>The text here discusses the "Seven Double Letters" of the Hebrew alphabet: Bet, Gimel, Dalet, Kaf, Pe, Resh, Tav</note>. Therefore, many of the Kabbalists have erred and sinned regarding the secret of what they have heard, and they did not tie it to the truth of the Supernal Man in the light that shines and holds fast. They became like damagers <note>original: מזוקין; spiritual entities that cause harm</note>, and all of this happened to them because they lacked the true names. For they did not see their greatness and their essence except by way of the secret of "speculation" <note>original: הסתקלרות; likely "Histaklut" (contemplation)</note> by directing their thoughts. This is "Glory" <note>Kavod</note>, and for their end, only a portion was revealed to them. But he <note>the sage</note> alluded to it as the "Cloud," which is the secret of the "Child," and he is "Holy, Holy, Holy," according to the book of *Sefer Ha-Pardes*.

This study was received via <term>Gematria</term> <note>original: גימז'; a system where letters represent numbers to find hidden meanings</note>, and this is the "Good Light" above which there is nothing higher. To hint at this, the forms of the letters are like the light in a crown, as written by the Rabbi, Rabbi Reuben the Spaniard, in the *Sefer Ha-Emunah* <note>original: ספר האמונה; "The Book of Faith"</note>. Then there were "Torah" and "Torah-Light," for it is written that "the Commandment is a lamp and the Torah is light" <note>Proverbs 6:23</note>. This is like "comforting the King" <note>original: תנח למלכא</note>.

Moses Botarel <note>A 14th-15th century scholar known for his commentary on Sefer Yetzirah</note> said: Anyone who swears by the seven [names] of the L-RD, God of Israel—the names and the mentions will not help except for those who know the secret of their placement and resting. Once he knows the secret of the "Permutation" <note>Tzeruf</note>, whatever he asks for will be done for him.

They amount to 30 times 42, which are 1260 letters. And if we count them as 361 letters, they amount to 28 <margin>In his people <unclear>sh-n-r</unclear></margin> camps, for he desires 28 deeds of the decree for His people "to give them the heritage of the nations" <note>Psalm 111:6</note>, and to say "Great is our Lord and abundant in power" <note>Psalm 147:5</note>. And He wrote in the deeds all the decrees.

And he said "Twelve Simple [Letters]" <note>original: יב פשוטות</note>, meaning they are light and have no "exchange" <note>meaning they do not have two pronunciations like the double letters</note>, but they are simple and unified without change. Like Air, Water, and Fire—each one is composed of its fellow. They were established to be six, for in the Air there is the power of Water and Fire, and in the Water there is the power of Air and Fire, and in the Fire there is the power of Air and Water; behold, they are six.

And each one of these is included in the "hidden power" and the "revealed power," for the two of them judge the whole. Therefore they were established to be the number 12. These are the 12 hidden existences <note>original: הוויות</note>... and the 12 revealed existences... and the 12 constellations (Zodiac), and the 12 months, and the 12 organs, and the 12 leaders of the first things, and the 12 tribes of Jah <note>the tribes of Israel</note>, and the 12 leaders in the world...

And in the judgment and in the light, why did he call some of them "Doubles" and others "Simple"? Was it not because there is an exchange for Life [which is] Death, and an exchange for Peace [which is] War, and an exchange for Wealth [which is] Poverty, as mentioned above? Behold, these 12 Simple ones also have [opposites]: Sight, Hearing, Smell, Speech, Eating, Coition, Action, Anger, Walking, Laughter, Thought, Sleep. They have an exchange! For the exchange of Sight is Blindness; the exchange of Hearing is Deafness; the exchange of Smell is Blockage; the exchange of Eating is Gluttony; the exchange of Speech is Muteness; the exchange of Coition is Impotence; the exchange of Action is Disability; [the exchange of] Laughter is Anger; [the exchange of] Walking is Lameness; [the exchange of] Thought is Error; [the exchange of] Sleep is Wakefulness. If so, why are they called "Simple" while the former are called "Double"?

And there is no reason for this, except that the "Exchange" and the "Permutation" are not similar to the [original] action. If so, why is it called an "exchange" if it is only the absence of the first thing? And the matter is known, for the opposite is [made of] two things...

It emphasizes that true spiritual power comes from understanding the "Tzeruf" (permutation) of these divine letters.</summary>

# The Ra’avad, of blessed memory: Chapter 1, Mishnah 2; The Ramban, of blessed memory

## The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

And we shall say that the place of the completion of the Kingship <note>term: Kingship (Malkhut); the lowest of the ten Sephirot, representing the physical world and divine sovereignty</note> is in the fifth sphere. Its movement consists of twenty-eight "existences" <note>original: הויות; likely referring to the twenty-eight lunar mansions or stations of the moon</note> through Loving-kindness, completing a cycle of twelve "existences" through Grace and Secret. This is the place of their birth <note>original: מולדם; referring to the Molad, the appearance of the new moon</note> until they withdraw themselves from view to such an extent that the prayer is received back. And this is the secret of the "Grace" between one New Moon and the next: 29 [days], 12 [hours], and 793 [parts] <note>original: כט יב תש צג; the traditional Jewish calculation for the lunar month: 29 days, 12 hours, and 793/1080 of an hour</note>.

According to the division of these places, all the judgments of the Kingship are determined. For behold, all the foundations of Loving-kindness will be acted upon in one of the twenty-eight places; they receive decrees from the places of Loving-kindness through the twenty-eight mentioned times. This is the secret of the verse: "To everything there is a season, and a time to every purpose under the heaven" <note>Ecclesiastes 3:1</note>. This is also what is meant by "He will open to him..." <note>unclear in source</note>, and "it is well with the righteous person and well with his neighbor" <note>original: טוב לצדיק וטוב לו; a common Talmudic expression regarding divine justice</note>, and "it is ill for the wicked and ill for his neighbor."

A parable for this: His strength is like a righteous person, and all his potential is born at the time of the waning of the return. Through this lack, his affairs will be affected by deficiency, for "it is enough for the servant to be like his master." However, the Holy One, blessed be He—whose merit is always prepared for him—strengthens him in the Kingship. So it is in the *Book of Permutations* <note>original: ספר תמורות</note>. Conversely, if one is born as a wicked person in all his parts during the days of <unclear>destruction</unclear>, goodness will reach him from the Loving-kindness of his time, which is prepared to be aided by the Kingship—this is the Secret Presence <note>term: Shekhinah; the Divine Presence or the feminine aspect of God</note>. And he is saved from the day of evil and from the time his foot stumbles.

From this Torah, it will be clarified for you regarding the movement of the Moon at the completion of the cycle of days: 2 days, 9 hours, and 89 parts <note>unclear calculation</note>, encompassing all "existences" in 365 days, 60 hours, and 232 parts. Between one New Moon and the next: 29 days, 12 hours, 793 parts. Behold, the phenomena <note>original: הפנומנ'</note> will occur in the head of Aries during the Spring Equinox <note>term: Tekufat Nisan; the spring equinox</note>, in the head of Cancer during the Summer Solstice <note>term: Tekufat Tammuz</note>, in the head of Libra during the Autumnal Equinox <note>term: Tekufat Tishrei</note>, and in the head of Capricorn during the Winter Solstice <note>term: Tekufat Tevet</note>...

---

## The Ramban, of blessed memory

On this day, He began to show my greatness and the goodness of my hand <note>original: חופן שתנדותי; likely a poetic way of describing divine bounty</note>. The wise will understand me, but the <unclear>ignorant</unclear> will not perceive me. Why is the word *Elohim* <note>God</note> used? Regarding the Attribute of Judgment <note>term: Middat Ha-Din; the aspect of God characterized by strict justice and law</note>, the Master of Kabbalah wrote in the *Book of Visitation* <note>original: ספר הפקידה; a mystical work often cited by early Kabbalists</note> regarding the cycles, and these are his words:

The world was created with the Attribute of Judgment, as it is written: "In the beginning, *Elohim* created..." <note>Genesis 1:1</note>. The name *Elohim* hints at the Attribute of Judgment. For *Elohim* in the "Secret of the Treasure" permutation <note>term: Tzeruf; the mystical technique of rearranging or substituting letters to find hidden meanings</note> is like the word <unclear>judgment</unclear>. By its hidden secret, His loving-kindness judges the entire world, and He sits upon the Throne of Judgment. This is what is meant by "We shall take <unclear>together</unclear> and He shall do good for us." From within the name *Elohim*, we are strengthened and supported. This raises the unity of the Attribute of Mercy <note>term: Middat Ha-Rachamim; the aspect of God characterized by compassion</note> through the permutation...

Behold, the world is perfected through the "Secret of the Treasure" permutation. Therefore, one should be compassionate. Loving-kindness will give its hand as a secret of contemplation <note>original: הסתבוננות; a variant of "Hisbonenut" or meditation</note> at the city gate. He shall desire the secret of "Sufficiency." He shall shatter, shatter, shatter <note>original: מנפץ; likely referring to the breaking of husks or shells in Kabbalah</note>... and sustain every purpose. The hidden power is crowned with Loving-kindness. As the *Book of Visitation* says: the secret of the Name <note>original: יהור; likely a variation of the Tetragrammaton</note> is founded upon the crossroads.

"May Joseph protect me, may Joseph protect me, may Joseph protect me" <note>original: יוסף יסמרני; likely a protective incantation or a play on the name Joseph and the verb for 'guarding'</note>. "For He will order His angels to guard you in all your ways" <note>Psalm 91:11</note>. "For He will order His angels to guard you in all your ways." "For He will order His angels to guard you in all your ways." And they shall be arranged like a sphere, pure upon his face, established by the secret of the ruler over the nine edges, sealed with the Name of the Rings. All is holy, directed by every thought.

Moses Botarel <note>note: a 14th-century scholar known for his commentary on Sefer Yetzirah and his claims of mystical knowledge</note> said: Anyone who knows the permutation of His name in the secret of Wisdom and the Divine Presence <note>Shekhinah</note> is able to make use of them, for "Sufficiency" is found in holiness and purity, and in the turning away from sin. One can then be confident in his actions and his sustenance.

<meta>This page continues the technical and mystical commentary on the movement of the celestial bodies as they relate to the Divine Names. It builds on the previous page's discussion of the lunar cycle (29 days, 12 hours, 793 parts) and transitions into the specific permutations of the Tetragrammaton that govern the seasons and the zodiac.</meta>

### Chapter 1, Mishnah 2: The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

But the judgments of the worlds are conducted according to the "Existences" <note>original: הויות (Havayot); permutations of the four-letter Name of God, Y-H-V-H</note>, according to the Unity and its end, and according to the *YaKNeZ* <note>term: יקנעז (YaKNeZ); a mnemonic for the order of blessings in Havdalah, here used to represent a specific sequence of celestial or legal transitions</note>. And behold, I have arranged for you houses <note>meaning: astrological houses or positions</note> for the worlds, to focus the mind upon the Name for all the judgments of the Lord. For "the Lord God is a sun and shield" <note>Psalm 84:11</note>; the Divinity joins with twelve Existences. You shall not make a mistake: the camp of the sun’s path travels through every three Existences. 

And behold, for the Sun <note>The OCR likely has a typo here; the context refers to the Sun's movement</note>, two Existences cover six days and a quarter <note>referring to the calculation of time for the sun to move through specific celestial divisions</note>. From this calculation, you will be able to know on each and every day its strength; a degree stands in its "houses" in the division of the year. In the course of the days, it has another calculation, and you shall listen to understanding <note>a play on the word Binah (Understanding)</note>. As it is written: when it travels until an addition of 85 degrees within six days and a quarter of a day, it reaches one part of the sphere—meaning one zodiac sign of thirty days—with a calculation error of 507 parts.

When you divide this number by thirty in order to know how much time it takes to travel one degree, the path of the sun will reach one degree in one day and two parts <note>term: parts (chelaqim); the Hebrew calendar divides an hour into 1,080 parts</note>; for twenty degrees, it takes twenty-eight [days], [missing] hours, and 469 parts; for three degrees, it takes three days, 8 hours, and 462 parts. For the whole year, every day involves 9 hours and 516 parts of the sun’s travel for 26 degrees. In another 20 days and 340 parts, the sun travels 20 degrees. Thus, in 27 days, 7 hours, and 163 parts, the sun travels 29 degrees. The Moon <note>original: הלבנה (HaLevanah)</note> travels one degree in one hour and three "houses" of parts and a half-part.

This is the precise calculation of the "Kingship" <note>Malkhut</note> regarding the Sun and the Moon. Our Sages, of blessed memory, taught: 29 [days], 12 [hours], and 793 [parts] from one New Moon to the next. Know that there is no place for a zodiac sign to return the Moon to its inhabited place. However, the Moon, within ten [unclear], uses shaking degrees after it cleaves to them, and because of this, a "hole" <note>likely referring to the moon's phase or an eclipse</note> is born in it from the Sun. From the time of cleaving <note>conjunction</note> until the Moon completes its path of 85 degrees is 27 days and [unclear] hours and 230 parts. 

Ultimately, at the distant time, the Sun stands from the place of the previous "cleaving" <note>conjunction</note>—which passed in 27 days, 9 hours, and 409 parts—at 26 degrees. The Moon travels in 27 days, 7 hours, and 163 parts through 29 degrees. The total is two days, 22 hours, 1 minute, and 306 hours <note>textual error in OCR units</note>, 518 parts, and two-sevenths and a half-part for 30 degrees. The Moon travels in one season, including its "houses" of parts, half a part, and a quarter part, one degree. It follows that the Moon joins with the Sun within 29 days, 12 hours, and 953 parts <note>This is a slight variation from the standard 793 parts, perhaps a specific manuscript variant or calculation</note>.

By this light, look at the calculation I have raised for you; you will be able to discern from it at every moment, every hour, and every day, that you should pursue the equality of the three "M-Z-B" Existences for the Name of all Existences. Understand this, and it will reveal to you all future events according to the secret of the Name... <note>The text becomes highly metaphorical, mentioning the "burning of the Queen of Existences like stars"</note> for the "M-Z-B" <note>likely a mnemonic or abbreviation for a specific set of divine names or celestial positions</note> tell the depth of matters, as it is said: "In the beginning..." and "Let them stand now..." <note>Isaiah 47:13</note>. They also mentioned the heavens acquired like stars, providing knowledge for the months that shall come upon you; and our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "From some of [what will happen]," but not "all [that will happen]." And from here:

-> You can understand the prayers and the secret of the operations. <-

Know that the wheel of Names is from the Name of twelve Existences <note>permutations of Y-H-V-H</note>. The one that begins with the letter *Yod* <note>Y</note> is for the month of Nisan <note>March/April</note> and the sign of Aries—this is the secret of dreams. Three names from their beginning start with the letter *He* <note>H</note> for the summer season <note>Tammuz</note>, corresponding to Cancer, Leo, and Virgo. Three names from their beginning start with the letter *Vav* <note>V</note> for the autumn season <note>Tishrei</note>, corresponding to Libra, Scorpio, and Sagittarius. Three names from their beginning start with the letter *He* <note>H</note> for the final ones, the winter season <note>Tevet</note>, corresponding to Capricorn, Aquarius, and Pisces.

Know that the Name of 26 <note>note: The numerical value of Y-H-V-H is 26</note> has the "Power" <note>original: כח (Koach); numerical value 28</note> of the ten of Israel. This is the secret of the verse: "And now, I pray, let the power of my Lord be great" <note>Numbers 14:17</note>. Every use of the word "Power" <note>Koach</note> hints at this secret. Behold, this Name is established for the name of all the prophets of the Lord, and the Name of all "He-Will-Be" <note>original: יהוף; likely a variant of the Name</note>, and it reaches to the [unclear] of each one of them—meaning the seventy gifts and peace.

# The Ra’avad, of blessed memory — Chapter 1, Mishnah 2 — The Rambam, of blessed memory

They said that regarding the category of two opposites, there is a neutral space <note>original: חלין (hullin); literally 'profane' or 'unconsecrated,' here referring to a middle state</note> between them to transition, such as in an even number... and the second is a category between them representing a total lack, as noted among the Prophets, such as "the change" and "the walking," for the lack of all the Prophets between them rests in a simple state. Opposites exist in reality and are found in the relationship between "possession" and "privation" <term>Kinyan ve-Hefsed: an Aristotelian concept used in medieval Jewish philosophy to describe the presence of a quality (like sight) versus its absence (blindness)</term>, such as blindness and sight. Therefore, the tractate of *Temurah* <note>original: תמורה; meaning "Exchange" or "Permutation"</note> includes both opposites and the relationship of possession and privation. For this reason, the concept of "Permutation" was established for the fluctuations of death and for the secret of poverty—a lack of possession and a loss. 

Yet, "Permutation" still acts as a resting place for every opposite. The general rule of the matter is that within Permutations there are five established principles and six rules, and their permutations. [...] For a permutation may also occur in a state of deficiency. For example: two eyes meant for seeing—or a nose meant for hearing <note>This appears to be a hypothetical or mystical inversion of the senses</note>. Therefore, it is said here that an "opposite" is the matter that is prepared to receive sight or hearing and similar functions... until it can be said that here are "Oppositions and Permutations." Therefore, they are called "Sight," for the opposite only completes itself within a subject; sight and blindness are differentiated within the subject itself...

And behold, to change the simple forms... these are non-permutations. Because this secret is hidden, for there is no [simple] permutation, the Sages, of blessed memory, explained the meaning of "Hear, O Israel" <note>The *Shema*, the central declaration of Jewish faith</note>. For when Jacob called his sons and said, "Gather and I will tell you..." <note>Genesis 49:1</note>, the Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah: the indwelling presence of God</term> departed from him. He was concerned, thinking: "Heaven forbid, is there a defect in my offspring, as there was with Abraham [who fathered Ishmael] and Isaac [who fathered Esau]?" Therefore, the Presence departed. His sons all opened their mouths and said: "Hear, O Israel, the Lord is our God, the Lord is One." Just as there is no division in His heart, so too there is no division in our hearts. Then Jacob opened his mouth and said, "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom forever and ever."

---

...they will fail, for the mouth will not avail the warrior in battle if the Name **ALAD** <note>A mystical Name of God often associated with protection</note> was not already written and engraved upon his helmet. And though the mouth may open in war to cry out to the nations, and they will be moved and perish, it will not avail him if he does not focus his intention <note>kavanah</note> on the "Opening of Peor" <note>A reference to an idolatrous site, used here perhaps as a warning against improper focus</note>. It is like a small prayer shawl in the water, or a thorn in a man's hand—it will not help him on the day of wrath if he only mentions it by rote. For a matter that is not known through deep research will not avail its possessor...

Therefore, it is known that the secret of the "Good Voice" <note>perhaps referring to a specific incantation or prayer</note> should be mentioned three times, and this Name will bring rest... forever. And this Name is prepared for war, and one should cover it on the helmet based on the secret of the bond that is bound to the chain of the **Ten Sefirot** <term>Eser Sefirot: the ten creative powers or attributes of God in Kabbalah</term>, and through it, one will be saved from every evil encounter in war and defeat his enemy.

**Moses Botril** <note>A famous 14th-15th century scholar and commentator on *Sefer Yetzirah*</note> said: This is the way I have explained the secret of the foundation and the root. And in its brevity, all research is completed. Therefore, the amulet writers have sinned by writing the Name **AGLA** <note>A common protective Name, an acronym for 'Atah Gibor Le-olam Adonai' (You are mighty forever, O Lord)</note>, for this is a famous error. In the amulets of **Rashi**, of blessed memory, they would write differently. Furthermore, they write on "dusty" <note>unrefined</note> boards, and therefore their action does not take effect for the sick. Furthermore, they do not know that a person must first nullify the "Evil Accusers" through prayer before writing the amulet. Furthermore, one must have the proper intention at the time of writing for the secret of the amulet, and in this, the "pleasant aroma" <note>an idiom for Divine favor</note> will be accepted before the Clear Light. Furthermore, there is a specific material for every amulet, such as deer parchment or *gewil* <term>gewil: a type of rough parchment prepared for Torah scrolls</term>. Those who write amulets today do not know these heights, and therefore they are merely "writers"—meaning they are removed from the hidden way. In this, we are not at fault if the matter is not completed. Blessed is He who knows the secret of wisdom within the perfect Torah of the Lord.

For every soul has a place above and not below...

It concludes with a critique by Moses Botril regarding the improper and ignorant practices of contemporary amulet writers who fail to use the correct Divine Names and intentions.</summary>

# The Rambam, of blessed memory — Chapter 1, Mishnah 3 — The Ra’avad, of blessed memory

<margin>
By the number five: This explanation serves as a sign for the matter. It is mentioned at this time regarding every "five": that when the Lord intends to make known the power of the number, He removes the difficulty of preparing the matter through His expertise: to crown fingers for Himself upon the Place, Blessed be He <note>The "Place" (Ha-Makom) is a traditional Jewish name for God, implying He is the location of the world, rather than the world being His location.</note>. Take these as an example in the world, planted with the number... and the congregation of five among the lower beings, they are as all.
</margin>

### Ten Sefirot of Nothingness. <note>original: בלימה (Belimah); a term from Sefer Yetzirah often interpreted as "without substance" or "ineffable."</note> The number of
### ten fingers, five against five,
### and a single covenant aligned in the middle,
### in the word of the tongue and in the word of the nakedness. <note>original: המעור (Ha-Ma'or); a euphemism for the organ of circumcision.</note>

<margin>
Ten Sefirot of Nothingness. Another explanation: He arranged in a general way, as a gift, the 32 Paths for all existing things in the world—which are Power and Soul. Afterward, He founded one structure around another structure, and arranged in the third structure according to the style of the Talmud: "To what can the matter be compared?" This is the secret of existing things... there are substances from which all beings are formed. It is a hidden secret, and this is as it is said: "Do not let your mouth cause your flesh to sin" <note>Ecclesiastes 5:5</note>—meaning, do not allow yourself to fall into ignorance. For it is known that the complex parts of our bodies were formed from simple parts, and the number of simple parts from which all complex parts are composed is ten. He established and categorized their names as follows: (1) the Substances, (2) the Skin, (3) the Sinews, (4) the Veins, (5) the Pulsing Vessels [Arteries], (6) the Living Pulsing Nerves, (7) the Flesh, (8) the Fat, (9) the Marrow, and (10) the Ligaments. And all of these ten simple foundations are established according to the form of man... specifically this: for the "sea" which is within the power of the [reproductive] drop contains all the power of heat and moisture, and this... returned to become bones, and the spirit in the drop... in the way the waters were solidified and became firmaments...
</margin>

---

## Commentary of the Ramban, of blessed memory

Regarding the first, he mentioned the intellect in Wisdom <term>Chokhmah: the first spark of an idea</term>; second, he mentioned the intellect in Understanding <term>Binah: the analytical development of that spark</term>; third, he mentioned the intellect in Knowledge <term>Da'at: the internalizing of wisdom and understanding into tangible reality</term> as something visible to the eye. And this is what is meant by "a number of ten fingers," namely the law of the Head for Knowledge, as it is written: "And you lacked... <unclear>to withhold</unclear>."

And the "Covenant of Unity" of the tongue is the model of the ten, and this is the deciding factor between the ten fingers of the hands, etc., as if the 32 letters [of the Hebrew alphabet] have the power of the ten fingers. This is what is meant by "aligned in the tongue." And this is what is meant by "in the word of the tongue and the word of the nakedness," for it possesses the sign of the covenant... and it is the decider between the fourteen joints <note>original: אצבעות (etzba'ot); literally "fingers," but here referring to the digits or joints of the feet/toes.</note> of the feet, and it brings forth offspring which is planted within it. In the second [set of] 32 letters, you should contemplate the revealed order.

Two hands equal 28.</note>, for "Hand" stands for the letters in every Name and its specific title. All of the fourteen are engraved and arranged according to the Name of the twelve permutations <note>original: יב הוויות (Yud-Bet Havayot); the twelve ways the four letters of the Tetragrammaton (Y-H-V-H) can be rearranged, each corresponding to a month and a zodiac sign.</note> and according to the Name of *Adonai* <note>meaning: My Lord.</note>, just as your eyes behold. Their arrangement is constant, from the "tongue" of the Sun and from the "tongue" of the Moon <note>This refers to the solar and lunar cycles or perhaps the "scales" of judgment mentioned in Sefer Yetzirah.</note>... every... contains two camps or three camps for each and every Name of the Tetragrammaton, in order to instill power into this Name. 

This is the secret of all existing things and all things to come. It is the Name through which the heavens and the earth and all that is within them were created, as it is said: "The Lord is among them, Sinai in holiness" <note>Psalm 68:18</note>. And this is:
